[1] Reinforcement Learning Unlearns Corrupted SFT Reasoning cs.CLPDF

Anika Misra, Shreyas Vinod, Huteng Dai

TL;DR: 论文研究在推理任务的监督微调（SFT）数据被严重损坏时，强化学习（RL）是否仍能奏效。作者在GSM8K上系统注入多种错误并改变损坏比例，发现仅SFT性能显著下降，而GRPO后训练可将准确率恢复到约60%的黄金基线；甚至用8条错误的三行“仅形式”轨迹初始化RL，后RL准确率达到69.30%。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注SFT数据质量对推理模型后训练的影响，试图回答当SFT轨迹包含严重错误时，RL能否纠正或绕开损坏数据并恢复推理能力。

Result: 在GSM8K的361种损坏配置下，SFT-only准确率从43.61%降至13.80%，而GRPO后训练稳定恢复至约60%；8条错误“仅形式”轨迹初始化后RL达到69.30%，超过gold SFT初始化的60.71%和无SFT GRPO的35.00%。

Insight: 核心创新是指出SFT与RL在后训练中的功能不对称：SFT主要建立答案的结构布局/形式，RL独立发现功能推理；因此SFT精度与RL后精度几乎不相关（r≈0）。这提示可用极少形式化轨迹为RL提供结构先验，而不依赖高质量SFT内容。

Abstract: We show that reinforcement learning (RL) in reasoning tasks can succeed even when the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data is severely corrupted. In a mathematical reasoning dataset (GSM8K), we systematically corrupt training trajectories across 361 configurations by injecting realistic error types, varying error locations, trace-answer consistency, and corruption ratios up to 100%. While aggregated SFT-only performance steadily deteriorates under corruption (dropping from 43.61% to 13.80%), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) post-training consistently restores model accuracy back to gold-baseline levels (approximately 60%). Crucially, initializing RL with as few as N = 8 minimal, incorrect, three-line “form-only” traces boosts post-RL performance to 69.30%, outperforming standard gold SFT initialization (60.71%) and GRPO without SFT (35.00%). Further, we show that SFT accuracy is largely uncorrelated with post-RL accuracy (r approximately 0), demonstrating a fundamental asymmetry in post-training: SFT primarily establishes structural layout (form), while RL independently discovers functional reasoning (function).

[2] What Helps Language-Model Agents Review Violent Video? A Controlled Comparison with a Human Evidence Reference cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.MMPDF

Mohammed El Amine Mokhtari, Matei Mancas, Virginie Vandenbulcke

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型智能体应如何接收感知模型观察来审查暴力视频，在XD-Violence测试集799个片段上固定感知智能体和推理模型，比较智能体消息、类型化证据图加工具、扁平文本一次读取三种接口，以及单一音视频模型直接输入原始片段，并以两名标注者的人类证据参考分析决策、事件片段和依赖模态。

Details

Motivation: 现有系统可无训练地组合感知模型并由语言模型决策，但尚不清楚感知观察应以何种接口到达决策，以及声音、语音、视觉等哪类证据更重要。

Result: 扁平文本F1为0.967，接近单一模型0.968；证据图和消息传递更差，图摘要与多轮工具使用降低准确率。声音事件带来+0.012的F1提升；智能体管线GPU时间约为单一模型的3倍。

Insight: 创新在于受控比较不同证据接口与证据模态，并引入人类参考记录决策依据；客观看，其价值在于揭示扁平文本已足够强、复杂图与工具未必增益，声音证据关键，且视觉语言描述器伪影会误导管线。

Abstract: Systems that flag violent video for a human operator can now be built from language models without training, by combining perception models and letting a language model decide. We ask how the observations of the perception models should reach the decision, and which evidence matters. On the XD-Violence test set (799 clips) we fix one set of perception agents (video description, object detection, sound tagging, speech recognition and speech tagging) and one reasoning model, and compare three interfaces: messages between agents, a typed evidence graph explored with tools, and flat text read in one pass; and a single audio-visual model given the raw clip. Two annotators provide a human reference that records the decision, the incident segments and whether they relied on sight, sound or speech. Flat text matches the single model (F1 0.967 against 0.968), while the graph agent and message passing are less accurate; a variant of the graph agent developed on a separate set did not close the gap, and both graph summaries and multi-turn tool use lowered accuracy. These results hold with reworded prompts and with a larger reasoning model, except that the larger model weakens message passing enough for the graph agent to beat it. Sound events improve F1 by +0.012 [+0.004, +0.021], in line with the annotators, who used sound or speech on most violent clips. Citations of stored observations are faithful, changing about half of the decisions when removed, but no more so with the graph than with flat text, and the agent pipeline costs about 3 times the GPU time of the single model. We also report two artefacts of vision-language describers that mislead pipelines built on them.

[3] Neural Geometry as a Learning Signal cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Eric Todd, Tal Haklay, Nikhil Prakash, Matthew Finlayson, Michael Hanna

TL;DR: 论文研究是否可以利用神经网络内部表示的几何结构作为学习信号，在不对模型输出进行监督的情况下改善模型行为。作者在 Llama-3.1-8B 上通过监督残差流低维子空间中的理想几何结构（如傅里叶谐波编码的月份/偏移量、低维螺旋结构编码的换行前字符数），完成月份推算和固定宽度文本换行预测任务。结果表明，内部几何监督能达到与标准微调相近的性能，且仅替换理想几何表示并不足够，说明几何监督还带来了超出目标表示本身的收益。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注能否不依赖输出标签，而直接利用模型内部表示的几何结构作为学习信号来塑造模型行为，从而验证机制可解释性发现能否转化为训练方法。

Result: 在两个任务上，内部几何监督尽管没有输出监督，仍取得了与标准微调相近的性能；同时，直接 patch 理想几何表示不能恢复微调效果，说明几何监督的收益不只来自目标表示本身。

Insight: 创新点在于将神经几何的机制理解转化为训练信号：监督残差流低维子空间匹配理想结构（傅里叶谐波、螺旋结构），并发现几何监督具有超出表示替换的训练效应。客观来看，这提供了一种无需输出标签、利用内部结构引导模型行为的新思路，但其通用性和可扩展性仍需更多任务验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether the geometric structure of neural representations can provide a sufficient learning signal for improving model behavior without any supervision on model outputs. First, we fine-tune Llama-3.1-8B to answer questions such as “What month is four months after June?” by supervising internal representations of the input month (June = 6) and offset (4), which are encoded with Fourier harmonics. Second, we fine-tune the model to predict line breaks in text wrapped to a fixed width by supervising an internal representation of the number of characters since the preceding newline, which is represented by a low-dimensional helical structure. In both tasks, we supervise a low-dimensional subspace of the residual stream using an idealized target structure, and find that internal geometric supervision achieves similar performance to standard fine-tuning (despite no supervision on outputs). Moreover, we find that simply patching in an ideal geometry is not sufficient to recover fine-tuning performance, and thus demonstrate geometric supervision provides beneficial effects beyond the representations it targets. Together, these findings show how mechanistic understanding of neural geometry can be leveraged to shape the behavior of neural networks.

[4] Non-Expanding Gated Continued Fraction Architecture for Feedforward Layers in Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Amit Dhurandhar, Vijil Chenthamarakshan, Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy, Dennis Wei, Rahul Nair

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非扩张门控连分数架构，用于替换语言模型中的前馈层（包括MLP、GLU及MoE中的专家），并通过对数与指数重实现除法非线性。实验在GPT-2、Llama-3、Mamba-2和Llama-MoE等架构上验证，所提方法性能与先前连分数架构相似或显著更优，且吞吐更高。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在提升连分数启发式通道混合的性能，并简化其集成到标准语言模型预训练流程中，避免先前为稳定训练而引入的增量训练带来的实现复杂度。

Result: 在多种语言模型架构上，所提架构相比先前连分数架构性能相似或显著更好，并具有更高吞吐；除法重实现使更深CoFrGeNets稳定训练，无需增量训练。

Insight: 创新点包括非扩张门控连分数前馈组件，以及用对数-指数重实现除法非线性以稳定深层训练；客观看，其价值在于将参数高效架构、吞吐提升和训练稳定性结合，并可直接替换标准FFN或MoE专家。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that continued fraction inspired neural architectures (CoFrGeNets) can be highly parameter efficient yet performant for language modeling. In this work, we revisit continued fraction-based channel mixing with the goal of improving its performance and simplifying integration into standard pre-training protocols for language models. We achieve this by two simple yet critical contributions: i) we propose a novel non-expanding gated continued fraction inspired architectural component to replace feedforward layers in established language model architectures that may use vanilla multilayer perceptrons or gated linear units or (vanilla/gated) experts in mixture-of-experts (MoE) type architectures. ii) We propose a way to re-implement divisions – the non-linearity in these architectures – in terms of logarithms and exponentials. By experimenting on different language modeling architectures such as GPT-2, Llama-3, Mamba-2 and (nano) Llama-MoE we find that our proposed architecture performs similarly or significantly better than the continued fraction architecture proposed in prior work along with having higher throughput. In addition, our re-implementation of divisions makes training of deeper CoFrGeNets stable without the need for incremental training which was proposed in prior work as a means to stabilize training, but added notable complexity to existing large language model training implementations. Code is available at http://github.com/IBM/CoFrNets

[5] LLMs Memorize Their Own Hallucinations During Post-Training cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Anirudh Atmakuru, Tanya Goyal

TL;DR: 论文研究将面向推理任务的后训练配方（如基于结果的强化学习奖励、推理链中的假设提出与回溯等）直接用于知识任务时带来的副作用。作者假设这会促使模型记住自己在长推理链中生成的幻觉，并在六个开源模型、两个模型家族和两种架构上验证：训练中被奖励强化的自生成幻觉在后续训练中更易被复现，甚至包括模型随后撤回的假事实。

Details

Motivation: 前沿模型的后训练越来越多采用推理导向的训练策略，但这些策略也被用于数学和代码之外的知识任务。论文关注其潜在风险：原本为提升推理能力的设计可能无意中让模型在知识任务上记忆自身生成的幻觉，从而形成一条此前未被系统研究的幻觉学习路径。

Result: 实验在六个开源模型上确认假设：被训练信号强化的自生成幻觉平均召回率提升4.83%，而无训练信号的自生成幻觉仅提升0.17%；奖励幻觉还会使模型后续更少拒答，平均降低7.23%；记忆效应在训练中最难的问题上最强。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于识别并系统追踪后训练过程中“模型记忆自身幻觉”的机制，尤其揭示推理中心训练中的奖励和回溯模板可能强化错误事实。客观来看，其价值在于提醒后训练配方迁移到知识任务时需监控自生成内容，避免把推理行为带来的幻觉误当作可学习信号。

Abstract: Reasoning domains have shaped the post-training recipes of frontier models even though they are trained and deployed in domains beyond math and code. In this paper, we study the consequences of directly adopting reasoning-centric training recipes, e.g., the use of outcome-based rewards for reinforcement learning (RL), injection of “cognitive” behaviors such as proposing and backtracking from hypotheses in reasoning chains, etc., for knowledge tasks. We hypothesize that these decisions, while useful for reasoning, inadvertently encourage models to memorize hallucinations they themselves generate in their long reasoning chains when trained on knowledge tasks. To study this, we train models with standard post-training, extract their self-generated hallucinations during RL, and track memorization throughout the rest of training. Our experiments on six open models across two model families and two architectures confirm our hypothesis. We find that models’ recall of hallucinations reinforced during training increases by 4.83% on average, compared to just 0.17% for self-hallucinations that receive no training signal. In fact, models even memorize false facts that they immediately retract in their reasoning; a backtracking template inherited from reasoning-centric training that has no benefit for knowledge recall. Furthermore, we find that memorization is strongest on the hardest training questions, and rewarding a hallucination makes the model less likely to abstain from it later (by 7.23% on average). Overall, our work identifies and systematically studies a previously unexamined pathway through which models learn hallucinations during training.

[6] How Long Will My Language Model Run? A Benchmark for Pre-Execution Runtime Prediction cs.CLPDF

Ashish Molakalapalli, Kangda Wei, Haonan Duan, Alex Carter, Ruihong Huang

TL;DR: 论文提出 PreRun 基准，用于在任务执行前、仅利用执行前可得的信息，预测深度学习训练与推理的运行时间。该基准在 GPU 集群上采集了 801 条训练记录和 1,884 条推理记录，覆盖语言与视觉模型，并配有工作负载配置和明确定义的计时目标；此外还包含 64 个在标准化条件下测量的文本生成配置，每个配置分别独立启动 3 次。作者将 Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-OSS-120B、Claude Opus 5.5 三个 LLM 与回归基线对比，并在支持的前向传播子集上评估解析估计。

Details

Motivation: 向共享 GPU 集群提交深度学习作业的研究者需要运行时间估计，以便申请时限、选择硬件和规划算力预算；直接向 LLM 描述作业并询问运行时间看似方便，但这一做法缺乏系统评测和公开数据支撑。

Result: 在 16 个留出的文本生成配置上，开放权重 LLM 在零样本下平均低估延迟 90–99%；Claude 的零样本平均绝对误差（MAE）是用 32 个配置拟合的随机森林的 4.5 倍。加入 5 个带标签示例能明显降低 Claude 的生成误差，但收益会随计时目标的不同而变化。

Insight: 创新点在于构建了首个面向前执行阶段的运行时间预测基准：明确定义计时目标，并对同一配置重复启动三次以刻画测量波动；同时系统揭示出，LLM 在数值型延迟预测上存在严重系统性低估，少样本示例的收益也不稳定，说明该任务上专用回归基线仍明显占优。

Abstract: Researchers submitting deep learning jobs to shared GPU clusters need runtime estimates to request time limits, choose hardware, and budget compute. Large language models (LLMs) offer a tempting shortcut: describe the job and ask. We introduce PreRun, a benchmark for predicting deep learning training and inference runtime from information available before execution. PreRun contains 801 training and 1,884 inference records of language and vision models collected on a GPU cluster, paired with workload configurations and explicitly defined timing targets. It also includes 64 text-generation configurations measured under standardized conditions, each in three separate program launches. We compare three LLMs (Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Claude Opus 5.5) with regression baselines and evaluate analytical estimates on a supported forward-pass subset. On 16 held-out generation configurations, the open-weight LLMs underestimate latency by 90-99% on average without examples. Claude’s zero-shot mean absolute error is 4.5 times that of a Random Forest fit on 32 configurations. Five labeled examples substantially reduce Claude’s generation error, but their benefit varies across timing targets. We will open-source our data and evaluation pipeline upon acceptance.

[7] Your Judge Inherits Your Prompt: In-Context Label Bias in an LLM-as-Judge Is Directional, Not Symmetric cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Abdullah Ahmad, Ahmed J. O. Ahmed, Luca Citi

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM-as-judge在多模态视频问答评测中是否会继承few-shot示例的标签偏置。作者固定生成答案，仅改变judge示例中Yes/No标签比例，发现偏向接受的示例会显著抬高judge接受率，而偏向拒绝没有对称下降，表现为方向性、仅膨胀的偏置；单次示例抽样也不可靠，建议平衡示例并多次抽样平均。

Details

Motivation: LLM-as-judge常由少量上下文示例引导，但冻结语言模型可能吸收示例的标签平衡而非将其视为中立说明。论文关注这种标签偏置是否进入多模态视频问答评测流程，以及它呈现何种形式。

Result: 在NExT-QA和MSVD-QA上，偏向接受的示例使judge接受率相对人类共识分别约上升30和20个百分点，且错误呈强单侧性；偏向拒绝则无匹配下降。五次抽样分析显示单次结果波动可达18个百分点，超过提示条件间差异。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新是揭示LLM judge中的示例标签偏置是方向性、仅膨胀的失败模式，可伪造benchmark收益但不会伪造损失。客观看，其创新在于将该偏置诊断扩展到多模态评测pipeline，量化单次抽样不稳定性，并给出平衡示例与多次平均的低成本缓解策略。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge has become a standard way to score open-ended model outputs, yet the judge is itself a frozen language model steered by a few in-context examples, and such models are known to absorb the label balance of their demonstrations rather than treat them as neutral illustrations. We ask whether this label bias reaches an LLM judge used inside a multimodal (video question answering) evaluation pipeline, and what form it takes. Using a fully frozen generator-judge pipeline, we hold the generated answers fixed and vary only the Yes/No balance of the judge’s few-shot examples across four demonstration sets. Each verdict is validated against a two-annotator human consensus on 200 examples per dataset. The judge’s acceptance rate stays close to human consensus when its examples are balanced or skewed toward rejection, but jumps sharply (by about 30 points on NExT-QA and 20 on MSVD-QA, with a strongly one-sided error profile) when the examples are skewed toward acceptance; the opposite skew produces no matching drop. The bias is therefore one-directional within the tested range of label ratios. A separate five-draw analysis shows that results from a single example draw are unreliable, varying by up to 18 points, more than any difference between prompting conditions. We conclude that demonstration label bias in an LLM judge is a directional, inflation-only failure mode that can fabricate apparent benchmark gains but not losses, and that it is cheaply avoided by balancing the judge’s examples and reporting results averaged over several draws.

[8] Beyond Prompt Heuristics: Assessing Child Safety Alignment in LLMs cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.HCPDF

Anika Misra, Chimaobi Okite, Rada Mihalcea

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM儿童安全对齐在缺乏明确伤害类别线索的请求上是否仍然有效。作者在六个模型上发现，无伤害类别线索的请求比显式包含线索的请求更容易出现错位；并通过行为与机制分析表明，单个伤害相关词就能降低多数模型的错位率，说明儿童安全推理部分依赖提示词中的词汇和语义关联。

Details

Motivation: 现有儿童安全分类法和评测大多通过显式触发伤害类别的提示来评估风险，但儿童安全问题也可能出现在看似无害、缺乏可识别伤害线索的请求中，因此需要考察安全对齐在这些场景下的泛化能力。

Result: 在六个模型上，无伤害类别线索请求的错位率显著高于有线索请求，Qwen3.5-27B、GPT-5.4-mini和Claude-Haiku-4.5分别高出80.0、54.0和48.4个百分点；引入单个伤害相关词后，多数模型的错位减少，机制证据指向词汇和语义关联的作用。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示儿童安全对齐对提示词中伤害类别线索的依赖，并提出应将儿童安全视为上下文相关而非可归约为固定伤害类别的评测视角；可借鉴之处是结合行为评测与机制分析来检验安全对齐的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Abstract: Child-safety taxonomies typically characterize safety risks through a predefined set of harm categories, and existing evaluations largely operationalize these categories using prompts that explicitly invoke them. However, child-safety considerations can also arise in seemingly benign requests that lack recognizable harm-category cues. We therefore investigate how child-safety alignment generalizes to such settings. Across six models, we find considerably higher misalignment rates on requests without recognizable harm-category cues than on those containing such cues (80.0, 54.0, and 48.4 percentage-point gaps for Qwen3.5-27B, GPT-5.4-mini, and Claude-Haiku-4.5, respectively). Through controlled behavioral and mechanistic analyses, we find that introducing a single harm-associated word reduces misalignment across most models, with mechanistic evidence suggesting that child-safety reasoning may rely in part on lexical and semantic associations with words in the prompt. Our findings motivate evaluations that treat child safety as context-dependent, and not reducible to a fixed set of harm categories.

[9] Isolating the Language Shell: A Content-Controlled Study of Multilingual Reasoning cs.CLPDF

Hoejoon Kwon, Jinhee Jang, Byeonggeuk Lim, YoungBin Kim

TL;DR: 论文研究多语言推理蒸馏中“监督语言外壳”的影响：将相同的已验证英语推理轨迹渲染为英语、目标语言翻译版和语言混合版，在三种目标语言、两个难度级别上微调两个预训练学生模型，并评估多语言数学推理表现。研究发现，即使推理内容固定，语言外壳仍会改变下游准确率，且影响随学生模型、语言和难度变化；预训练兼容性并不能稳定预测这些结果。

Details

Motivation: 多语言推理蒸馏需要选择教师推理所用语言，但现有方法常将语言形式与生成、翻译、过滤等差异纠缠在一起，难以单独判断语言外壳本身的作用。论文旨在内容受控条件下隔离语言外壳这一设计选择。

Result: 实验表明，在推理内容固定时，不同语言外壳可导致不同的多语言数学推理准确率，且效应因学生模型、目标语言和难度级别而异；预训练兼容性与结果并不总是一致。摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA声明，但指出即使准确率无明显差异，输出语言行为也可能显著不同。

Insight: 创新点在于用内容受控的平行监督构造，将“推理内容”与“语言外壳”解耦，系统分析语言外壳对多语言推理蒸馏的独立影响。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是提醒研究者将监督语言选择视为独立设计变量，并同时关注准确率与输出语言行为。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning distillation requires choosing the language in which teacher reasoning is presented, yet existing recipes often entangle linguistic form with differences in generation, translation, and filtering. We construct content-controlled supervision by rendering the same verified English trajectories into English, translated target-language, and language-mixed shells, and fine-tune two pretrained students in three target languages, evaluating them on multilingual mathematical reasoning at two difficulty levels. Even with the underlying reasoning content held fixed, we find that shell choice can change downstream accuracy, with effects varying across students, languages, and difficulty levels. Pre-training compatibility does not consistently track these outcomes, and even when our tests find no clear accuracy difference between shells, their output-language behavior can still differ substantially. These results suggest that the supervision shell is a distinct design choice in multilingual reasoning distillation.

[10] How Far Does Lossless Re-tokenization Move an RLVR Update? cs.CLPDF

Zhe Dong, Fang Qin

TL;DR: 论文研究无损重新分词（同一字符串的不同token序列）对基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练更新的影响，比较重新分词与替换batch的更新距离，发现拆分约1/25的token使更新移动0.556倍于替换batch，且位移方向一致；理论证明只有零梯度步在不同分词下一致；实验表明重新编码影响少量rollout和token，训练中规范分词略有优势但不显著。

Details

Motivation: 语言模型对解码为同一字符串的不同token序列赋予不同概率，RLVR训练中重新编码可能改变训练用的token序列，需要量化这种影响的大小和方向一致性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B三位数加法任务上，拆分1/25 token使更新移动0.556倍于替换batch，比率随拆分比例上升；理论证明只有零全梯度步一致；审计显示重新编码影响最多5.2%的rollout但仅0.08%的token；训练中规范分词在18对预注册中14对领先，但平均准确率差异区间包含零。

Insight: 论文揭示了无损重新分词对RLVR更新有非平凡影响且位移方向一致，提供了理论分析和实证量化，为RLVR训练中分词选择提供了参考。

Abstract: Language models assign different probabilities to token sequences that decode to the same string. In reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), a trainer that re-encodes sampled text may train on a different token sequence with the same reward. We ask how far this moves a training update, and whether the movement has a consistent direction. From one checkpoint, we compare updates on the same rollouts, canonical and with selected tokens split into two pieces, against an update on a different batch. On Qwen2.5-0.5B with three-digit addition, splitting about one token in 25 moves the update 0.556 times as far as replacing the batch. The ratio rises with the fraction split in four models and depends on which tokens are split; within a checkpoint, the displacements share a direction across batches. In a tabular model with one table per tokenization and a single sampled string, we prove that whenever two strings below an unsampled prefix differ in probability, only the zero full-gradient step agrees across tokenizations, however it is weighted. In an audit that decodes and re-encodes resampled rollouts, re-encoding touches up to 5.2% of rollouts but at most 0.08% of tokens, far below the split fractions we test. Training on fixed rollouts, the canonical arm finishes ahead in 14 of 18 pre-registered pairs; the interval for the mean accuracy difference includes zero over all pairs and excludes it only under the registered exclusion rule, which favours the canonical arm.

[11] Do Senior Agents Do Their Job? Measuring Role Integrity in How LLM Agents Use Subordinate Material cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jinxiang Xie, Xinyi Zhang, Changjiang Jiang, Kexu Liu, Qi Yang

TL;DR: 论文关注多智能体 LLM 系统中高级智能体如何使用下属智能体提供的材料，提出“角色完整性”概念，即高级智能体是否履行其应尽职责，而非只看最终答案是否流畅正确。作者构建 RI-Bench，基于同行评审、上诉裁判和议会调查三类制度层级，覆盖权衡、核验和转述三种职责，通过受控改变下属材料来测量高级智能体的行为响应。

Details

Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 评估大多只评分最终答案，无法判断高级智能体是否真正权衡、核验或转述了下属报告，还是仅做简单汇总；因此需要直接评估高级智能体在使用下属材料时是否保持角色职责。

Result: 在 8 个 LLM、超过 12,000 个智能体回合的 RI-Bench 上，高级智能体很少采纳植入的程序性错误（仅 2.9% 裁决），但常跟随多数通用评审意见（最高 71% 决策）或遗漏证人陈述的限制（最高 65% 被引用主张）；义务提示能减少但不能消除这些失败，且会改变控制条件中的行为。

Insight: 论文的创新在于提出角色完整性这一行为评估视角，并用三类具有明确高级角色职责的制度层级构建 RI-Bench；它不评最终答案正确性，而是测量输出对下属材料受控变化的响应，结合规则检查与经人工标注校准的 LLM judge 来评估权衡、核验和转述职责。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems increasingly divide long tasks among subagents whose reports a senior agent turns into the final answer. Such a division of labor pays off only if each agent, and above all the senior agent, carries out the duties of its role. Current evaluations, however, mostly score the final answer, and a fluent, well-cited answer cannot show whether the senior agent weighed its subagents’ reports or merely aggregated them. To assess whether a senior agent meets its assigned responsibilities, we define role integrity as the preservation of an agent’s functional responsibilities when it acts on its subordinates’ material. To measure it, we introduce RI-Bench, a behavioral benchmark grounded in the structures and public records of three institutional hierarchies: peer review, appellate adjudication, and parliamentary inquiry. We choose these hierarchies because their rules explicitly assign the senior role a duty toward subordinate material, and together they cover three basic duties: to weigh, to verify, and to relay. Rather than scoring output correctness, we measure how senior agents’ outputs respond to controlled changes in subordinate material, using rule-based checks and a rubric-guided LLM judge calibrated against human annotation. Across eight LLMs and over 12,000 agentic episodes, senior agents rarely adopt a planted procedural error (2.9% of rulings) but often follow a majority of generic reviews (up to 71% of decisions) or drop witnesses’ stated limitations (up to 65% of cited claims). Obligation prompts reduce these failures without removing them, and they also shift behavior in the control conditions. These findings motivate direct, obligation-specific evaluation of role integrity rather than inferring it from general capability or explicit role instructions. Code and data are available at https://github.com/jxtse/ri-bench.

Jenitha Reddy Patel, Sameeksha Kini, Amulya Rayasam, Anisa Dye, Abhinav Sharma

TL;DR: 论文面向个性化长文本生成，提出显式风格抽取方法 SP-Refine：先用 StylePrism（7 类 28 个风格维度）从用户历史文本中抽取可检查的风格画像，再让模型基于用户样本起草，并依据画像和自身风格分数进行两轮修订。在 LongLaMP 的三个长文本任务和三个 LLM 上，该方法优于检索增强基线。

Details

Motivation: 标准检索增强生成只把用户历史文本放入提示，依赖模型隐式决定学习哪些风格特征，风格抽取不明确、可控性不足。论文希望将个性化长文本生成中的风格提取变成显式步骤，以提升风格模仿效果。

Result: 在 LongLaMP 三个长文本任务、三个 LLM 上，SP-Refine 在 27 项 ROUGE/METEOR 比较中胜出 25 项，平均提升 10.2%，最高提升 35.8%，优于 LongLaMP 的检索增强基线。消融显示风格画像主要用于修订指导而非替代用户样本；仅凭画像写作在全部 27 项比较中更差，单次隐式推断是更便宜且接近的替代。

Insight: 创新点在于用 StylePrism 将风格拆成 28 个可检查维度，并把风格抽取、样本起草、基于画像与自评分的两轮修订组合为推理时流程。客观看，可借鉴的是显式结构化风格画像作为修订约束，以及通过消融说明画像与样本的互补关系。

Abstract: Personalized long-form generation asks a language model to write new text the way a specific user would. The standard recipe, retrieval-augmented generation, places a few of the user’s past texts in the prompt but leaves the model to decide what to take from them. We introduce StylePrism, a taxonomy of 28 stylistic aspects in seven categories, and SP-Refine, an inference-time method that makes style extraction an explicit step: the model first fills StylePrism with the user’s patterns, quoted evidence, and habits to avoid, then drafts from the user’s samples and revises the draft twice against this profile, guided by its own style scores. On the three long-form tasks of LongLaMP with three LLMs, SP-Refine outperforms LongLaMP’s retrieval-augmented baseline in 25 of 27 ROUGE and METEOR comparisons, by 10.2% on average and by up to 35.8%. Annotating users’ own texts with the extracted profiles shows that StylePrism captures concrete, checkable habits, from the order of rhetorical moves to a reviewer’s sign-off. Ablations show that the profile helps as revision guidance, not as a replacement for the samples: writing from the profile alone is worse in all 27 comparisons, while a single implicit-inference call is a cheaper alternative that comes close. Oracle variants that let the reviser see the reference, together with reference-free controls, show that larger refinement gains require information unavailable at inference time.

Details

Motivation: IPO文件描述了公司的现状和未来愿景，但其中前瞻性声称是否真正实现难以评估。论文旨在建立一个系统化基准和流水线，以可靠地衡量公司对IPO声称的后续兑现情况。

Result: 在VFT基准上，语言模型分类人工标注声称的准确率明显高于从完整IPO文件中提取声称；提取覆盖率因类别而异，组织/文化类声称的恢复率远低于市场机会类；后续评估中模型判断与人类评估以不同方式偏离，不存在单一一致偏差。

Abstract: Initial public offering (IPO) filings describe what a company does and what it intends to become. Yet whether individual forward-looking claims are subsequently realized is difficult to assess. We introduce Vision Follow-Through (VFT), a benchmark and agentic pipeline that separates claim extraction, category assignment, verifiability, and evidence-grounded follow-through assessment. \vftclaims{} contains 5,066 human-annotated claim spans from 50 IPO filings, while \vftverify{} adds verifiability tiers, follow-through outcomes, and cited later evidence for 1,000 claims. Across language models, classifying human-annotated claims is substantially easier than extracting claims from full filings. Extraction coverage also varies sharply by category, with Organizational/Culture claims recovered far less often than Market Opportunity claims. Follow-through assessment poses a separate challenge: model judgments diverge from human assessments in different ways rather than exhibiting a single consistent bias. These results show that reliable assessment of corporate follow-through requires both finding the relevant claims and grounding subsequent judgments in later evidence.

[14] Do Language Models Know Which Words Steer Their Reasoning? cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Yanbei Jiang, Chao Lei, Jey Han Lau, Krista A. Ehinger, Rena Gao Wei

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型在思维链推理中是否知道哪些上下文词真正影响其推理，提出“幻象上下文影响”（PCI）现象：语义无关词会显著左右预测，但模型自评时却否认其相关性。作者用留一词JSD分析定位影响预测的枢纽词，并发现生成与判断之间存在矛盾；据此提出Online KV Suppression，对模型自判为无关但实际高影响的词进行KV缓存置零并重解码，从而提升思维链准确率。

Details

Motivation: 思维链提示虽能引出中间推理步骤，但上下文中哪些词真正驱动推理并不清楚；论文关注模型对自身推理影响因素的自我认知缺失问题。

Result: 在4个模型和3个基准上发现模型普遍无法识别真正影响推理的词，形成Generation-Judgment Contradiction；所提Online KV Suppression在4个模型和5个推理基准上提升CoT准确率，数学推理基准最高提升8.9个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于将“词级因果影响定位”与“模型自判断”结合，揭示模型生成与判断的不一致，并利用该矛盾进行单次前向的影响评分和KV-cache干预，无需额外训练即可改善推理。

Abstract: While chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting elicits intermediate reasoning steps from language models, it is unclear which words in the context actually steer the CoT. We identify Phantom Context Influence (PCI): language models are disproportionately steered by semantically irrelevant context words, even as the same models deny those words’ relevance when asked. To localize PCI, we introduce Leave-One-Out Word JSD analysis, which removes one context word at a time from the otherwise intact context to isolate its marginal effect on the next-token distribution; words whose removal flips the prediction (pivot words) are passed to the same LLM to self-judge whether it recognizes this predictive dependence. Across four models and three benchmarks, language models largely do not know which words steer their reasoning, a mismatch we call the Generation-Judgment Contradiction. Inspired by this phenomenon, we propose Online KV Suppression: it scores every prompt word’s influence over the whole chain of thought in a single pass, then zeroes the KV-cache entries of the highest-influence word the model itself judges inert and redecodes. This single-word intervention improves CoT accuracy across four models and five reasoning benchmarks, by up to 8.9 percentage points on math reasoning benchmark.

Details

Motivation: CoT提示能提升LLM推理，但长自回归解码导致推理成本高；已有丢弃token或使用连续隐表示的方法虽有损降本，却牺牲推理过程的可解释性与可审计性。论文希望在不破坏推理透明性的前提下压缩中间推理。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-14B和Qwen2.5-32B及四个多样化基准上，方法平均减少总生成token 14.7%，推理轨迹长度最多下降27.1%，平均准确率仅损失1.6个百分点。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点是用BPE学习频繁语义跨度对应的PUA压缩token，并通过严格1:1的可逆编码实现无损推理压缩；客观看，其价值在于保持可解释/可审计性，且与现有有损压缩方法正交，可叠加使用，为LLM推理提供高效透明的中间语言。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting significantly improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) but incurs high inference costs due to lengthy autoregressive decoding. While previous efficiency approaches reduce costs by discarding tokens or utilizing continuous latent representations, these lossy methods fundamentally sacrifice interpretability and auditability. In this work, we introduce a novel compression framework for intermediate reasoning. We apply Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) to reasoning corpora to learn a compressed vocabulary of Private Use Area (PUA) tokens, each representing frequent semantic spans. After expanding the LLM’s vocabulary, we fine-tune the model via strictly 1:1, our method is lossless. We evaluate our approach on Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-14B, and Qwen2.5-32B across four diverse benchmarks. Our method reduces total generated tokens by 14.7% on average with reasoning trace lengths dropping by up to 27.1%, while incurring a negligible average accuracy cost of 1.6 percentage points. Crucially, this approach is orthogonal to existing lossy methods, allowing practitioners to stack lossless and irreversible compression techniques. We demonstrate that a compressed intermediate language provides a highly efficient computational resource that accelerates inference while fully preserving the transparency of LLM reasoning.

TL;DR: 论文针对长文本生成中仅用文档级结果奖励导致监督延迟且粗糙的问题，提出短视野过程监督框架：将长文本按语义分解为片段级决策，在保留前缀上下文的条件下生成各片段，并用基于评分准则的忠实性、连贯性和相关性奖励进行局部打分，再计算逐步优势以进行局部策略更新。作者在相同GRPO框架下对比结果级监督与SH-PRM，验证监督粒度对长文本生成训练的影响。

Details

Motivation: 现有强化学习方法通常用单个结果级奖励优化整篇文档，难以定位错误并在语义不同部分之间进行信用分配。论文希望让监督粒度与长文本的语义结构对齐，从而提升长文本生成的训练效果。

Result: 在多个LLM和两个长文本生成基准上，SH-PRM在相同优化器和可比预算下稳定优于结果级监督及强推理时基线；分析表明性能提升主要来自改进的信用分配。

Insight: 创新点在于将长文本生成重构为带前缀上下文的片段级决策，并用评分准则奖励提供局部过程监督，再转化为逐步优势进行局部策略更新；客观看，其可借鉴之处是通过语义分解把稀疏结果奖励细化为片段级信用分配，并在同一GRPO框架下隔离监督粒度的影响。

Abstract: Long-form generation is increasingly important for agentic LLM systems, yet current reinforcement learning methods typically optimize an entire document with a single outcome-level reward. This delayed and coarse-grained supervision makes it difficult to localize errors and assign credit across semantically distinct parts of the output. To address this, we propose a short-horizon framework that reformulates long-form generation through semantic decomposition into segment-level decisions. Each segment is generated with prefix-conditioned context, allowing cross-segment coherence to be preserved while keeping supervision local. The generated segments are then scored with rubric-based rewards for adherence, coherence, and relevance, enabling explicit local supervision at the segment level. These local rewards are used to compute step-wise advantages, yielding localized policy updates. To isolate the effect of supervision granularity, we instantiate both outcome-level supervision and our short-horizon process reward method (SH-PRM) under the same GRPO framework. Across multiple LLMs and two long-form generation benchmarks, SH-PRM consistently outperforms outcome-level supervision and strong inference-time baselines under the same optimizer and comparable budgets. Further analyses suggest that these gains primarily arise from improved credit assignment. Overall, our results suggest that aligning supervision granularity with the semantic structure of long-form generation is an effective design principle for long-form LLM training.

Abstract: Multimodal fact verification faces a fundamental accessibility bottleneck: while large proprietary models exhibit cross-lingual robustness, small vision-language models suffer a severe, disproportionate accuracy collapse on low-resource languages. Closing this gap traditionally requires prohibitive scale or extensive language-specific fine-tuning. In this work, we propose Offset Recovery via Bilingual Inference-time Technique (ORBIT), an algorithmic intervention that mitigates this collapse at inference time. ORBIT estimates span-specific offset vectors from parallel claim-evidence instances and applies them to claim and evidence representations, targeting the cross-lingual degradation induced by low-resource evidence. Experiments across 81 configurations reveal that ORBIT substantially recovers both accuracy and F1-score in low-resource settings, exposes calibration offset norms as a diagnostic proxy, and outperforms translation-based and steering baselines.

Junying Wang, Yijin Guo, Yalun Wu, Ziheng Jiao, Farong Wen

TL;DR: 论文关注科学视觉语言模型在信息错误或缺失场景下的鲁棒性，提出Scientific Model Robustness概念，构建包含1,220个样本、六种退化类型的RoSci-Bench诊断基准，并提出RoSci-Agent，通过Parse-Verify-Decide流程在推理前核查前提。评估发现部分VLM会对无效或不完整前提仍作答，而RoSci-Agent取得更高的Overall Robustness Score。

Details

Motivation: 现有科学VLM通常假设输入信息完整且准确，忽视真实场景中信息不一致或缺失的问题，因此需要研究模型检测异常、评估问题可解性并进行自我校准的能力。

Result: 论文构建RoSci-Bench，包含1,220个样本和六种退化类型；被评估VLM有时会对无效或不完整前提给出答案；RoSci-Agent的Overall Robustness Score高于所评估的独立VLM。摘要未提及具体数值或SOTA水平。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable significant progress in scientific tasks. However, these models are generally assumed to be presented with complete and accurate information, overlooking the challenges of erroneous/missing information in wild scenarios. To address this gap, we introduce the concept of Scientific Model Robustness, which defines a model’s ability to detect anomalies, assess solvability, and perform self-calibration when faced with Information Error (inconsistencies) or Information Loss (insufficiency). Specifically, we present RoSci-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,220 samples using a seed-variant strategy across six degradation types. To address VLM fragility, we further propose RoSci-Agent, a framework that employs a Parse-Verify-Decide pipeline to vet premises before reasoning. The evaluated VLMs sometimes answer questions whose premises are invalid or incomplete. RoSci-Agent achieves a higher Overall Robustness Score than the evaluated standalone VLMs. RoSci-Bench gives the multimodal reasoning community a common test of when models answer, request clarification, or reject a scientific question.

[19] Model the Annotation, Not the Noise: Semantic Annotation-Channel Prompt Learning against Noisy Labels cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zibin Geng, Yitong Bao, Jia Li, Xuefeng Jiang, Sheng Sun

TL;DR: 论文针对预训练视觉语言模型提示学习在少样本Web与检索场景中易受标签噪声影响的问题，提出语义感知标注通道提示学习框架SACP。该方法从数据生成视角出发，将潜在干净标签预测与实例相关标注生成解耦，结合冻结VLM语义先验和轻量视觉残差构建结构化标注通道，并用广义EM与软后验进行优化，同时引入后验感知记忆用于测试时推理。

Details

Motivation: 现有噪声鲁棒提示学习方法多依赖硬样本选择或确定性标签校正，既丢弃了有价值的语义歧义，也忽视了观测标注的实际生成过程，因此需要从标注生成机制建模噪声而非启发式抑制噪声。

Result: 在多种合成噪声基准和真实世界噪声数据集Food101N上，SACP在不同标签噪声模式下均取得相比同类方法的最优性能，尤其在严重非对称噪声下表现突出。

Insight: 主要创新在于将噪声标签学习重述为标注通道生成建模，解耦干净标签与实例相关标注生成；结合冻结VLM语义先验与轻量视觉残差，并通过广义EM软后验和后验感知记忆保留标签不确定性，避免硬选择造成的信息损失。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient prompt learning has become a cornerstone for adapting pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), yet it remains highly vulnerable to label noise in few-shot Web and retrieval scenarios. Existing noise robust methods typically rely on hard sample selection or deterministic label correction, which discard valuable semantic ambiguity and overlook how observed annotations are actually generated. To bridge this gap, we reformulate noisy prompt learning through the perspective of data generation, proposing SACP, a Semantic-aware Annotation Channel-based Prompt learning framework. Instead of suppressing noise heuristically, SACP decouples latent clean-label prediction from instance-dependent annotation generation. By integrating frozen VLM semantic priors with lightweight visual residuals, SACP constructs a structured annotation channel optimized via generalized expectation-maximization with soft posteriors. Furthermore, a posterior-aware memory is introduced to preserve label uncertainty during test-time inference. Extensive experiments across different synthetic benchmarks and the real-world noisy Food101N dataset demonstrate that SACP achieves state-of-the-art performance against its counterparts in different label noise patterns, especially under severe asymmetric noise.

[20] INCISE: Training-Free In-Context Segmentation of Surgical Videos cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yifan Xu, Bofang Zheng, Guangyu Meng, Nishchal Sapkota, Xianshi Ma

TL;DR: 论文提出 INCISE，一种用于手术视频的免训练上下文分割框架。它不微调模型，而是从多个手术视频中挑选少量未标注帧并标注为参考，使其联合覆盖目标视频的局部外观，再用这些参考对目标视频进行分割。方法仅使用 1.7% 训练帧作为参考且目标视频无需标注，在手术视频分割上取得 67.2% 平均 mIoU。

Details

Motivation: 现有手术视频目标分割通常依赖大量专家标注对预训练模型进行微调，成本高；上下文分割虽可用少量参考避免微调，但现有方法随机选择参考，容易偏向手术视频中的主导视角，漏掉瞬态器械和小结构等目标。

Result: INCISE 仅选择并标注 1.7% 的训练帧作为参考，目标视频帧不标注，取得 67.2% 平均 mIoU，并比最佳 SOTA 微调方法高 9.2 个点。

Insight: 创新点在于免训练的手术视频上下文分割框架，以及跨多个手术视频选择未标注参考帧以联合覆盖目标视频局部外观，从而缓解随机参考带来的视角偏差和稀有目标遗漏问题。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将参考帧选择视为覆盖性问题，以少量标注提升冻结模型在手术视频分割中的实用性。

Abstract: Surgical video analysis requires video object segmentation (VOS), yet existing methods are commonly based on pretrained model fine-tuning using a large number of expert labels. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers the promise of bypassing this high cost by segmenting a target video using a few annotated references, which are the sole source of surgical knowledge for a frozen model. However, existing ICS methods tend to draw such references at random, which concentrate on a few views that dominate a surgical video and can miss other objects of interest (e.g., transient instruments and small structures). To address these challenges, we propose INCISE, a training-free ICS framework for surgical videos. INCISE selects unlabeled frames from multiple surgical videos that jointly cover the local appearances of a target video, and then segments the target video using these references. With only 1.7% of training frames selected and annotated as references and no target video frame labeled, INCISE obtains 67.2% mean mIoU and outperforms the best SOTA fine-tuned method by 9.2 points. We hope INCISE can help establish ICS as a practical alternative to fine-tuning in surgical video analysis and other domains with a costly labeling burden.

Result: 在 Panoptic Studio、DexYCB 和 Multi-View Kubric 基准上达到 SOTA，优于依赖估计深度的方法；在 10 视图和 100 查询点下推理约 7 FPS、内存低于 4 GB，并相比现有方法在更长距离上保持准确跟踪。

Abstract: We introduce Tracktail, a novel architecture designed to efficiently track query points in 3D from multiple calibrated views without any depth input. Our architecture separates the task as a per-camera video encoding of features and a per-query decoder. Notably, camera cross-attention is only performed across queries in the decoder, allowing for efficient inference (7 FPS and less than 4 GB memory for 10 views and 100 query points). We further introduce a per-camera scene scale computed from camera geometry, which allows us to normalize inputs and predictions appropriately across a vast variety of dataset scales. We train on a synthetic Multi-View Kubric dataset as well as a novel Multi-View Point Odyssey benchmark dataset, which consists of synthetic renders of humans and animals across 499 frames. Tracktail achieves state-of-the-art results on the Panoptic Studio, DexYCB, and Multi-View Kubric benchmarks, outperforming methods that rely on estimated depth. Tracktail also demonstrates a strong ability to maintain accurate tracking over longer ranges compared to existing methods. Our code, data, and weights are publicly available: https://ai-hhmi.github.io/tracktail/

[22] CADENCE:Temporally Coherent Whole-Body Human Mesh Recovery that Preserves Expressive Hand Motion cs.CV | cs.GR | cs.LGPDF

Keniel Peart, Jagmohan Chauhan, Indu P. Bodala, Shelly Vishwakarma

TL;DR: 论文提出 CADENCE，一个面向单目视频的全身 SMPL-X 人体网格恢复系统，旨在同时保持时序平滑和手部快速动作的表达性。系统先用共享 C-RADIOv4 编码器及高分辨率手部通路进行逐帧估计，再用离线时序精炼器沿运动学树为每个关节和帧选择学习的零相位滤波器组合。这样可在平滑躯干的同时保留手势和手语中的快速手指运动。

Details

Motivation: 逐帧全身 SMPL-X 估计器在视频上会产生抖动，而简单时序平滑虽能稳定躯干，却会抹平手语和手势中重要的快速手指动作；论文关注这一平滑性与手部表达性保持之间的权衡。

Result: 在 3DPW 上，精炼器将加速度误差从 17.4 降至 5.4 mm/s²，并把 PA-MPJPE 从 41.9 降至 40.2 mm，优于同类平滑器的 6.2–7.7 mm/s²；在 SignAvatars/How2Sign 上手部抖动最低，手速峰值幅度为 0.98，而重训练 SmoothNet 仅 0.35；在 EHF 上取得最低全身网格误差 25.7 mm 和手部关节误差 8.3 mm。

Insight: 创新点在于共享编码器的高分辨率手部通路，以及基于运动学树注意力、逐关节逐帧选择零相位滤波器混合的离线时序精炼机制；客观看，其价值在于针对全身动作中不同关节动态特性进行自适应滤波，避免全局平滑对手部细节的破坏。

Abstract: Whole-body SMPL-X estimators recover body, hand, and face meshes from single images, but applied frame by frame to video they produce jittery motion. Smoothing the output removes the jitter at a cost that body-only benchmarks do not measure: a temporal filter strong enough to steady the torso also flattens the fast finger strokes that carry meaning in sign language and gesture. We present CADENCE, a whole-body SMPL-X system for monocular video designed around this trade-off. A per-frame estimator built on a shared C-RADIOv4 encoder, with a high-resolution hand pathway that reuses the same encoder on hand crops, produces each frame’s estimate. An offline temporal refiner then filters the SMPL-X rotation sequence: attention along the kinematic tree selects, for every joint and every frame, a mixture of learned zero-phase filters, so the torso is smoothed while fast finger strokes pass through. On 3DPW, the refiner reduces acceleration error from 17.4 to 5.4 mm/s2 while lowering PA-MPJPE from 41.9 to 40.2 mm; generic smoothers applied to the same predictions reach 6.2 to 7.7 mm/s2. On SignAvatars/How2Sign sign-language sequences, the refiner has the lowest hand jitter among the compared smoothers while keeping a hand-speed peak amplitude of 0.98, against 0.35 for a SmoothNet retrained on the full SMPL-X vector. The per-frame estimator also obtains the lowest full-body mesh error (25.7 mm) and hand joint error (8.3 mm) among the compared methods on EHF.

Details

Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在多物体、多步空间关系组合推理上仍不可靠，而人工标注视觉数据通常关系固定、定位粗糙，难以获得精确的中间推理监督。论文希望自动化地提供精确推理过程监督和可验证结果奖励。

Result: 受控实验表明，基于推理轨迹的 SFT 优于仅使用最终答案监督，后续 GRPO 可进一步提升性能；所得模型还能迁移到外部空间推理 benchmark，并在多个 LVLM backbone 上有效，且无需针对特定 benchmark 微调。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain unreliable on spatial reasoning problems that require composing grounded relations across multiple objects. Yet, scaling reasoning supervision is challenging since human-annotated visual datasets often rely on fixed relational annotations and coarse object localization, making it difficult to obtain precise intermediate reasoning states across diverse spatial configurations and complexities. We introduce FARON (Fully Automated Reasoning Over spatial relatioNs), a framework that executes spatial queries to provide both execution-grounded reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards. Rather than relying on existing annotated data, FARON constructs synthetic 2D and 3D scenes with explicit geometries, enabling configurable object arrangements, spatial relations, and multi-step compositions. Deterministic query execution yields intermediate sets and final answers, providing precise supervision without manual relation annotation. Our pipeline converts intermediate states into natural-language reasoning traces for supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while the execution-derived answers provide verifiable rewards for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Controlled experiments show that trace-based SFT improves multi-step spatial reasoning over final-answer supervision and that subsequent group relative policy optimization (GRPO) provides further gains. The resulting models also demonstrate transfer across external spatial reasoning benchmarks and multiple LVLM backbones without benchmark-specific fine-tuning. These results show the potential of using spatial query execution as a unified, flexible source of reasoning supervision and verifiable outcome rewards for multimodal spatial reasoning.

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D object detection remains a significant challenge for outdoor scenes, with existing methods often struggling to effectively transfer the rich semantic knowledge from powerful 2D Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into a 3D context. Second, to enhance zero-shot capability and inject domain-specific information, we employ a powerful Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as an image captioner to generate rich textual captions for each of these proposals. These captions are integrated into the training objective to directly align the 3D features with these rich semantics, creating a composite loss that combines direct feature distillation with caption-based semantic enrichment. Abstract: Color walking video records body boundary motion alongside clothing folds, surface appearance, and recording-environment cues. Our architecture uses each patch’s pixel deviation from its clip mean to weight two low-rank query and value updates inside a pretrained RGB encoder, fusing the resulting video representation with a silhouette transformer sequence and regional kernel ridge scores extracted from one native-resolution RGB frame per window. [26] FlowScope: 3D Scene Flow Pretraining Improves Surgical Robot Policies cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

TL;DR: FlowScope 利用无标注临床立体视频进行手术机器人策略预训练：用现成立体深度和光流模型计算场景流标签，让 Action Chunking Transformer（ACT）主干预测场景点的度量 3D 运动，预训练后丢弃场景流预测头，部署时仍是标准 ACT 且无额外推理成本。作者在 1,149 个临床 procedure（1.35M clips）上预训练，并在真实手术机器人系统上验证其显著提升针拾取和交接任务成功率。

Motivation: 手术机器人模仿学习受演示采集成本限制，而大量机器人辅助手术以立体视频形式记录却无动作标签，无法直接用于策略学习。论文希望从无标签临床视频中预训练可迁移的手术策略表征，减少对昂贵专家演示的依赖。

Result: 在真实手术机器人系统上，相比从头训练，FlowScope 将针拾取成功率从 54% 提升到 90%，首次交接成功率从 4% 提升到 52%，完整任务成功率从 2% 提升到 14%（每组 n=50 次试验），且与生成式 flow-matching 动作头结合仍保留增益。消融显示度量 3D 运动是最强预训练信号，优于运动学、2D 轨迹、仅深度和无尺度 3D 流等方案。

Insight: 创新点在于用现成深度与光流模型自动生成度量 3D 场景流伪标签，在无动作标签临床视频上预训练 ACT 主干，并丢弃预测头以保持部署零额外推理成本。客观来看，其价值在于把自监督几何运动表征引入手术机器人策略学习，并通过消融和表征分析说明度量 3D 运动信号及针深度表征与真实任务成功率更相关。

Abstract: Imitation learning for surgical robots is limited by the cost of collecting demonstrations. Meanwhile, many robot-assisted procedures are recorded as stereo video, yet policies cannot learn from them directly; clinical video carries no action labels. We present FlowScope, which pretrains a surgical policy network on unlabeled clinical video by forecasting scene flow, the metric 3D motion of scene points, with labels computed by off-the-shelf stereo depth and optical flow models. We pretrain the trunk of an Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) on a dataset of 1,149 clinical procedures (1.35M clips) and discard the flow head afterward, so the deployed policy is a standard ACT with no added inference cost. On a real surgical robotic system, FlowScope raises needle pickup success from 54% to 90% and first-handover success on needle regrasp from 4% to 52% (full task: 2% to 14%) over training from scratch (n=50 trials each), and retains its gains with a generative flow-matching action head. Ablations indicate that metric 3D motion is the strongest pretraining signal among those tested; kinematics, 2D tracks, depth alone, and scale-free 3D flow, each paired with kinematics, yield smaller or no gains. Representation analyses suggest that scene-flow pretraining encodes metric needle depth more accurately, that kinematics pretraining increases reliance on the robot state, and that needle-depth probes follow the ordering of real-robot success where offline validation error does not.

Abstract: Learning robotic ultrasound policies requires interactive environments for scalable data generation and repeatable evaluation, while exploration on real patients is limited by acquisition costs and safety constraints. Generative world models offer an alternative, but image history and probe actions alone do not ensure anatomical consistency across wide-range scans and changing acoustic windows. Our three-stage pipeline combines anatomy-conditioned pretraining, causal conversion and long-rollout alignment. Inference optimizations achieve pose-to-ultrasound throughput of 81.2 FPS. Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used for embodied perception and reasoning, yet their physical understanding is largely evaluated on questions answerable from images. Many properties critical to manipulation, such as an opaque bottle’s fill level or an object’s compliance, may only be revealed through touch. We introduce TouchBench, an egocentric vision-language-tactile benchmark that distinguishes visually answerable questions (Tier A) from those involving properties hidden from the camera (Tier B). It pairs head-mounted video with synchronized 137-channel whole-hand tactile sensing, comprising 900 clips from five participants across 11 tasks, with 900 absolute and 4,102 relative questions. Four state-of-the-art VLMs average 66.4% accuracy on Tier A relative questions but only 49.8% on Tier B, near chance (50%). To bridge this gap, we propose Tactile-as-Prompt (TaP), which presents 34 interpretable tactile statistics as text, enabling VLMs to reason with touch without a learned tactile encoder. TaP raises zero-shot Tier B relative accuracy to 62.9% and reaches 73.1% after leave-one-user-out fine-tuning, outperforming video alone (67.4%) and a learned tactile-token encoder (67.3%). The gains extend to unseen objects and users. On a separate 20-hour in-the-wild corpus, tactile text improves fine-tuned contact-question accuracy from 67.1% to 75.5%. These results highlight the limits of vision-only physical reasoning and establish language as an effective interface for tactile perception.

[29] Perceive Once, Escalate on Demand: Handing Off Visual KV Memory from Small to Large VLMs cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LG | cs.ROPDF

Qiang Zhou, Jianing Deng, Siyan Dong, Jingxian Chen, Han-Yi Lin

TL;DR: 论文面向具身智能体长视频理解中的级联推理：小VLM持续感知，仅将不确定决策升级给大VLM；为避免大模型重复编码历史，提出Baton将小模型的视觉KV缓存经逐层线性映射交接给大模型作为可用记忆。Baton无需标签，仅用大模型对当前帧的答案蒸馏映射，使大模型只编码当前帧即可复用历史视觉记忆。实验表明该方法在RoboMME和OpenEQA上有效降低升级计算并保持性能。

Details

Motivation: 级联中小模型到大模型的每次升级都会迫使大模型重新编码小模型已看过的历史，导致升级成本主导整体开销。论文要解决如何在不重复编码长视频历史的情况下，把已有视觉感知高效转移给大模型。

Result: 在RoboMME上，Baton优于单独小模型、剪枝或缩短重看以及文本记忆；作为级联升级通道，与重看性能差距约1分以内，同时减少28-47%计算，64帧时每次升级计算少8倍。收益随大模型从8B增至32B而增大，在四个模型家族八个模型对上跟随大模型余量，并零样本迁移到OpenEQA。

Insight: 创新点在于不追求复现大模型自身的KV缓存，而是从大模型答案中无标签蒸馏逐层线性映射，将小模型历史KV转成大模型可用记忆；分析还定位收益主要来自大模型中间三分之一层的一个方向。客观看，这抓住了级联推理中“记忆可迁移性”而非“缓存保真度”的关键，提供了低成本视觉记忆交接机制。

Abstract: An embodied agent cannot afford a large vision-language model (VLM) at every step of a long video, so a cascade runs a small VLM continuously and escalates only uncertain decisions to a large one. Each escalation, however, forces the large model to re-encode a history the small model has already perceived, and this dominates its cost. We present Baton, which hands the large model the small model’s visual memory, its key-value (KV) cache over the history. Baton rests on one finding: the large model needs a usable memory, not a faithful copy of its own. A map trained to reproduce the large model’s cache matches it most closely, yet brings no gain over the small model alone. Baton instead distills a per-layer linear map, without labels, from the large model’s own answers, with the large model encoding only the current frame. Interventions on the map trace the gain to one direction, carried by the middle third of the large model’s layers. On RoboMME, Baton beats the small model alone, a pruned or shortened re-watch, and a text memory; as the escalation channel of a cascade it matches re-watching to within about one point at 28-47% less compute, and 8x less per escalation at 64 frames. Its gain grows with the large model, from 8B to 32B, tracks the large model’s headroom across eight model pairs from four families, and transfers zero-shot to OpenEQA.

[30] What Does a Zoom Actually Add? Decomposing Fine-Grained Perception Failures in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Zhixuan Ge, Anqi Li, Kexin Geng, Wei Qiu

TL;DR: 论文系统性地拆解了「放大局部区域为何能让视觉语言模型（VLM）答对原本答错的题」这一现象。作者固定首次前向传播的输入不变，只改变「再看一次」的方式，并把每个失败案例归因到能修复它的最廉价手段：复用缓存 token、隔离区域、对已见像素重新 tokenize，或获取新像素。作者在 Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、InternVL3 三个模型和 V*、ZoomBench、TextVQA 三个基准上的 2,753 个「放大后得救」失败样本上分析，发现多数失败并非缺少像素，而是已有证据的渲染密度不足。

Details

Motivation: 现有文献普遍把「放大后答对」解读为模型缺乏高分辨率像素。但放大同时改变了多件事：去掉上下文、对已见像素做更高密度的重新 tokenize；若首轮被下采样，还会引入模型从未见过的细节。论文希望分离这些因素，弄清放大究竟「补」了什么，从而纠正对失败原因的误判。

Result: 即使首轮下采样 17 倍，仅对已见像素上采样即可修复 47–73% 的失败（随机区域仅 1–3%）；真正需要新像素的比例随首轮预算变化，17 倍时为 23–50%，4 倍时为 12–23%；物体类需 6–12 倍单边放大，文本类 4 倍即饱和；允许任意放大时，17 倍预算下 68–94% 的失败无需新像素。

Insight: 创新在于提出按「最廉价修复手段」逐个归因失败的分析框架，把放大效应分解为上下文移除、重 tokenize、新像素获取等可分离因素。客观来看，其核心洞见是：VLM 细粒度感知失败更多源于已有视觉证据的渲染或 tokenize 密度不足，而非像素缺失；这提示应先对区域重新 tokenize，再考虑获取更高分辨率输入。

Abstract: Zooming into a region often turns a wrong answer from a vision-language model (VLM) into a right one, and a growing literature takes this as evidence that the model lacked the pixels. But a zoom changes several things at once. It removes the surrounding context, re-tokenizes, at a higher density, pixels the model has already seen, and, if the first pass was downsampled, exposes detail the model never received. We separate these factors for each failure. Holding the first pass fixed, we vary only the second look and assign every failure to the cheapest one that fixes it: reusing cached tokens, isolating the region, re-tokenizing the seen pixels, or acquiring new ones. The study covers 2,753 zoom-rescued failures of Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3 on V*, ZoomBench and TextVQA, at first-pass budgets from 17x fewer pixels than the source to native resolution. First, most rescued failures did not lack the pixels: upsampling the pixels the model had already seen fixes 47-73% of them even at 17x downsampling, against 1-3% for a random region. Second, the first-pass budget decides how often new pixels are needed: depending on the benchmark, new pixels are required for 23-50% of the rescues at 17x and for 12-23% at 4x. The missing-pixel reading and the accounts that dispute it thus hold at different budgets. Third, the useful magnification is large and differs between objects and text: seen pixels fix more failures up to 6-12x their first-pass size per side on objects and saturate by 4x on text, and even native pixels gain from being rendered beyond native resolution. Allowing any magnification, 68-94% of the failures at 17x need no new pixels. These models more often need a better rendering of evidence they already have than new pixels, which motivates re-tokenizing a region before acquiring it.

[31] What Does the Visual Encoder Buy? Visual Information Gain in Encoder-Free Multimodal Pretraining cs.CV | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Zhixuan Ge, Anqi Li, Wei Qiu

TL;DR: 论文提出「视觉信息增益」（VIG）这一指标，即把图像替换为纯色图时下一词预测损失的上升量，并将其精确分解为「全局信息」（图像统计量）与「布局信息」（打乱图像块后被破坏的部分）。作者用该指标系统分析了158M到1.04B参数的无编码器多模态解码器（训练至40B token）以及19个公开checkpoint，并与前置冻结SigLIP 2编码器的同构模型对比。结论是：预训练视觉编码器带来的净收益几乎全部来自布局信息（尤其是图中文字识别能力），而在打乱图像上两者损失相差不足0.04 nats。

Details

Motivation: 近期关于视觉编码器是否必要的 scaling 研究多以图像-文本数据中文本的 next-token loss 作为比较基准，但该损失可以在完全不使用图像的情况下下降，且相同损失下的两次训练对图像信息的利用可能完全不同，因此需要一种能真正度量「模型从图像中获得了多少信息」的指标。

Result: 在自研解码器上，图像使损失下降3%46%（依图像类型而定）；全局信息在首个1B token后即出现，布局信息从接近零开始缓慢增长，且在六类图像中的五类上不随模型规模增长，更大batch可用更少布局信息达到同样损失。加冻结SigLIP 2编码器仅在图像布局完整时损失更低，打乱图像后两者在五类图像上差距≤0.04 nats。生产级无编码器模型（Gemma 4 12B、NEO、Mono-InternVL）已获得0.530.76 nats的布局信息，接近编码器模型的0.650.82，远高于作者自研解码器的0.040.07。

Insight: 创新点在于提出可精确分解的VIG指标，将「视觉编码器的价值」从模糊的loss对比中剥离为全局信息与布局信息的可量化归因，并辅以patch shuffle这一简洁的反事实干预。客观来看，其价值主要在评测方法论层面：它解释了从头训练时两类模型性能持平的现象，并把预训练编码器的优势明确定位在布局/文字读取能力上，为无编码器架构的设计与训练策略提供了可诊断的度量工具。

Abstract: Recent scaling studies ask whether vision-language models still need a visual encoder by comparing encoder-free and encoder-based models on the next-token loss of the text in image-text data. This loss can fall without any use of the image, and two runs with the same loss can differ in what they take from it. Visual information gain (VIG) is the loss increase when the image is replaced by a uniform one. It splits exactly into global information, carried by image statistics, and layout information, which shuffling the patches within the image destroys. We apply it to decoders with 158M to 1.04B parameters that read pixel patches through a linear embedding, evaluated every billion tokens up to 40B, and to 19 public checkpoints. In our decoders the image lowers the loss by 3% to 46%, depending on the image type. Global information is present after the first billion tokens. Layout information starts near zero, grows slowly with training and does not grow with model size on five of six image types, and a larger batch reaches the same loss with less of it. With a frozen SigLIP 2 encoder in front of the same decoder the loss is lower only when the arrangement of the image is intact. On shuffled images the losses with and without the encoder agree to within 0.04 nats on five of six image types, so the net advantage of the encoder in the loss is layout information, above all the ability to read text in images. Public encoder-free models at production scale (Gemma 4 12B, NEO, Mono-InternVL) do acquire layout information. On the same documents they have 0.53 to 0.76 nats of it, against 0.65 to 0.82 for recent encoder-based models and 0.04 to 0.07 for our decoders, and their released training stages show it rising. These results agree with the reported parity of the two families when both are trained from scratch and place the reported advantage of a pretrained encoder in layout information.

[32] Tokenize Moments, Not Frames: Tokenizer-Aware Budget Allocation for Training-Free Video LLM Acceleration cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zirui Zhao, Kai Liu, Linghe Kong

TL;DR: 论文研究视频大语言模型（Video LLM）的无训练加速问题，指出精度损失发生在token剪枝或合并之前：在固定token预算下，长视频的帧采样间隔往往超过约2秒的帧相似窗口，Qwen-VL等模型的双帧3D patch会把两个不相关场景混合到同一个视觉token中，形成“双重曝光”；而InternVL等固定尺寸tokenizer虽不混合，但对同样的缩减响应不同。为此作者提出ESSENCE：按“时刻”（帧保持相似的片段）而非按帧进行tokenize，在编码前用闭式规则根据采样间隔和tokenizer类别确定token网格数与需要缩减的维度，并在LLM较后的层中释放视觉token。

Details

Motivation: 现有无训练视频LLM加速器在长视频上剪枝/合并token会掉点，作者把原因追溯到token选择之前的采样与tokenization阶段：帧间隔过大导致3D patch混合不同场景，且不同tokenizer对缩减的敏感度不同，因此需要tokenizer感知的预算分配。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL、Qwen2.5-VL和InternVL3.5上与5种近期加速器对比，在平均首token时延（TTFT）匹配的条件下，60组比较中有58组精度更高；仅用80%的token即可达到完整Qwen模型的平均精度，且TTFT快1.6倍；在GLM-4.1V上最高超过全模型3.4个点。

Insight: 创新点在于将tokenization粒度从“帧”改为“时刻”片段，并用闭式规则实现tokenizer类别感知的预算分配、配合视觉token的后层释放。客观来看，其价值在于把加速问题的定位从“剪枝/合并”前移到“编码前采样与分词”，揭示了双重曝光这一被忽视的失效模式，且方法无需训练、跨模型通用。

Abstract: Video large language models (LLMs) represent each video with thousands of visual tokens, and training-free accelerators that prune or merge these tokens lose accuracy on long videos. Analyzing frame similarity and the vision tokenizers of 16 open models, we trace this loss to a step before token selection. Under a token budget, the frames of a long video are sampled farther apart than the roughly 2 s over which frames remain similar. The two-frame 3D patches of models such as Qwen-VL then blend two unrelated scenes into each visual token, a double exposure that arises before any pruning or merging. Fixed-size tokenizers such as InternVL’s avoid this blend but respond differently to the same reduction. We propose ESSENCE, a training-free method that tokenizes moments, segments within which frames remain similar, rather than frames. Before encoding, a closed-form rule uses the sampling interval and the tokenizer class to set the number of token grids and the dimension to reduce; visual tokens are then released from later LLM layers. Against five recent accelerators on Qwen3-VL, Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5, ESSENCE achieves higher accuracy in 58 of 60 comparisons at matched mean time to first token (TTFT). With 80% of the tokens, it matches the average accuracy of the full Qwen models at $1.6\times$ faster TTFT; on GLM-4.1V, it exceeds the full model by up to 3.4 points. Code is available at: https://github.com/zhkandsrz/ESSENCE.

[33] SecretAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Privacy Protection against Vision Language Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CR | cs.LG | cs.MAPDF

Minda Zhao, Yue Zhang, Mengyu Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 SecretAgent，一个面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的图像隐私保护多智能体框架，在抵御 VLM 推断隐私信息的同时保持图像视觉保真度。LLM 智能体迭代定位泄露隐私的视觉证据，并自适应生成不可见扰动，同时由外观审查智能体拒绝有明显伪影的候选。方法包括基于六个代理编码器优化扰动的 SecretAgent-Gradient 和无梯度的 SecretAgent-Tool。

Details

Motivation: VLM 可从共享图像中推断位置、个人可识别信息、年龄性别乃至医疗图像中的敏感信息；现有对抗扰动方法虽能保护黑盒 VLM，但忽略图像质量并引入可见伪影。论文旨在同时实现隐私保护与视觉保真。

Result: 在定位、PII、人脸和医疗四类隐私应用、五个数据集和六个 VLM 上，SecretAgent 的图像质量保持显著优于现有方法，并在每类应用中达到 SOTA 隐私保护，且保护可迁移到其他 VLM；SecretAgent-Tool 在五个数据集中的三个上取得最佳感知保真度。

Insight: 创新点在于将多智能体框架用于图像隐私保护：LLM 智能体定位隐私视觉证据、自适应扰动目标 VLM 回答，并引入外观审查智能体控制可见伪影；同时通过多代理编码器集成提升跨模型迁移性，并提供无梯度程序化噪声方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can infer private information from shared images, including locations, personally identifiable information (PII), and personal attributes such as age and sex, even from medical images. Adversarial perturbations can protect such information against black-box VLMs, but existing methods overlook image quality and introduce visible artifacts into the images they protect. We present SecretAgent, the first general multi-agent framework that protects image privacy against VLMs while preserving visual fidelity. LLM agents iteratively locate the visual evidence that reveals private information and adapt an invisible perturbation to the target VLM’s answers and to an appearance reviewer agent, which rejects any candidate with visible artifacts. We instantiate SecretAgent with two complementary variants: SecretAgent-Gradient optimizes the perturbation through six surrogate encoders for cross-model transferability, and the gradient-free SecretAgent-Tool composes procedural noise and achieves the best perceptual fidelity on three of the five datasets. Experiments on four privacy applications (geolocation, PII, facial, and medical) across five datasets and six VLMs show that SecretAgent preserves image quality substantially better than existing methods while achieving state-of-the-art privacy protection in every application, and that its protection transfers to other VLMs.

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言智能体从视频重建可编辑3D房间场景时的几何准确性，指出现有渲染效果具有迷惑性。作者提出基于激光扫描的评测协议，在30个真实房间上以0-100分衡量表面、深度和家具摆放一致性，并通过受控实验分析输入几何、智能体自检与修订行为。结果表明智能体单凭视频重建几何精度有限，且会继承输入几何误差；作者进一步提出几何误差报告，帮助智能体在修订时发现场景与输入点云的不一致。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注智能体生成的3D场景即使渲染逼真，也未必几何准确的问题，旨在建立扫描参考评测并检验智能体能否验证和修正自身重建结果。

Result: 在30个真实房间上，最强被测智能体GPT-6 Astra仅从视频得到51.4分，而同一视频的密集重建为75.9分；给定重建所用点云后，Astra生成的可编辑场景与点云分数相差3分以内。几何误差报告使GPT-6.1 Terra和Sol的修订增益分别比并发控制提高3.9和5.5分。

Insight: 创新点包括扫描参考的几何评分体系、对智能体输入依赖与自检缺陷的受控分析，以及将保存场景与输入点云对比并列出最大差异的几何误差报告。客观看，论文揭示了“看起来对”不等于“几何对”，并提供了轻量自检信号来改善智能体修订，但主要评测规模和智能体范围仍有限。

Abstract: Vision-language agents can reconstruct rooms from video as editable 3D scenes, but convincing renders do not establish geometric accuracy. We introduce a scan-referenced evaluation and use controlled experiments to study reconstruction accuracy, the role of geometric inputs, and agents’ ability to verify and correct their scenes. Our geometric score measures surface, depth, and furniture placement agreement with laser scans of 30 real rooms on a 0-100 scale. The strongest tested agent, GPT-6 Astra, scores 51.4 from video alone, while a dense reconstruction from the same video scores 75.9. Given the point cloud used for that reconstruction, Astra produces an editable scene that scores within three points of it. Agents also inherit input errors: rescaling the supplied geometry changes scene scale almost proportionally. During revision, viewing renders improves appearance more consistently than geometry. Logs of their tool calls show that agents repeatedly measure their inputs but rarely compare them with the scenes they build. To provide this missing check, we introduce a geometric error report that compares each saved scene with the agent’s input points and lists the largest discrepancies. Without new geometric evidence, the report raises the revision gains of GPT-6.1 Terra and Sol by 3.9 and 5.5 points over concurrent controls.

Jingchen Sun, Jiaxing Zhang, Qingqiao Hu, Dingqiang Ye, Yuqi Jia

TL;DR: 论文提出Future-OPSD框架，利用未标注视频进行视频预见推理。该框架通过在线策略自蒸馏将过去-未来视频对作为自监督信号，无需额外标注或LLM监督；同时设计预见分数和对比特权优势，引导模型聚焦最有价值的未来信息，在开放式预测和封闭式规划任务上显著提升基础模型性能。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频预见推理方法依赖标注或外部大模型进行监督，可扩展性受限；且将整个未来片段视为同等重要，忽略了大量无关上下文信息。论文旨在解决这两个问题。

Result: 在开放式预测和封闭式规划任务上，Future-OPSD将基础模型分别提升最多14.2和15.9分，且不影响通用视频理解性能。

Insight: 创新点在于：1）利用在线策略自蒸馏将过去-未来视频对转化为自监督信号，无需额外标注或LLM监督；2）定义预见分数并推导对比特权优势，通过可处理的下界替代目标优化，放大决策相关信息并抑制无关信息。

Abstract: Video foresight reasoning requires vision-language models to anticipate future events or next actions from currently observed videos. Existing methods fit future captions, rationales, or full visual states, but face two limitations. First, they rely on annotations or external large models for supervision, limiting scalability. We address this with an on-policy self-distillation framework that turns past-future video pairs into a natural self-supervised signal without extra annotations or LLM supervision. Second, they treat the whole future clip as equally valuable, although most of it is irrelevant context information. We address this by defining a foresight score, from which we obtain a contrastive privilege advantage that drives the model to learn the most valuable future information. Since the score is intractable, we optimize a tractable lower-bound surrogate that interpolates this advantage, computed between the true and a random reference future, with a teacher-student distillation advantage; together, they amplify decision-relevant content information and suppress irrelevant context information. We call the resulting framework Future-OPSD. On open-ended prediction and closed-ended planning, Future-OPSD improves base models by up to 14.2 and 15.9 points without compromising general video understanding. Code will be available at https://jingchensun.github.io/future-opsd/

[36] Causal Diffusion for Extreme Super-Resolution in Interactive Games cs.CVPDF

Christina Zhang, Jenna Kang, Colin Groth, Akshay Jindal, Stavros Diolatzis

TL;DR: 论文提出 CIDER，一个面向交互游戏的因果扩散超分框架，用于从极低分辨率（如120p）输入合成高保真细节，并支持8×甚至潜在16×放大。方法仅利用当前和过去低分辨率帧，结合深度和运动向量等G-buffer引导，保持场景结构和时序一致性；同时通过轻量latent投影编码器、TAE解码器微调和扩散模型蒸馏实现交互级推理速度。

Details

Motivation: 现有游戏超分多针对中等放大倍数，在120p等极端低分辨率下难以恢复细节，任务重心从信息重建转向缺失细节生成；而视频扩散模型虽生成先验强，却依赖未来帧，不适合交互游戏。

Result: 在《赛博朋克2077》和《黑神话：悟空》数据上，实现120p到960p的8×超分，约13 FPS，视觉质量优于当前先进的游戏超分方法，并展示16×放大的潜力。

Insight: 创新点在于将因果扩散引入交互游戏极端超分，用当前/过去帧和G-buffer引导保证时序一致与结构保持，并通过轻量编码器、解码器微调和蒸馏降低推理负担。客观看，结合游戏引擎辅助缓冲与生成先验、并针对实时性做系统优化，是较有实用价值的思路。

Abstract: Existing game super-resolution methods typically operate at moderate upscaling factors, struggling to recover fine visual details from extremely low-resolution inputs (e.g., 120p). At such resolutions, super-resolution shifts from reconstructing existing information toward synthesizing missing visual details. While video diffusion models offer powerful generative priors for this challenging task, their dependence on future frames limits their applicability to interactive games. We present CIDER, a causal diffusion framework for extreme game super-resolution that synthesizes high-frequency details from current and past low-resolution frames, guided by G-buffers (depth and motion vectors) to preserve scene structure and temporal consistency. We collect data from two AAA games (Cyberpunk 2077 and Black Myth: Wukong). To reduce inference load, we develop a lightweight latent projection encoder tailored to ultra-low-resolution inputs, fine-tune a decoder (TAE) on our high-resolution outputs, and distill the diffusion model to reach interactive frame rates. Our experiments demonstrate high-fidelity 8$\times$ upscaling from 120p to 960p at around 13 FPS, outperforming state-of-the-art game upscalers in visual quality and showing potential for 16$\times$ magnification.

Details

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在真实视频数据上表现良好，但在视频证据与内部先验/常识强烈矛盾时，模型难以有效整合模态信息，可能走向盲信先验或盲信感知的失败极端。论文旨在系统诊断这一感知-认知冲突问题。

Result: 在TheaterBench上的实验表明，当前SOTA视频理解模型表现出严重的模态极化；许多去幻觉方法并未根本解决冲突，而只是重新平衡感知惯性与认知惯性之间的权衡。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于提出模态极化这一失败模式及认知惯性/感知惯性的二分框架，并构建违反物理规律或常识的反直觉视频基准进行诊断。客观来看，其价值在于将感知-认知平衡问题显式化，为评估和提升未来视频理解模型提供针对性诊断工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong video comprehension, largely benefiting from extensive training on realistic video data. However, when video perception severely conflicts with internal priors or common sense, we identify a critical failure pattern termed Modal Polarization, where models fail to integrate modalities and instead bifurcate into two distinct extremes: Cognitive Inertia, where models rigidly adhere to priors despite contradictory visual evidence, and Perceptual Inertia, where they blindly trust video perception while ignoring logical inconsistencies. To investigate this, we introduce TheaterBench, a benchmark designed to expose Modal Polarization using counter-intuitive scenarios that violate physical laws or common sense - situations rarely encountered in standard training distributions. Extensive experiments reveal that current state-of-the-art (SOTA) models exhibit severe Modal Polarization, and many de-hallucination methods merely rebalance the trade-off between Perceptual and Cognitive Inertia rather than fundamentally resolving the conflict. This work serves as a foundational step for evaluating and enhancing the perceptual-cognitive equilibrium of future video understanding models.

Details

Motivation: 现有视频生成世界模型通常独立生成每个相机视角，或以固定布局拼接所有视角，难以适应不同机器人本体中相机数量、位置和顺序变化的问题。

Result: 在覆盖 1 至 4 个相机的 RoboView-Bench 上，ViewDreamer 取得所评估模型中最佳生成质量，并相较独立逐视角生成提升跨视角一致性。其收益可迁移到 WAM 和 AC-WM；WAM 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0、RoboDojo 及真实 Franka 机器人上优于 FastWAM-Joint，两类变体均提升对未见相机设置和场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly important for robot learning, yet most generate each camera view separately or tile all views in a fixed layout. Robot demonstrations, however, are captured by synchronized cameras whose number, placement, and order vary across embodiments. We present ViewDreamer, a single world model that handles variable sets of cameras by disentangling per-view generation from cross-view interaction. Each view follows the generation path of a pretrained single-view model, and views interact only through shared slot tokens. We stabilize multi-view training with a curriculum that gradually increases the number of views. Cross-View Slot Agreement uses visual foundation models to group geometrically and semantically similar regions across views into shared slots. On RoboView-Bench, which covers one to four cameras, ViewDreamer achieves the best generation quality among the models evaluated and improves cross-view consistency over independent per-view generation. [39] SceneRuler: Measuring Physical Quantities in Video via Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction cs.CVPDF

Yumeng He, Gezheng Kang, Fanlian Zeng, Yin Yang, Chenfanfu Jiang

TL;DR: 论文研究从视频中进行定量物理推理的任务，即根据视频、问题及已知事实先验回答高度、距离、速度等数值问题。作者提出 SceneRuler，通过“最小充分重建”为每个问题构建仅足以测量目标量的显式物理场景，并以程序表示场景结构、通过物理引导拟合测量参数，最终以该场景为证据生成答案。方法在 QuantiPhy 及 27 个野外视频上评估，答案准确率优于最强基线，并提供可追溯的中间物理量。

Details

Motivation: 端到端视频语言模型缺乏显式度量尺度和物理一致性，且错误难以检查，难以可靠回答视频中的定量物理问题。论文希望将数值推理转化为可测量、可验证的显式场景重建过程。

Result: 在 QuantiPhy 验证集上，最终答案平均相对准确率达到 0.709，高于最强基线的 0.624；在 158 个问题中，重建能确定所问量的 47 个问题上，最终答案准确率达到 0.781。作者还将该方法应用于收集的 27 个无真值野外视频。

Insight: 创新点在于提出最小充分重建原则，仅为当前问题构建必要场景并在测量层面检验充分性；将场景结构交由视觉语言模型生成程序，而量值通过物理运动定律拟合，甚至用重力锚定度量尺度；同时显式标记参数和读出量的确定/不确定状态，使答案可追溯。客观上看，其价值在于把定量物理推理分解为可解释的场景重建、测量与证据化回答，而非端到端黑箱预测。

Abstract: Many questions about videos ask for a number, such as how tall a person is, how far a cart travels, or how fast a ball leaves a ramp. We study this task, quantitative physical reasoning from video, where the inputs are a video, a question, and a prior of known facts such as object sizes, speeds, and distances from the camera to named objects. End-to-end video-language models lack an explicit metric scale and physical consistency, and their errors cannot be inspected. We present SceneRuler, which instead recovers a measurable physical scene and answers with that scene as evidence. Its central principle, Minimal Sufficient Reconstruction, builds, for each question, only the smallest scene that suffices to measure the asked quantity, and tests sufficiency on measurements rather than assuming it. The scene is written as a program whose structure a vision-language model writes and whose magnitudes are left to measurement. Physics-guided Fitting measures them through physical motion laws, so that gravity itself can anchor the metric scale, and marks every parameter, and the read-out of the asked quantity, as determined or undetermined. Each question receives a read-out and an answer. The read-out is the asked quantity evaluated on the scene, and it is empty when the scene does not fix that quantity. The answer is always given by a model that takes the scene as evidence. We evaluate on QuantiPhy and also apply the method to 27 in-the-wild videos that we collected, which have no ground-truth answers. On its validation split, the final answer reaches a mean relative accuracy of 0.709 against 0.624 for the strongest baseline, and the reconstruction determines the asked quantity on 47 of 158 questions, where the final answer reaches 0.781. Because every intermediate quantity is explicit, each answer can be traced to the objects, scale, and motion behind it.

[40] ReactDrive: Self-Reactive Driving World Model cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReactDrive，一种面向自动驾驶的自反应式驾驶世界模型。它将“自反应性”定义为：根据观察历史和指定的自车动作，建模其他交通参与者的响应，并生成反映这些响应的视觉未来，而不依赖预设的未来 agent 轨迹。方法通过共享 agent 状态表示联合学习 agent 动力学与视频生成，并利用动力学监督和视频目标共同优化自反应性与视觉真实感。

Details

Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要把其他 agent 对动作的响应纳入世界自身动力学中；在自动驾驶里，若响应建模错误，会误判机动安全性，并削弱生成视频用于策略评估和学习的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明该方法在 agent 响应、自车可控性和视频质量上有提升；在五项基线的人体对比中获得 96.0% 的 agent 反应性偏好，并通过生成视频增强取得更好的下游规划效果。

Insight: 创新点在于明确提出并形式化驾驶世界模型的 self-reactivity 能力，且无需给定未来 agent 轨迹；通过共享 agent 状态表示将动力学建模与视频生成联合优化，并用生成视频增强下游规划，体现了世界模型评估与训练的一体化思路。

Abstract: The world evolves through the dynamics of its constituent entities and their interactions, with changes in one entity influencing the behavior of others. To simulate this evolution under different actions, interactive world models need to capture the resulting agent responses as part of the world’s own dynamics. In autonomous driving, errors in modeling these responses can misrepresent maneuver safety and undermine the reliability of generated videos used for policy evaluation and learning. To formalize this intrinsic requirement for driving world model, we define self-reactivity as the capability to model agent responses from observed history and a specified ego action, and generate visual futures that reflect those responses without prescribed future agent trajectories. To realize this capability, we introduce ReactDrive, a driving world model that jointly learns agent dynamics and visual generation through a shared agent state representation. Within this framework, dynamics supervision grounds a reactive dynamics prior in observed agent states, while the video objective jointly optimizes the self-reactivity and visual realism. Experiments show improved agent responses, ego controllability, and video quality, a 96.0% preference for agent reactivity in human comparisons across five baselines, and better downstream planning through augmentation with generated videos. We hope this work encourages the community to recognize self-reactivityas a foundational capability for developing and evaluating interactive world models. Project page: https://reactdrive.github.io/ .

TL;DR: 论文关注医学图像分割中视觉-语言模型生成的掩码与文本描述不一致的问题，提出一致性增强的两阶段分割框架C2Seg：预训练阶段用聚类感知对比学习缓解硬对比学习中的假负例，融合阶段用双向互补注意力模块和基于KAN的注意力门控增强视觉-文本交互与结构一致性。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法在多部位/多病灶描述下常出现文本与掩码冲突，原因包括模板化临床语言导致一对一硬对比学习产生大量假负例，以及单向视觉主导交叉注意力缺乏语言主导且具空间感知的通路，难以将文本语义有效注入空间视觉域。

Result: 在不更新语言编码器的条件下，方法在四个公开医学影像数据集上显著提升了文本-掩码一致性和分割精度；摘要未给出具体数据集名称和SOTA对比细节。

Insight: 创新点在于用冻结强基线构造批内文本相似度矩阵作为软标签进行聚类感知对比学习，以缓解假负例；并引入双向互补注意力让各模态沿自身路径主导注意力，结合KAN注意力门控增强多模态特征表达。客观看，其可借鉴处是面向医学文本高度模板化特点改进跨模态对齐，并以双向注意力提升文本语义到空间视觉的注入效果。

Abstract: Vision-language models for medical image segmentation often produce masks that conflict with the accompanying text, especially under multi-site/multi-lesion descriptions. We trace this failure to two factors: (i) highly templated and repetitive clinical language causes one-to-one hard contrastive learning to yield numerous false negatives, weakening cross-modal alignment; and (ii) predominantly vision-driven, one-way cross-attention lacks a language-dominant, spatially aware pathway, hindering effective injection of textual semantics into the spatial visual domain. To this end, we propose Consistency-enhanced Two-stage Segmentation (C2Seg). In the pretraining stage, Cluster-aware Contrastive Learning uses a frozen strong baseline to construct an intra-batch text similarity matrix as soft labels, thereby alleviating false negative conflicts and producing more discriminative visual representations. In the fusion stage, we introduce a Bidirectional Complementary Attention Module, where each modality dominates attention along its own path, fostering deep interaction and structural consistency between visual and textual representations. In order to enhance the expressive power of multimodal features, we further adopt KAN-based Attention Gating. Without updating the language encoder, our approach significantly improves text-mask consistency and segmentation accuracy on four public medical imaging datasets. The source code is available at https://github.com/JGuiidev/C2Seg.

Abstract: Learning representations of visual images is a challenging task. It requires extracting abstract patterns from high-dimensional data without explicit supervision. This becomes pronounced with 3D MR imaging due to sparse features, higher dimensional inputs, and smaller datasets. Many state-of-the-art representation learning algorithms successfully rely on transformers and diffusion-based architectures. However, these methods struggle with smaller datasets. In effect, solution methods relax the problem by slicing 3D volumes and solving the problem for 2D medical imaging, resulting in a loss of spatial 3D information. We present R2RG, a representation learning method based on Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), with Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) honing the reconstruction task. Our key insights lie in exploiting architectural biases in 3D CNNs, combined with data-efficient auto-encoding GANs and a composite loss function to enhance reconstruction fidelity and stabilize training. Our experiments on three standard datasets and a locally collected dataset show that R2RG not only achieves superior performance but also generalizes to heterogeneous and smaller datasets. Furthermore, representations learned with R2RG outperform those learned with contrastive-based representation learning methods on Parkinson’s and Alzheimer’s disease classification. Our work demonstrates that reconstruction-based representation learning is a promising direction for 3D medical imaging under limited data availability.

Result: 在红外-可见、多曝光、多聚焦视频融合任务及复杂退化上验证，V2VFusion在融合质量和时序稳定性上优于现有方法；摘要未给出具体定量指标。
