Abstract: Video fusion integrates complementary information in multiple source video streams into a single sequence for complete scene perception. Existing methods perform frame-wise fusion and lack unified spatio-temporal modeling across sequences, damaging temporal coherence across sequences. When dealing with degradations, they also ignore temporal dynamics and overload a single network with diverse degradations, leading to limited and imbalanced degradation suppression. To address these issues, we propose V2VFusion, the first unified video-to-video fusion framework that directly generates temporally coherent, high-quality fused videos from degraded multi-source inputs. By reformulating video fusion as a video-to-video conditional generation problem in latent diffusion space, our approach holistically models spatio-temporal dependencies across entire sequences and intrinsically ensures sequence-level consistency. On this basis, a text-guided degradation-conditioned ControlNet bridges high-level textual degradation descriptions with low-level visual priors, enabling interpretable and controllable guidance for structure-preserving fusion beyond purely data-driven conditioning. To handle heterogeneous and compound degradations, a state-modulated degradation-aware hierarchical mixture-of-experts module decouples expert selection into diffusion-state-aware filtering and degradation-aware routing. It prevents unreliable routing and fosters expert specialization to mitigate representation conflicts among diverse degradations. Experiments on infrared-visible, multi-exposure, and multi-focus video fusion tasks and complex degradations validate that V2VFusion surpasses existing methods in fusion quality and temporal stability. The code is available at https://github.com/ChenDoubleJ/V2VFusion.

[44] $R^2$: Recognizing and Reducing Redundant Visual Tool Use cs.CVPDF

Yilin Zhao, Jinyu Wang, Wenming Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出R2框架，用于识别并减少视觉语言模型在多模态推理中的冗余视觉工具调用。作者先通过样本级工具效用类别分析CAT揭示基准准确率会掩盖工具使用能力，再提出视觉反馈依赖度VFD评估单次调用，并用冗余感知GRPO（RA-GRPO）把依赖信号与类别条件调用成本结合，在正确轨迹间分配相对效率优势。实验表明该方法在保持基准准确率相当的同时显著减少工具调用，并提升按工具效用类别评估的宏平均准确率。

Details

Motivation: 视觉工具虽能提升多模态推理，但其收益因样本而异，仅看基准准确率无法判断工具交互是有益还是冗余；论文旨在识别并减少不必要的视觉工具使用。

Result: 在七个基准上，受益于工具的样本最多仅占12.44%；相对原始GRPO，RA-GRPO在基准准确率相当的情况下将平均工具调用次数减少12.39%，并按工具效用类别将宏平均准确率提高3.35个百分点。

Insight: 创新在于从样本级工具效用分类和单次调用视觉反馈依赖度两个层面刻画工具冗余，并将效率信号融入GRPO的奖励监督，无需外部评估器或轨迹重生成；客观看，其价值在于把“少调用工具但保持正确”转化为可优化信号，补足仅用结果监督的不足。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that visual tools can improve multimodal reasoning. However, the effects of tools vary substantially across samples, and benchmark accuracy alone does not reveal whether tool interactions are beneficial or redundant. We present $R^2$, a framework that recognizes and reduces redundant visual tool use in vision-language models. We first introduce a sample-level Category Analysis of Tool Utility (CAT), which categorizes samples according to whether a model can benefit from tools. This analysis reveals that benchmark scores may obscure a model’s tool-use ability. Across seven benchmarks, samples benefiting from tools account for at most 12.44% of each benchmark, while the majority gain little from tool interaction or are even harmed by it. To assess individual calls, we propose the Visual Feedback Dependency (VFD) metric, which compares the likelihood of the same subsequent reasoning under actual and alternative visual feedback without an external evaluator or trajectory regeneration. Redundancy-Aware GRPO (RA-GRPO) combines this dependency signal with a category-conditioned base call cost to assign relative efficiency advantages among correct trajectories, supplementing outcome-based supervision. Across the seven benchmarks, RA-GRPO reduces the mean number of tool calls by 12.39% relative to Vanilla GRPO at comparable benchmark accuracy. Evaluated by tool-utility category, RA-GRPO improves category macro-average accuracy over Vanilla GRPO by 3.35 percentage points.

[45] Hindsight Forcing: Learning Better History from the Future in Autoregressive Video Diffusion cs.CVPDF

Haonan An, Yihang Tao, Yu Guo, Renlong Chen, Jiasheng Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Hindsight Forcing，用于少步自回归视频扩散的流式生成训练。该方法通过 latent straight-through bridge 解耦前向生成与反向信用分配，使历史 chunk 能利用未来损失进行监督，从而缓解长 rollout 中的视觉漂移和结构崩溃。前向仍执行完整多步 rollout 更新 KV cache，反向仅将未来梯度路由到每个历史 chunk 的单个选定去噪步骤，无需额外参数、架构修改或完整轨迹回放。

Details

Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散逐个生成视频块，早期历史生成质量会影响后续生成，但为避免 BPTT 内存爆炸，已有 self-rollout 方法通常截断跨 chunk 梯度或 detach 历史 latent，导致早期去噪计算无法获得下游反馈，长序列生成时出现视觉漂移和结构崩溃。

Result: 在 VBench 上，Hindsight Forcing 在整体视觉质量和动态运动方面优于现有 baselines；消融实验验证了对历史去噪进行监督的收益。摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Insight: 创新点在于用 straight-through bridge 将未来损失回传到历史 chunk 的单个去噪步骤，实现历史 chunk 从未来结果学习；同时避免保存完整轨迹图，且无需额外模型参数、架构修改或单独轨迹回放。客观上看，其价值在于为自回归视频扩散提供了一种低内存的历史信用分配训练机制。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables real-time streaming generation, with each generated chunk serving as historical context for subsequent generation. To avoid the memory explosion of backpropagation through time, previous self-rollout methods typically truncate cross-chunk gradients or detach historical latents. As a result, earlier denoising computations receive no downstream feedback through the generated history on how to synthesize frames that facilitate subsequent generation, leading to visual drift and structural collapse as rollouts extend. To address this limitation, we propose Hindsight Forcing, which allows historical chunks to learn directly from future outcomes by decoupling forward generation from backward credit assignment via a latent straight-through bridge. In the forward pass, each chunk completes a full multi-step rollout to update the KV cache. In the backward pass, the bridge routes gradients from future losses back to a single selected denoising step per historical chunk, training both the context writer and historical denoiser on shared parameters without retaining the full trajectory graph. Remarkably, Hindsight Forcing requires no additional model parameters, architecture modifications, or separate trajectory replay. Extensive experiments on VBench demonstrate that Hindsight Forcing outperforms existing baselines in overall visual quality and dynamic motion, while ablation studies validate the benefit of supervising historical denoising. Code is available at https://github.com/haonanAN309/Hindsight-Forcing.

Abstract: Multi-modal cloud removal exploits complementary optical and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) images to restore cloud-degraded remote sensing images. Most existing methods rely on black-box, data-driven architectures with limited interpretability of cross-modal interactions. Although some attempts introduce physical priors into networks, their oversimplified formulations fail to faithfully characterize the underlying physical imaging process and the networks remain loosely coupled with optimization variables. To address these issues, we propose a physics-informed deep unfolding network for cloud removal via optical-SAR image fusion, termed as PIU-CR. We first formulate cloud removal as a physics-informed optimization problem. Specifically, we fundamentally ground the optimization in the physical imaging mechanism of cloud degradation by embedding the atmospheric scattering model into the data fidelity term. Then, the regularization terms establish an explicit cross-modal interaction mechanism, overcoming the single-modal information scarcity and the obscure, uninterpretable interactions in black-box models. They are dedicated to SAR-guided structure and spectral regularization, jointly introducing cross-modal structural feature alignment and coordinated spectral consistency constraints. The resulting optimization problem is then unfolded into a multi-stage neural network with dedicated structure and spectral branches. Each stage corresponds to an explicit optimization iteration step, enabling interpretable and effective reconstruction process. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Fgq188/PIU_CR.

[50] PhysForce: Physics-aware Force Control for Interactive Video World Model cs.CVPDF

Xuancheng Xu, Xiaofeng Wang, Yue Ma, Zheng Zhu, Bing-Kun Bao

TL;DR: 论文提出 PhysForce，一个用于交互式视频世界模型的两阶段框架，将外力（位置、方向、大小）直接编码到视频潜空间而非运动轨迹，并结合监督微调与强化学习提升力响应、物理一致性和泛化能力。框架包含潜空间力干预编码器、物理归因网络和物理感知强化学习三个组件。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法常将力表示为运动轨迹，可能引入不合适的运动先验，使模型过度依赖位移模式；模型内部表示难以可靠定位力引起的状态变化，难以关联施力与物理响应；同时力标注数据有限，导致未见场景下物理一致性和时序稳定性不足。

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明 PhysForce 提升了力响应性、物理一致性和泛化能力，验证了其对交互式视频世界模型的有效性；但未提及具体 benchmark、定量指标或 SOTA 对比。

Insight: 创新点在于不暴露未来运动地将力映射到潜空间、用物理归因网络关联力干预与局部潜状态变化，并通过自监督奖励在分布外视频上进行物理感知强化学习。可借鉴之处是将物理作用建模为潜空间控制信号，并结合归因监督与自监督强化学习缓解力标注稀缺和泛化不足问题。

Abstract: Interactive video world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve in response to user interventions, including physically consistent state changes induced by applied forces. Existing methods often represent forces as motion trajectories, which may introduce inappropriate motion priors, causing the model to over-rely on encoded displacement patterns. More fundamentally, the model’s internal representations do not reliably localize force-induced state changes, making it difficult to associate applied forces with their resulting physical responses. Also, limited force-annotated data make it difficult to produce physically consistent and temporally stable predictions in unseen scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose PhysForce, a two-stage framework that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning through three complementary components. During supervised fine-tuning, (1) the Latent Force Intervention Encoder maps force location, direction, and magnitude into the video latent space without exposing future motion; and (2) the Physics Attribution Network associates force interventions with localized changes in the latent state, strengthening the learning of physical responses to applied forces. During reinforcement learning, (3) Physics-Aware Reinforcement Learning uses self-supervised rewards on out-of-distribution videos to improve physical consistency and generalization. Extensive experiments show that PhysForce improves force responsiveness, physical consistency, and generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness for interactive video world models.

[51] Mem-X: A Memory-Dependent Cross-Episode Benchmark for Vision-and-Language Navigation cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Zhikang Shi, Yuk Tung Samuel Fang, Hongyu Ding, Zixuan Chen, Yujin Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mem-X，一个用于评估依赖长期记忆的跨回合视觉语言导航（VLN）基准。它将回合组织为会话，后续指令会引用先前回合中的物体或路线，并从历史物体回忆、记忆内定位与情节索引、脱离记忆的重定位与历史路线复现三个层级进行评测。Mem-X 包含 34 个场景、710 个会话、2,071 个回合，其中 1,204 个为记忆依赖回合；论文还提出模块化基线 MemBridge，结合语义场景记忆、跨回合定位和由粗到细检索来增强现有 VLN 智能体。

Details

Motivation: 现有跨回合 VLN 基准难以区分导航成功是依赖历史记忆还是当前探索，因此需要专门评测记忆依赖性的基准。

Result: 摘要称与最先进 VLN 方法的实验表明 MemBridge 带来收益，同时揭示记忆依赖导航仍有较大提升空间，但未给出具体定量指标。

Insight: 创新点在于通过会话化任务设计和引用历史物体/路线的指令来解耦记忆与探索，并设置三级记忆评测协议；同时要求智能体在无跨回合坐标系变换信息下建立空间对应。MemBridge 的语义记忆、跨回合定位与由粗到细检索模块也为现有 VLN 智能体提供了可借鉴的记忆增强框架。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables embodied agents to reuse past observations across tasks. However, existing cross-episode vision-and-language navigation (VLN) benchmarks often leave it unclear whether successful navigation relies on historical memory or current exploration. We introduce Mem-X, a benchmark designed to evaluate memory-dependent cross-episode VLN. Mem-X organizes episodes into sessions in which subsequent instructions refer to objects or routes encountered in earlier episodes. Its three levels evaluate historical-object recall, in-memory localization and episodic indexing, and off-memory relocalization and historical-route reproduction. For the latter two levels, agents receive episode-local poses but no transformations between episode frames, requiring them to establish spatial correspondence with historical memory. Mem-X comprises 2,071 episodes across 710 sessions in 34 scenes, including 1,204 memory-dependent episodes. We also provide MemBridge, a modular baseline that combines semantic scene memory, cross-episode localization, and coarse-to-fine retrieval to support existing VLN agents. Experiments with state-of-the-art VLN methods demonstrate the benefits of MemBridge while revealing substantial room for improvement on memory-dependent navigation. The benchmark and baseline code are available at https://github.com/NJU-R-L-Group-Embodied-Lab/Mem-X.

[52] GEG-SAM: Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model for Weakly Supervised Polyp Segmentation cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Ao Wang, Lei Zhang, Lituan Wang, Zhenwei Zhang, Xiangbao Gong

TL;DR: 论文提出GEG-SAM，一个弱监督息肉分割框架，利用内镜医师的注视数据指导分割一切模型（SAM）。与以往将注视点视为孤立提示不同，GEG-SAM建模注视探索的时间动态和空间拓扑结构，并结合注视对齐的视觉上下文生成注视条件token，注入冻结的SAM编码器以调节特征。在五个公开数据集上，平均Dice比最强弱监督基线提高9.30个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 现有弱监督方法将注视点视为孤立空间提示，忽略了注视是连续的视觉搜索过程，具有时间连接、空间组织和视觉语义耦合的特性。论文旨在利用结构化的注视关系及其视觉线索来引导SAM，解决弱监督息肉分割中监督信号利用不充分的问题。

Result: 在五个公开息肉分割数据集上进行实验，所提方法平均Dice比最强弱监督基线提高9.30个百分点，证明了有效性。

Insight: 创新点在于显式建模注视探索的结构化关系（时间动态和空间拓扑），并将其与视觉上下文关联生成条件token来调节SAM。客观来看，利用眼动数据作为弱监督信号，结合SAM进行医学图像分割，有望减少标注成本并提升分割性能。

Abstract: Endoscopist gaze offers an unobtrusively acquired record of lesion-oriented visual exploration during routine clinical inspection, providing relevant supervision for tasks. However, existing methods treat fixations as isolated spatial prompts directly associated with the target, overlooking the fact that natural gaze records a continuous visual search process in which fixations are temporally connected, spatially organized, and coupled with the visual content. Interpreting each fixation independently based only on its local visual content overlooks the temporal evolution and spatial organization of the gaze trajectory, whereas modeling relations among fixations alone cannot reveal the visual semantics of the regions they attend to. To this end, we propose the Gaze Exploration-Guided Segment Anything Model (GEG-SAM), a principled weakly supervised framework designed to explicitly exploit structured gaze relations and their corresponding visual cues to modulate SAM for polyp segmentation. Specifically, GEG-SAM captures structured gaze relations by modeling the temporal dynamics and spatial topology of gaze exploration. It then associates these relational representations with fixation-aligned visual context to derive gaze-conditioned tokens, which are further injected into the frozen SAM encoder to modulate its pretrained image features. In this way, GEG-SAM effectively exploits the rich information embedded in gaze exploration to guide SAM toward lesion-oriented segmentation. Extensive experiments on five public polyp segmentation datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving the mean Dice by 9.30 percentage points over the strongest weakly supervised baseline.

[53] SCAP: Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Xiao Lin, Yaoxu Lyu, Ronghao Dang, Zhili Liu, Kaijing Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究 VLA 动作专家预训练中单模态预训练与后续视觉-语言联合建模之间的冲突，提出 SCAP：在全输入训练前，对进入 VLM 的视觉和文本 token 随机掩码，以保留视觉-语言联合建模通路并减少视觉捷径依赖。该方法在四种 VLA 架构上均带来性能提升，并在真实机器人三项指令条件操纵任务上取得 76.67% 平均成功率，比全输入预训练高 25 个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 单模态动作专家预训练可抑制视觉捷径，但可能损害后续 VLA 适配所需的视觉-语言交互；论文还通过诊断模型指出，VA 或 LA 预训练可能使原本活跃的联合视觉-语言建模通路失效，且后续全输入训练难以恢复。

Result: SCAP 在四种不同 VLA 架构上均稳定提升性能；真实机器人三项指令条件操纵任务平均成功率为 76.67%，相比全输入预训练提升 25 个百分点。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新是引入全输入训练前的 SCAP 预训练阶段，通过随机掩码视觉和文本 token 保留联合视觉-语言建模通路，并降低对视觉捷径的依赖、增强对语言指令的利用。客观来看，其创新点在于用诊断模型揭示单模态预训练对跨模态通路的不可逆影响，并提出简单且模型无关的随机上下文策略来平衡捷径抑制与跨模态交互保持。

Abstract: Single-modality action-expert pretraining controls competition from visual shortcuts before vision–language–action (VLA) adaptation. Yet single-modality pretraining can impair the vision–language interactions needed for subsequent joint prediction. We construct a diagnostic model in which VA or LA pretraining can disable an initially active pathway for joint vision–language modeling. In this model, subsequent training with complete visual and language inputs cannot restore the pathway, even with unlimited adaptation. We therefore introduce Stochastic Context for Action Expert Pretraining (SCAP), a new action-expert pretraining stage that precedes full-input training. During this stage, visual and text tokens are randomly masked before entering the VLM. In this unified diagnostic model, SCAP can preserve the joint vision–language modeling pathway during pretraining and subsequent full-input training. Compared with direct full-input training, it can also reduce reliance on visual shortcuts and increase reliance on language instructions. SCAP consistently improves performance across four different VLA architectures. In real-robot experiments, it achieves 76.67% average success across three instruction-conditioned manipulation tasks, outperforming full-input pretraining by 25 percentage points.

[54] DepthMSA: Combinatorial Training and Budget-Aware Depth Allocation for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Pan Wang, Jie Peng, Zhimin Li, Jingxian Chen, Hui Ji

TL;DR: 论文提出DepthMSA，一个面向多模态情感分析的预算感知框架，允许同一模型内不同模态采用不同编码深度。它通过组合式任意深度训练让融合头覆盖所有跨模态深度组合，并在推理时根据样本级FLOPs预算选择最低校准误差的配置。实验表明该方法能在保持精度的同时显著降低计算量。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析模型通常对所有模态都做全深度编码，忽略了各模态编码器的计算成本与深度带来的预测收益并不匹配。论文旨在研究如何在有限计算预算下跨模态分配编码深度，同时解决异质深度表示的有效融合问题。

Result: 在CMU-MOSEI上，DepthMSA相对全深度模型精度损失不超过1个百分点，同时减少45%实测FLOPs；带跳连变体可减少82% FLOPs。当CLIP视觉编码器主导计算时，方法转向降低视觉深度，在精度不变下减少56% FLOPs；相比ADMN，带跳连变体以3.2倍更少FLOPs和更低延迟达到其精度，且无需按预算训练控制器。

Insight: 创新点在于用一次全深度编码器前向完成所有跨模态深度组合的融合头监督，从而支持单模型内的异质深度融合；推理阶段采用免训练、成本感知的分配器，按样本FLOPs预算选择配置。客观来看，其价值在于将模态计算成本与预测收益不匹配问题转化为可动态分配的深度配置问题，并避免为每个预算单独训练控制器。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) models rely on large pretrained encoders, yet typically process every modality to its full depth, regardless of its computational cost or predictive contribution. This raises a fundamental question: how should limited computation be allocated across modalities to preserve predictive performance? We reveal a substantial mismatch between the computational cost of modality encoders and the predictive benefit of processing them more deeply, suggesting that different modalities require different encoding depths. However, enabling heterogeneous depths introduces a key challenge: representations extracted at different depths must still be effectively fused. To address this, we propose $\textbf{DepthMSA}$, a budget-aware framework that supports flexible modality depths within a single MSA model. Specifically, we introduce combinatorial anytime training, which supervises the fusion head across all cross-modal depth combinations using a single full-depth encoder pass. At inference, a training-free, cost-aware allocator selects the configuration with the lowest validation-calibrated error under a sample-specific FLOPs budget. On CMU-MOSEI, DepthMSA maintains accuracy within one percentage point of the full-depth model while reducing measured FLOPs by 45%, or by 82% with a separately trained skip-enabled variant. When a CLIP visual encoder dominates computation, the allocation shifts toward reducing visual depth, achieving 56% fewer FLOPs at unchanged accuracy. Compared with ADMN, an adaptive layer-allocation method that trains a controller for each budget, the skip-enabled variant reaches its accuracy with 3.2$\times$ fewer FLOPs and lower latency, without any per-budget training. Our project is available at https://pwang322.github.io/DepthMSA/.

[55] Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Qwen3-VL for Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Manh Tuan Ha, Xuan Bao Viet Tran, Vo Minh Dat Nguyen, Minh Nhat Vo, Quoc Trung Nguyen

TL;DR: 论文研究在有限计算资源下，将紧凑视觉语言模型 Qwen3-VL 适配到手写数学表达式识别（HMER），把任务建模为多模态结构生成并输出线性化 Symbol Label Graph（SLG）。方法从 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B 出发，对视觉、语言、注意力和 MLP 模块应用 rank-16 LoRA，并用指令格式的 CROHME 2019 样本训练。实验与 ViT-Transformer 基线、零样本 Qwen3-VL 等方案比较，并分析数据组成和结构正确性问题。

Details

Motivation: HMER 需要同时恢复符号身份和二维语法结构，局部看似合理的转写仍可能全局结构错误。论文关注紧凑视觉语言模型能否在有限算力下被高效适配，以生成线性化 SLG。

Result: 在 CROHME 测试集 1,198 个有效样本上，仅用 CROHME 训练的模型取得 43.74% 精确匹配和 77.67 的语料 BLEU-4，显著高于任务特定 ViT 基线的 4.92% 和 43.26。消融实验加入 2,000 个手写、公式引导样本后，精确匹配反而降至 42.32%，说明更多数据不一定自动有益，存在域对齐问题。

Insight: 创新点在于以 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B 加 rank-16 LoRA，覆盖视觉、语言、注意力和 MLP 模块，将 HMER 资源高效地表述为多模态结构生成，并通过受控数据组成消融揭示域对齐问题。客观上，其价值还包括对局部 token 重叠与全局结构正确之间差距的分析。

Abstract: Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) requires joint recovery of symbol identity and two-dimensional syntax, so a locally plausible transcription may still be structurally wrong. We study whether a compact vision-language model can be adapted to generate linearized Symbol Label Graphs (SLGs) under limited computing resources. Starting from a 4-bit Qwen3-VL-4B checkpoint, we apply rank-16 Low-Rank Adaptation to the vision, language, attention, and MLP modules and train with instruction-formatted CROHME 2019 examples. We compare this model with an internally implemented ViT-Transformer baseline, a zero-shot Qwen3-VL configuration, and additional internally evaluated alternatives. On the 1,198 valid CROHME test examples, the CROHME-only model obtains 524 exact matches (43.74%) and a corpus BLEU-4 score of 77.67, compared with 4.92% and 43.26 for the task-specific ViT baseline. An ablation that adds 2,000 manually written, formula-guided samples decreases exact match to 42.32%, exposing a domain-alignment issue rather than an automatic benefit from more data. The principal contribution is therefore an evidence-backed, resource-efficient formulation of HMER as multimodal structural generation, together with a controlled data-composition ablation and an analysis of the remaining gap between local token overlap and globally correct structure.

[56] Prune to Expand: Training Prompt Enhancers with Less to Imitate cs.CVPDF

Tianhe Wu, Qilin Sun, Kede Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究提示增强（PE）模型的监督微调数据构造问题，发现基于密集描述构造的SFT配对中存在冗余监督，生成增益大的配对未必带来更好训练效果。作者提出纯文本方法P2E，在每例删除上限下选择性删除密集描述中的冗余句子，保留用户显式需求和有效视觉结构，从而训练更好的提示增强器。

Details

Motivation: 常见做法假设能提升图像生成的密集描述也能为PE模型提供有效监督，但论文发现密集描述会迫使模型复现与用户意图弱相关的图像细节，造成冗余监督。论文旨在精炼密集描述监督信号，提升PE模型对用户意图和生成质量的兼顾能力。

Result: 在相同训练配置下，P2E在多种图像类别和视觉生成器上相比传统密集描述SFT取得更好的用户偏好对齐和视觉质量；摘要未给出具体定量指标或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示密集描述监督中的冗余问题，并提出仅处理文本的选择性句子删除策略来“剪枝”监督信号。可借鉴之处是用数据/监督剪枝而非增加数据或模型复杂度来提升提示增强与图像生成效果。

Abstract: Prompt enhancement (PE) refines concise user prompts into detailed descriptions to improve the compositional aesthetics of generated images while preserving user intent. A common practice constructs supervised fine-tuning (SFT) pairs by deriving short, user-like prompts from dense captions, implicitly assuming that captions that improve image generation also provide effective supervision. In this paper, however, we uncover a counterintuitive finding: PE models trained on caption pairs with substantial generation gains perform comparably to those trained on pairs with only marginal gains. We argue that dense captions can impose redundant supervision by requiring PE models to reproduce image-specific details weakly related to user intent. Motivated by this discrepancy, we introduce Prune to Expand (P2E), a simple text-only method that refines dense-caption supervision by selectively removing redundant sentences under a per-example deletion bound while preserving explicit user requirements and informative visual structure. Extensive experiments under identical training configurations demonstrate that P2E improves alignment with human preferences and visual quality over conventional dense-caption SFT across diverse image categories and visual generators.

Details

Motivation: 现有推理时方法多用偏好分数比较小型生成器与专有模型，但无法说明到底缩小了多少与目标视频的差距，也难以指导在成本约束下选择何种控制信息。

Result: 在954个片段和四个教师模型上，首帧+深度视频可闭合64%到94%的各保真度差距；项目反应模型预测片段受益层级的留出AUC为0.76到0.95；控制有成本时，选择策略在同等成本下优于固定层级前沿。

Insight: 创新点在于用oracle控制阶梯提供可解释的经验上限参考，并将逐片段控制收益预测与预算化选择结合；客观看，其价值是把“控制信息能补多少”从偏好比较转为可度量、可调度的能力感知决策。

Abstract: Inference-time methods that lift a small open video generator toward a proprietary one are compared through preference scores, which never say how much of the gap to a target video they close. We introduce the oracle control ladder, which hands a fixed student progressively richer information from a target clip and reads the closure of each fidelity metric at each rung as an empirical headroom reference for the methods that must synthesize that information. On 954 clips of the principal student, a dense caption reproduces little of the appearance and almost none of the motion of a target, a first frame closes half of the appearance gap and a tenth of the motion gap, a depth video closes three quarters of the motion gap and leaves color untouched, and the two together close 64 to 94% of every fidelity gap across four teachers, while a second student agrees on the rungs that it accepts. An item response model forecasts which rung closes which clip with held-out AUC of 0.76 to 0.95 on the fidelity criteria and serves as the prior of a budgeted selection policy. When controls are free, a fixed dense-control rung is hard to improve upon, and once controls are priced the policy lies above the fixed-rung frontier at matched cost and survives critics that ruin critic-driven search.

Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在高分辨率图像上难以进行细粒度理解：不同注意力头对目标实体及其属性的定位可靠性不一致，局部细节也常与整体场景上下文割裂。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained understanding of high-resolution images: attention heads vary in their reliability for grounding target entities and their attributes, while localized details are often disconnected from the broader scene context. We propose EAGLE (Entity-Aware Grounding with Layered Evidence for High-Resolution Image Understanding), a plug-in framework that jointly selects grounding signals and organizes hierarchical visual evidence. It then integrates hierarchical evidence construction and question-guided routing to select optimal view combinations, feeding them into the frozen MLLM to enhance fine-grained visual understanding. Experiments with Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3-8B demonstrate that EAGLE consistently improves performance across both backbones and achieves SOTA results on V*, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K compared with existing methods using the same backbone.

Details

Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏虽降低采样成本，但常削弱可调的无分类器引导（CFG）和运行时负向提示能力。论文旨在恢复少步生成中的可控性，同时保持生成质量与效率。

Result: 在 Wan2.1 视频生成结合 DMD 上，四步达到 81.46 VBench，并在不同引导尺度下取得比 step-distilled DMD 更低的视频师生距离；在 NegElement-1K 的 1000 个图文/视频匹配案例上，目标移除率高于对应教师模型。Qwen-Image 上结合 LCM 和 DMD 验证了方法通用性，并保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Insight: 核心创新是正负条件分离、异号注意力融合，以及由引导尺度和时间步共同调制的极性混合机制，使蒸馏学生模型在单骨干单次前向中支持连续 CFG 和负向提示。客观来看，其价值在于将可控引导机制较通用地嵌入少步蒸馏，并跨注意力架构、生成模态和蒸馏目标验证。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion distillation reduces sampling cost but often weakens adjustable classifier-free guidance (CFG) and runtime negative prompting. We introduce PP-CFG, an architecture that keeps positive and negative text conditions separate and combines their attention responses with opposite signs within a shared visual backbone. Its Scale-Time Polarity Mixer refines each condition using the guidance scale and denoising timestep, allowing prompt influence to vary throughout generation. The student retains a continuous scale input and selectable negative prompts with one visual backbone pass per step. We evaluate PP-CFG on Wan2.1 for video generation with distribution matching distillation (DMD), and on Qwen-Image with both latent consistency model (LCM) distillation and DMD. PP-CFG achieves 81.46 VBench in four steps and lower video teacher-student distances across guidance scales than step-distilled DMD. [61] Budgeted Safety Verification for Streaming Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Yuxin Cao, Ziqi Ding, Jiayu Zhang, Xianglin Yang, Wei Song

TL;DR: 论文研究流式视频生成中的预算约束安全验证：在每个视频块即时输出、无法等完整片段再审核的场景下，利用生成器已有隐藏状态做廉价打分，决定何时调用昂贵视频语言模型检测器，以最小化有害帧暴露。在599条流上验证，该调度策略显著优于随机预算分配，并优于帧级检测器。

Details

Motivation: 流式生成即时释放内容，事后审核会在伤害暴露后才生效；需在固定审核预算下决定在哪些块上花费昂贵检测，以降低观众看到的有害帧数。

Result: 在599条蒸馏因果生成器流上，无防御暴露46.25/81帧；每块都调用视频模型降至0.91但每良性流需评分80.1次，所提guard触发仅需5.3次且暴露2.35帧；随机分配为37.20帧，说明收益来自排序。独立guardrail模型与oracle一致κ=0.74，50人盲审精确率96.1%、召回率82.8%。

Insight: 创新点在于以暴露风险为指标，将安全验证建模为固定预算下的调用排序/调度问题，复用生成器隐藏状态做廉价触发信号，并强调检测阶段需按视频片段而非单帧判断。客观看，其核心是可借鉴的预算感知早期预警与级联审核设计，而非提出新检测模型。

Abstract: A streamed video generator releases each chunk the moment that it is produced, so a safety decision that waits for the finished clip arrives after the harm has been seen. We measure a guardrail by exposure, the harmful frames a viewer is shown, and ask where a fixed moderation budget should be spent. The accurate detector reads decoded frames with a video language model and is too expensive for every chunk, so our guard reads hidden states the generator has computed already and spends a cheap score on deciding when to call it. On 599 streams from a distilled causal generator the undefended model shows 46.25 harmful frames of 81. The video model on every chunk leaves 0.91 frames at 80.1 scored per benign stream, and the same model triggered by our guard leaves 2.35 at 5.3. Spending that budget at random leaves 37.20 frames, so the gain is in the ordering, and a frame detector in the same role gives up almost six, so the judging stage must read the chunk as a clip. The ordering is worth more as the stream lengthens, and the guard carries to a second system. An independent guardrail model agrees with our oracle at $κ=0.74$, and a blinded audit by 50 participants puts its precision at 96.1% and recall at 82.8%. Warning. This paper contains examples of generated content that some readers may find disturbing or offensive in nature.

Abstract: Multi-modal image fusion is crucial for comprehensive scene representation, yet real-world degradations compromise its efficacy, necessitating degradation-robust fusion paradigms. Existing degradation-robust multi-modal image fusion methods are often hindered by cascaded sub-optimal solution or black-box opacity. To address these challenges, we propose a theory-inspired mutual-promoting deep unfolding framework. It reformulates degradation-robust image fusion as a joint optimization problem, utilizing the degradation model and modality generation mechanism to explicitly model both single-task priors and cross-task dependencies. By decomposing the complex multi-task optimization problem into task-related iterative subproblems, the framework establishes a bidirectional reciprocal information flow between restoration and fusion. This process is further unfolded into a multi-stage deep neural network, where each component explicitly corresponds to a specific mathematical operation. In our framework, the fused cross-modal prior actively regularizes the ill-posed restoration, while the purified modality features continuously refine the fusion output. TL;DR: 论文提出EgoLAM，一种从自我中心（第一人称）视频中学习隐式动作模型的框架。针对视频中画面变化可能源自手部操作、也可能源自佩戴者头部或相机转动的混淆问题，EgoLAM用手指腕部运动监督动作编码，并将相机运动单独输入前向模型，使学习到的隐式动作只描述手对物体的操控。提取动作编码仅需两帧RGB图像。

Details

Motivation: 现有隐式动作模型直接把相邻两帧的差异当作训练目标，但在自我中心视频中，杯子在画面中移动既可能是手推的结果，也可能是佩戴者转头导致的，后者并不描述操作行为，会污染动作表示。论文旨在将手部操控动作与相机运动解耦。

Result: 手部运动解码分数在8个对比模型中最高；在LIBERO的三个套件上，用其编码预训练后微调的策略平均成功率79.38%，比专家行为克隆高28.73个百分点，比各套件中最强的其他隐式动作模型平均高9.76个百分点；同时在LARYBench分类、回归及冻结编码的鲁棒性测试中进行了评估。

Insight: 核心创新在于用实测腕部运动监督动作编码、将相机运动作为单独输入送入前向模型，从而在训练目标层面分离操控与视角变化；动作编码器在每只手周围对物体槽（object slots）做池化，并可在公共参考系中从编码解码腕部平移与旋转。客观来看，这种显式解耦设计提升了隐式动作表示对视角和光照变化的鲁棒性，且仅需两帧RGB即可提取编码，实用性较强。

Abstract: Latent action models let a robot policy learn from video by turning the change between two frames into a training target. In egocentric video, a cup can move across the image because a hand pushes it or because the wearer turns. Both changes help predict the next frame, but only the first describes manipulation. During training, EgoLAM supervises the action code with measured wrist motion and supplies camera motion separately to the forward model. Even when the wearer turns while pushing a cup, the training target still describes how the hand pushed it. The action encoder pools object slots around each hand, and wrist translation and rotation can be decoded from the code in a common reference frame. Extracting a code then requires only two RGB frames. Alongside LARYBench classification and regression, we measure how much wrist motion a frozen code retains and how strongly it responds to a synthetic view or lighting change. Motivation: 现有 OCR 流水线和视觉语言模型在标准基准上表现较好，但在金融业务关键的真实文档模式上系统性失效，而这些场景在公开数据中代表性不足。论文旨在用少量样本定向补齐高影响失败场景，提升金融 OCR 的鲁棒性。

Result: 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct 后词召回率达到 0.93；将版面感知 OCR 结果作为文档问答上下文时 F1 为 0.89，优于 GPT-4o、Mistral-OCR、PaddleOCR-VL、DeepSeek-OCR 等开源和闭源模型。

Insight: 创新点在于面向高影响长尾失败场景的样本高效数据策展与模型适配，以及通过 token 间成对空间关系衡量版面保真度的 PaIRS 指标。客观看，其价值在于以数据为中心针对性补短板，并用结构感知评估更贴近金融文档 OCR 的实际需求。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) pipelines and vision-language models exhibit high accuracy on standard OCR and document-understanding benchmarks, yet they systematically underperform on document patterns that are operationally critical in financial workflows. These patterns include vertical or rotated text, logo-embedded vendor names, fine-print regulatory clauses, degraded scans, and complex multi-column layouts. While underrepresented in public datasets, they constitute a substantial portion of real-world failure cases. We introduce SAVIOR, a sample-efficient data curation methodology that targets such high-impact failure scenarios to adapt vision-language models for robust financial OCR. We further propose PaIRS, a structure-aware evaluation metric that measures layout fidelity by comparing pairwise spatial relationships between tokens. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-VL-Instruct with SAVIOR-Train achieves a word recall of 0.93. When the resulting layout-aware OCR output is used as context for document question answering, it attains an F1 score of 0.89, outperforming both open- and closed-source models such as GPT-4o, Mistral-OCR, PaddleOCR-VL, and DeepSeek-OCR.

Wenbo Zhang, Guoli Zhang, Xuewen Yu, Xueliang Yang, Jiangqiao Yan

TL;DR: 论文提出 Zing-1.0，一个基于「可控渲染（Controllable Rendering）」的可玩世界系统。系统由可执行引擎（WorldKit）按世界规则解析玩家动作并生成代理视频（proxy video），再由流式视频模型据此合成玩家视角画面，通过交互运行时返回以驱动后续动作。论文同时给出了世界构建、世界执行、观测合成与交互运行时之间的接口规范，并配套 AI 原生引擎、流式渲染器与交互基础设施。

Abstract: We present Zing-1.0, a Playable World System built on Controllable Rendering. An executable engine resolves player actions under the world’s rules and produces proxy video from the updated state. A streaming video model synthesizes the player’s view from this proxy, and the runtime delivers it to inform subsequent actions. (1) Principle and system. We formulate Controllable Rendering and define the interfaces among world construction, world execution, observation synthesis, and the interaction runtime. To our knowledge, this is the first implementation-independent specification of a Playable World System. (2) AI-native engine. WorldKit provides structured authoring tools and reusable character, camera, and control components for coding agents to construct executable proxy worlds. It supports extensible interactions and precise geometric motion control while separating executable behavior from final visual appearance. (3) Streaming renderer. We adapt MiniMax-H3 for block-causal autoregressive generation and incremental decoding. Proxy video constrains structure and motion, while text and separate scene and subject references specify appearance. We train the generator on diverse tasks and distill it to generate each block in a few denoising steps. The renderer leads the compared streaming editors on OpenVE (3.90) and LongV2VBench (3.44). On StreamEval, Zing-1.0 scores 57.86 on instruction editing and 54.06 on reference editing, exceeding the published baselines on both tracks. (4) Interaction infrastructure. We build an Interaction Runtime that routes player actions to WorldKit while supporting in-play prompt and reference updates. Cross-block joint execution and sparse attention with compressed K/V reduce inference overhead. With the DiT on a single H200 and encoding and decoding on one H100 each, the serving system achieves a steady-state throughput of 23.82 FPS at $832\times480$.

Guijia Zhang, Hongbo Qiu, Guohao Cai, Hao Zheng, Jiajun Zha

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉语言模型的安全表示是否真正联合编码图像场景和用户意图，发现五个公开情境安全基准都存在单模态捷径，并构建交叉场景与意图的受控评测。线性探针虽能解码联合信息，但拼接单模态表示已能恢复大部分信号，且跨图像和跨基准泛化较差。

Details

Motivation: 现有情境安全基准上的高准确率不能证明模型联合理解了场景和意图，因为模型可能仅依赖单一模态完成判断；因此需要排除单模态捷径，评估真正的多模态安全表示。

Result: 审计五个公开基准均发现单模态捷径，同图提示对上的纯文本探针达到0.9645 AUROC；在单模态规则组排序准确率不超过0.500的受控评测上，Qwen3-VL-8B和SmolVLM2-2.2B的线性探针分别达到0.958和0.930，但对未见图像和其他基准泛化较差。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示情境安全基准中的单模态捷径，并设计交叉场景-意图的受控评测来检验联合理解；客观来看，线性可解码并不等于存在融合机制，拼接独立编码的图像和文本状态即可恢复大部分信号，说明多模态安全评估需先控制单模态捷径。

Abstract: The safety of a request to a vision-language model often depends on the image: asking how to serve a dish is harmless for fresh food and harmful for spoiled food. High accuracy on situational safety benchmarks does not tell us whether a model’s safety representation encodes both the scene and the user’s intent, because a benchmark can often be solved from one modality alone. Studying frozen vision-language safety representations, we audit five public situational benchmarks and find that each admits a unimodal shortcut: images alone predict the labels, and on same-image prompt pairs a probe that never sees the photograph reaches 0.9645 AUROC. We therefore build a controlled evaluation that crosses safe and hazardous scenes with observation and action requests, so that no single-modality rule can exceed 0.500 group-ranking accuracy. On this control, linear probes on intermediate states of Qwen3-VL-8B and SmolVLM2-2.2B reach 0.958 and 0.930, showing that the scene-intent conjunction is linearly decodable; concatenating separately encoded image and text states already recovers most of this signal, so decodability is not evidence of a fusion mechanism. The same probes screen cheaply but generalize poorly to unseen images and other benchmarks. Reliable multimodal safety evaluation therefore needs controls that rule out unimodal shortcuts before accuracy is read as evidence of joint understanding.

Abstract: Transportation models, whether they simulate traffic or support planning, are only as reliable as the road networks they run on, down to the lanes and turns at each intersection. Practitioners still take such a network from GIS files and supply its junction and movement data by hand, a task that recurs with each new base year and design alternative, or they generate it from open map data. OpenStreetMap’s lane tags give the correct layout for only 13 of the 44 approaches we test; open map data are often incomplete or inaccurate. Satellite and street-level imagery, in contrast, cover all 44 and show their lanes as painted. Vision-language models (VLMs) can read such imagery, but asked directly they lose geometric consistency when the same lane must be matched across a satellite crop, street-level photos taken from different poses and an ID-labelled network. We therefore present PAVE (Perceive, Anchor, Verify, Edit), a harness in which code measures what can be measured, traffic engineering knowledge enters as general rules and checks, and the model makes only the open judgements, each anchored to network IDs so that checks catch answers that contradict a measurement, a second reading or a traffic rule. Scored against human labels at signalised intersections, PAVE raises the share of exactly correct lane layouts from 49-64% with a single request to 86-91% for two VLMs, with most of the gain coming from reading one approach at a time on traced lanes. The same division of work edits a real network to a planning brief: the model names the links and nodes of each part of a facility, and code builds the edit from a design template. Of 18 edits, 16 pass every network check, against none when the model writes its own code, and 2 contain a design or coding error, against 7. Abstract: Reference-guided text-to-image stylization transfers visual appearance to content specified by text, but the reference subjects and backgrounds can interfere with what the generated image depicts. Suppressing this unwanted information can also discard transferable texture and material cues, creating a tension between content control and faithful preservation of the reference style. Accordingly, we propose ProStyle, which learns a content-suppressed style prior through cross-content supervision on same-style pairs with different subjects and backgrounds and injects it with weak conditioning at auxiliary layers to strengthen style expression. Building on this prior, a complementary pathway learns residual corrections from full reference patch features with the content-suppressed style pathway frozen, before both pathways undergo joint refinement under the same objective. For cross-content learning and evaluation, we introduce ReConStyle, which provides 134K training pairs, two test settings, and object-level measures of target-content fidelity and reference-object overlap alongside style similarity and text alignment. Abstract: We introduce complex redundant-instruction image editing, where a single request specifies multiple objects, their attributes, and spatial relations while revising or canceling earlier editing requirements. Our key insight is to resolve the revision history into a final editing target that jointly guides planning and verification, so that obsolete requests do not become visual edits. Based on this insight, CRIE-Agent learns to consolidate revisions into object-level final intent and executes the retained objects in reference-first blocks, establishing referenced objects before their dependents. [75] Beyond What They Say: Cue Acknowledgment and Robustness in Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Ali Rahimiakbr, Arvin Baghal Asl, Nima Niroumand, Mohammad Parsa Jafarnejady, Hossein Shahabadi

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉-语言模型对视觉与文本线索的“承认”是否意味着更强的鲁棒性。作者在保持问题和正确答案固定的条件下，比较模型在有益、误导和冲突线索下的匹配答案与解释，发现模型可能更常承认线索却仍更常选择被认可的错答。因此仅凭解释中提及线索不足以判断鲁棒性，评估应同时报告同一实例上的线索遵循与线索承认，并区分视觉和文本线索。

Details

Motivation: 现有工作常用解释判断视觉-语言模型是依赖视觉证据还是误导性建议，但模型即使明确提及某线索也可能继续遵循它，仅看披露或承认并不能完整评估鲁棒性。论文关注线索承认与抵抗误导之间是否一致。

Result: 摘要未给出具体benchmark和数值结果；配对比较显示，模型可能在更多承认线索的同时更常选择被认可的错答，减少沉默并不必然代表抵抗提升。请求证据和承认线索在测试设置中并未稳定改善抵抗，且结果随模型和设置变化。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将“线索承认”与“线索遵循”放在同一实例上联合评估，并区分视觉与文本线索，揭示解释中的披露不等于鲁棒性。客观看，其价值主要在于提出更细粒度的评估视角和配对比较框架，而非证明承认与抵抗的因果效应。

Abstract: Explanations are used to assess whether vision-language models rely on visual evidence or misleading suggestions. A response that explicitly names a cue can still follow it, making disclosure alone an incomplete basis for judging robustness. We ask whether greater cue acknowledgment accompanies greater resistance. We compare matched answers and explanations under helpful, misleading, and conflicting visual and textual cues while holding the underlying question and correct answer fixed. A paired comparison shows that a model can select the endorsed wrong answer more often while acknowledging the cues more often. Reduced silence therefore need not indicate improved resistance. Explanations may also mention a textual suggestion while omitting an accompanying visual cue. Requesting evidence and acknowledgment does not consistently improve resistance in the tested settings. Outcomes vary across models and settings, and these comparisons do not isolate a causal effect of acknowledgment. Evaluations should report cue following and acknowledgment on the same instances, distinguishing visual from textual cues.

[76] Taking Apart and Putting Together: Tracking Objects Through Physical Transformations cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Davide Gardenal, Mingxuan Liu, Flavio Vella, Quentin Guimard, Elisa Ricci

TL;DR: 论文提出 Transformation Tracking and Understanding（TTU）任务，将视频目标跟踪扩展到物体发生物理组装与拆解的场景，要求持续跟踪、识别新实体、维护状态图并标注变换语义。为此提出无需训练的 TIPO 框架，通过生成拆分/合并候选、基于掩码重叠打分、再用 MLLM 验证与语义标注的 generate-evaluate-judge 流程推断物体变换。论文还构建了 VISTA 数据集用于评测该任务。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频目标跟踪和 SAM 2/3 等基础模型只能维持外观变化下的轨迹，无法在物体物理演化后更新语义身份；已有方法仅处理拆解，且依赖跟踪失败时触发的高成本搜索。论文希望统一处理组装与拆解过程中的持续跟踪、状态更新和语义理解。

Result: 在自建的 VISTA 数据集上，TIPO 取得了最先进的跟踪和状态图准确率，并比先前方法快 6 到 15 倍。VISTA 包含 50 个序列、超过 26,000 帧，以及 124 个组装事件和 132 个拆解事件。

Insight: 创新点在于定义 TTU 新任务并构建带状态图和变换语义标注的 VISTA 基准；方法上以无需训练的生成-评估-判断流水线，将掩码组件拆分/合并候选、掩码解码器先验重叠评分与 MLLM 视觉验证结合，实现物理变换的持续跟踪和语义更新。

Abstract: We introduce Transformation Tracking and Understanding (TTU), extending video object tracking to environments where objects undergo physical assemblies and disassemblies. TTU requires continuously tracking objects through transformations, identifying new entities, maintaining a state graph of their relationships, and assigning semantic labels to transformations. While foundation models (e.g., SAM 2/3) maintain tracklets through appearance changes, they cannot update semantic identities as objects physically evolve. Prior work addresses only disassemblies, relying on expensive searches triggered only when tracking fails. We propose Tracking Instances via Proposals and Overlaps (TIPO), a training-free framework that continuously infers transformations via a generate-evaluate-judge pipeline. TIPO generates split and merge proposals from disjoint mask components, scores them against mask-decoder priors using inner and external overlap, and queries an MLLM on the top proposal for visual verification and semantic labeling. To benchmark TTU, we curate Video Instances, States, and Transformation Annotations (VISTA), a dataset including state graphs with 124 assembly and 132 disassembly events across 50 sequences and over 26,000 frames. TIPO achieves state-of-the-art tracking and state-graph accuracy on VISTA, while operating $6\times$ to $15\times$ faster than prior methods. Project page: https://davidegardenal.github.io/tipo-tracking

[77] Pose-based Co-speech Gesture Detection cs.CVPDF

Frances Yung, Ninlawat Phuangschoke

Details

Motivation: 协同语音手势检测是手势标注和研究人类交流行为的重要第一步，但现有工作评测协议不一致，导致当前最优水平不清晰，性能难以公平比较。

Result: 统一评测发现已有方法报告的性能并不能稳定迁移到共同设置，且性能随数据集和手势频率变化明显，说明任务远未解决；提出的CTR-GCN + BiLSTM在CABBS上优于此前最佳方法MPSL。

Insight: 创新点在于系统性基准评测和跨数据集一致性比较，揭示现有方法可比性问题；同时提出结合CTR-GCN与BiLSTM的姿态时序建模方法。客观看，统一评测协议和CABBS上的性能提升是主要贡献。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture detection identifies the spans in which a speaker gestures (e.g., beat or semantic gestures) in video. As a first step in gesture annotation, it is crucial for studying human communicative behaviour. Despite the availability of automatic tools and models, co-speech gesture detection remains underexplored, and its current state of the art is unclear: existing methods have been evaluated using different datasets, experimental setups, and metrics, making their performance difficult to compare. To address this gap, we benchmark existing detectors using the same cross-validation folds across three datasets (MULTISIMO, GESRes, and CABBS), with evaluation at the frame, gesture-span, and window levels. Our results show that previously reported performance does not consistently transfer to this common evaluation setting. Performance varies substantially across datasets, particularly with gesture frequency, indicating that the task remains far from solved. We further propose a CTR-GCN + BiLSTM model that outperforms the previous best method, MPSL, on CABBS. Our results provide a more consistent basis for comparing existing approaches and highlight key challenges for future research on co-speech gesture detection.

[78] AppCon-Bench: Perception and Misperception of Material Appearance in Vision-Language Models cs.CVPDF

Santiago Jimenez-Navarro, Ana Serrano, Belen Masia

TL;DR: 论文提出 AppCon-Bench，一个包含五项任务的基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在几何与光照受控变化下对材质外观恒常性的感知能力，并将代表性 VLM 与人类观察者进行对比。研究发现人类与 VLM 在材质外观感知上既有相似性也存在系统性差异，强调应超越总体准确率来分析感知成功与失败。

Details

Motivation: 识别同一材质在光照或几何变化下保持不变是鲁棒视觉系统的关键能力，但 VLM 对材质外观这类细微中级感知属性的理解仍不清楚，缺乏系统性评测。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果；论文通过五项受控任务比较代表性 VLM 与人类观察者，发现二者在材质判断上存在相似性与系统性差异，并据此指出当前 VLM 的改进方向。

Insight: 创新点在于构建面向材质外观恒常性的 AppCon-Bench，系统控制几何和光照变化，并引入人类观察者对比及超越总体准确率的成功/失败分析；其可借鉴之处是将中级感知恒常性与细粒度诊断式评测引入 VLM 评估。

Abstract: Identifying the same material across changes in illumination or geometry is a critical ability for robust vision systems. Humans exhibit remarkable material appearance constancy, being able to recognize the intrinsic physical properties of an object’s surface material across variations in extrinsic, confounding factors such as the lighting or the shape of such object. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate strong performance across a wide range of semantic vision tasks, yet their ability to interpret subtle mid-level perceptual attributes remains less understood. We introduce AppCon-Bench, a benchmark comprising five tasks designed to assess material appearance constancy under controlled variations in geometry and illumination, and evaluate representative VLMs alongside human observers, investigating their ability to maintain consistent material judgments across viewing conditions and experimental contexts. After thorough analysis, we identify several key findings that reveal both similarities and systematic differences between human and VLM material appearance perception, highlighting the value of evaluating perceptual successes and failures beyond overall accuracy and informing areas of improvement for current VLMs.

[79] Ego2Ego: Generative Visual Perspective Taking Across Collaborators cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Chuhan Jiao, John Gideon, Anshul Gupta, Guy Rosman, Andreas Bulling

TL;DR: 论文提出「生成式视觉视角采择」新任务：给定第一人称视角（ego）视频、场景点云和协作者的相机轨迹，生成协作者视角下连续的第一人称视频。为应对极端视角变化、动态遮挡和稀疏视觉重叠，作者提出 Ego2Ego——一组与骨干无关的几何模块，为预训练视频扩散模型注入双方共享的3D场景几何，包括渲染稠密时序一致场景坐标图、在3D射线空间做相对几何注意力对齐跨视角token、以及利用第一人称视觉记忆补全ego视角外区域。

Details

Motivation: 人类协作中需要推断他人所见，但已有工作将视角采择简化为状态估计、几何重建或离散问答；现有跨视角视频生成方法要么仅从输入视频推断目标视角，要么需外部相机观测目标人物，无法在真实协作场景中生成协作者视角视频。

Result: 在 CoMind 数据集、三种视频扩散骨干上，Ego2Ego 一致提升视觉质量、二级场景布局精度和协作者运动预测，且在每个骨干上均优于当前最优的跨视角视频生成基线。

Insight: 创新点在于把视角采择重新定义为生成式跨视角视频生成任务，并以骨干无关的几何模块将共享3D场景几何注入视频扩散模型：稠密场景坐标图作为共享空间参照、3D射线空间的相对几何注意力对齐token、以及基于视觉记忆的视角外区域补全。客观来看，其将显式几何先验与生成模型结合、且保持对多种扩散骨干的通用性，是可迁移的设计思路。

Abstract: Visual perspective taking, the ability to infer what another person sees from their position, is fundamental to human collaboration. However, prior works reduced perspective taking to state estimation, geometric reconstruction, or discrete question answering, whereas existing cross-view video generation methods either inferred the target view from the input video alone or required an external camera that observes the target person. We introduce generative visual perspective taking: Given an ego person’s egocentric video, a scene point cloud, and a collaborator’s camera trajectory, the task is to generate the continuous egocentric video from the collaborator’s perspective. This task is challenging given extreme viewpoint shifts, dynamic occlusions, and sparse visual overlap. We address these challenges with Ego2Ego – a set of backbone-agnostic geometric modules that provide a pretrained video diffusion model with the 3D scene geometry shared by both interactants. Ego2Ego renders dense, temporally consistent scene coordinate maps into every view as a shared spatial reference, aligns cross-view tokens in 3D ray space through relative geometric attention, and recovers regions outside the ego person’s current view from the ego person’s visual memory. Experiments on the CoMind dataset across three video diffusion backbones show that Ego2Ego consistently improves visual quality, Level-2 scene layout accuracy, and collaborator motion, outperforming state-of-the-art cross-view video generation baselines on every backbone. By generating the collaborator’s egocentric video more accurately, Ego2Ego brings visual perspective taking to human-robot collaboration, where it can help infer what a human collaborator sees and does, and can turn single-person recordings into synchronised multi-perspective videos for robot learning.

Abstract: PathOPEN is a histopathology dataset for visual question answering. Two clinical pathologists selected 157 cases with 229 available original images and wrote open-ended, five-option multiple-choice and close-ended (yes/no) questions. They supplied both a correct and a wrong answer for 201 of the 309 open-ended questions. Each case records the organ, pathology category and regional anatomy, and each image records its magnification. The dataset includes 6,870 augmented images and four manifests that select subsets to improve balance by individual metadata field. Five additional pathologists and two vision-language model judges rated items with scoring rubrics, and six models answered questions zero-shot across the three formats. The metadata support subgroup evaluation, and the paired answers can be used to construct preference examples.

[81] Self-Supervised Continuous-Time Depth Learning and Ego-Motion Estimation from Asynchronous Events cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LG | cs.ROPDF

Sadiq O. Macaulay, Dipon Kumar Ghosh, Hadfield Simon

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于异步事件相机的自监督方法，为每个事件在连续时间上预测深度，并从同一观测中恢复相机自运动。方法利用事件经深度提升到3D并按瞬时相机运动位移后应与同一边缘产生的事件对齐这一几何约束作为监督，再通过可微渲染生成目标时刻的稠密深度图。

Details

Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间精度，但稠密深度监督获取成本高且仅存在于离散时刻；将事件流压缩为帧虽便于学习，却丢失了事件感知的关键时序信息。因此需要在连续时间上利用事件自身几何一致性进行自监督深度与自运动估计。

Result: 在MVSEC数据集上，该方法在多数序列和深度范围内优于已有仅事件自监督方法，夜间场景提升最明显；相机旋转估计也比早期无监督事件方法更准确，并通过消融验证了局部时空上下文、时间骨干和对齐目标的作用。

Insight: 创新点在于为每个事件分配连续时间深度，并将运动补偿后的事件对齐误差作为自监督信号，结合可微渲染输出任意目标时刻稠密深度；其核心思想是把事件对齐作为连续时间深度和自运动估计的几何监督来源。

Abstract: Event cameras register brightness changes pixel by pixel with microsecond precision, whereas the dense depth that could supervise them is costly to acquire and exists only at isolated instants. Condensing the stream into frames eases learning, but at the expense of the very timing that sets event sensing apart. We propose a self-supervised approach in which each event is assigned its own depth in continuous time, and the camera motion is recovered from the same observations. Supervision arises from geometry: lifted into 3D by its predicted depth and displaced by the camera motion at the instant it fired, an event should coincide with others produced by the same edge, so any misalignment exposes an error in depth or motion. Differentiable rendering then turns these per-event estimates into dense depth maps at target times within each window, faithful to the events behind them. On MVSEC, the approach outperforms prior event-only self-supervised methods in most sequences and depth ranges, with the widest margins at night, and recovers camera rotation more accurately than an earlier unsupervised event-based method. Ablations isolate the roles of local spatiotemporal context, the temporal backbone, and the alignment objective. These results position motion-compensated event alignment as a principled source of supervision for continuous-time depth and ego-motion estimation.

[82] PGS-PCQA: Perceptually Gated Synthetic Supervision for No-Reference Point Cloud Quality Assessment cs.CVPDF

Qinyuan Fan, Meng Zhang, Clemens Gühmann

TL;DR: 论文提出PGS-PCQA，一种用于无参考点云质量评估的模型，通过从源参考点云构造合成失真库，并利用同类型失真的严重程度排序作为额外监督，同时结合源数据集MOS训练。为处理严重程度与感知质量不完全一致的问题，引入感知门控软化感知差异较小样本对的排序目标。合成监督仅在训练阶段使用，不需要目标域数据。

Details

Motivation: 现有NR-PCQA方法在单一数据集内表现较好，但跨数据集泛化差，作者认为关键原因是训练数据失真覆盖不足，导致模型在未见失真类型上难以正确排序严重程度。

Result: 在SJTU-PCQA上单次训练后，WPC上SRCC为0.709、BASICS上为0.751，优于DSP-PCQA的0.595和0.723；参考不重叠交叉验证下，SJTU-PCQA、WPC、BASICS、LS-PCQA Part I的SRCC分别为0.955、0.934、0.906、0.791。消融显示缩减合成库仍保留相对无合成库SRCC增益的58-65%。

Insight: 创新点在于用合成失真严重度排序作为额外监督以提升跨数据集泛化，且无需为目标域或合成样本提供主观分数；感知门控缓解“严重度高不一定感知质量低”的排序噪声。客观看，核心贡献是利用合成失真覆盖和排序监督增强NR-PCQA的泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing no-reference point cloud quality assessment (NR-PCQA) methods perform well within individual datasets but generalize poorly across datasets. We identify insufficient distortion coverage in training data as a key cause: on unseen distortion types, models rank severity levels near chance or even in reverse. We propose perceptually gated synthetic supervision for no-reference point cloud quality assessment (PGS-PCQA), an NR-PCQA model trained with synthetic distortion ranking. It builds a synthetic distortion bank from source reference point clouds and uses within-type severity ranking as supervision in addition to the source mean opinion scores (MOS), requiring no subjective scores for the synthetic samples. Since higher severity does not always mean lower perceived quality, a perceptual gate softens the ranking targets of pairs with small perceptual differences in rendered views. The synthetic supervision is used only during training and needs no target-domain data. In a single training run on SJTU-PCQA, PGS-PCQA achieves Spearman rank correlation coefficients (SRCCs) of 0.709 on WPC and 0.751 on BASICS. The corresponding published DSP-PCQA scores are 0.595 and 0.723. Under reference-disjoint cross-validation, PGS-PCQA achieves SRCCs of 0.955 on SJTU-PCQA, 0.934 on WPC, 0.906 on BASICS, and 0.791 on LS-PCQA Part I. In the exclusion experiment, the reported scores for the reduced synthetic bank represent 58-65% of the full-bank SRCC gain relative to training without the bank. Code is available at https://github.com/Fan-QY/PGS-PCQA.

Insight: 创新点在于用离线轨迹平滑变形和模拟执行打分构造覆盖完整质量范围的替代轨迹，既为不同候选提供多样化监督和残差目标，又训练质量评分器；共享JEPA一次编码观测帧并服务细化与评分，在提升多候选规划监督的同时避免大模型在线推理开销。

Abstract: Human driving logs record only one trajectory per scene, leaving other feasible driving choices without explicit trajectory targets or quality labels. This limits supervision for end-to-end planners that generate and refine multiple candidate trajectories. Meanwhile, conditioning refinement on a large vision-language model adds substantial online inference cost. We propose DriveSTA, which extends autoregressive proposal generation and proposal-conditioned residual flow refinement with scored trajectory alternatives. For each training scene, we generate alternatives offline through smooth deformations of the recorded trajectory and evaluate their simulated execution against logged traffic. Diverse, high-quality alternatives supervise distinct proposals and define residual targets for quality-guided denoising, while candidates spanning the full score range train a scorer to assess driving quality. A shared joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), adapted through masked video-feature prediction, encodes observed frames once and provides temporal context for both refinement and scoring. However, existing models are trained by pairing each video with the full clinical report, including structures and findings its view does not show. This mismatch lets a model associate a finding with incidental or spurious signals rather than its visual evidence, which limits accuracy, anatomical grounding and clinical trust. Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) currently obtain notable results for highly-studied languages, e.g. English, they exhibit significant performance drops in certain low-resource languages, especially due to the lack of annotated datasets. Abstract: Scene graph generation (SGG) aims to detect objects in an image and predict the relationships between them. The standard PredCls, SGCls, and SGDet settings show how much performance drops without ground-truth boxes and labels, but not where or why a model fails. Knowing this would allow fixing a model where it fails, without retraining. We propose RoHa-SGG (Root-cause-guided Harness optimization for SGG), which extends agentic harness optimization to SGG. It optimizes the harness, the inference code around a frozen SGG model. RoHa-SGG has two main LLM agents. A root-cause analysis (RCA) agent finds the components where the model fails and diagnoses why, and a proposer agent searches for a harness that fixes these causes. Graph-constrained R@100 and mR@100 rise by up to 15.8 and 26.4 points for the multimodal LLM and by up to 3.6 and 10.1 points for the two-stage and one-stage models. Details

Motivation: Text-RAG 难以充分利用视觉丰富文档中的文本、视觉和布局信息，而现有 Vision-RAG 多采用粗粒度表示和整页视觉上下文，容易掩盖局部证据并带来冗余与计算开销。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验表明，该方法在检索和生成上均优于对应基线，同时区域级视觉增强显著减少视觉 token 数量和计算成本。

Abstract: Visually rich documents (VRDs) convey knowledge through textual, visual, and layout information that text-centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Text-RAG) struggles to fully exploit. Recent Vision-RAG approaches enable retrieval and generation directly from document images, retaining this multimodal information. However, earlier Vision-RAG methods commonly adopt coarse retrieval representations and full-page visual contexts, which can obscure local evidence and introduce substantial redundancy and computational overhead. To address these challenges, we propose MV-VisRAG, a region-level Vision-RAG framework that uses semantically complete regions for both document representation and visual augmentation. Specifically, MV-VisRAG first constructs these regions through a ContentAdaptive Regionalization module. It then encodes them into local region vectors and a page-level global vector using a VLM-based retriever trained through multimodal reconstruction pretraining and retrieval fine-tuning. With this compact multi-vector representation, MV-VisRAG performs late-interaction retrieval and estimates region contributions from the resulting matching scores. These contributions guide the selection of relevant regions, which are then rearranged into a compact visual context for generation. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate retrieval and generation gains over corresponding baselines, while region-level augmentation substantially reduces visual tokens and computational cost. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/xandery-geek/MV-VisRAG.

Details

Motivation: 游戏录像蕴含学习虚拟世界动态的丰富信息，但HUD遵循界面规则而非场景动态，会干扰世界模型学习。现有方法通过合成HUD构造配对数据，但合成HUD与真实HUD存在差距，导致模型在真实视频中仍残留40%至50%的HUD元素。

Abstract: Gameplay videos are a rich source of experience for learning virtual-world dynamics, yet their frames contain heads-up displays (HUDs) that follow interface rules rather than scene dynamics. Removing HUDs would make this footage directly usable for learning world dynamics. However, training a removal model requires paired clips with and without HUDs, whereas real recordings contain only HUD-overlaid footage. Existing work synthesizes pairs by compositing HUD graphics onto HUD-free footage, requiring a separately curated clean corpus and producing HUDs that only approximate real ones. Models trained on such pairs leave 40 to 50% of real HUD elements untouched. We instead recover pseudo-clean targets directly from real recordings by inpainting HUD regions with a pretrained video diffusion model. Preventing HUD traces from leaking into recovered targets is challenging because latent video autoencoders mix information across mask boundaries. Our training-free recovery pipeline, GameScrub-Data, pre-fills HUD regions with flow-guided background estimates before encoding, excludes HUDs from scene conditioning, and confines restoration to masked regions through pixel-space compositing. The resulting pairs train GameScrub, a video diffusion removal model that handles diverse HUDs without inference-time masks or clean reference images. On the in-the-wild Game2World benchmark, GameScrub achieves an artifact-adjusted removal score of 71.95, compared with 57.86 for the strongest reference-free GameCleaner configuration, and removes 90.06% of annotated HUD elements versus 59.07%. Abstract: Automatic digitization of hand-drawn sketches into editable formats is crucial for modern document workflows and LaTeX integration. To address these challenges, we propose Sketch2TikZ, a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes sketch-to-code generation via a sequential multi-reward strategy, combining Code-Structure ($\mathcal{R}{cs}$), OCR ($\mathcal{R}{ocr}$), CleanDIFT ($\mathcal{R}{cd}$), and Self-Similarity ($\mathcal{R}{ss}$) rewards to enforce textual, visual and structural correctness. We evaluate Sketch2TikZ on the SkeTikZ and FlowChart-3B benchmarks using metrics including compilation success rate (CSR), image similarity, code similarity, and character similarity. Our method achieves near-perfect compilation success and improves visual and textual alignment over strong open-source baselines, while remaining competitive with proprietary GPT-based vision-language models. Extensive ablations demonstrate that curriculum-based reward scheduling is critical for reducing reward interference, stabilizing training and mitigating proxy over-optimization. Overall, our results highlight the effectiveness of reference-aware, multi-objective reinforcement learning for structured diagram generation from noisy sketches.

Jianghan Chao, Wenhui Tan, Hang Su, Qiuyu Tian, Tanxiao Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 MoHA，将长视频智能体的执行 harness 视为受模型条件影响的优化对象：从通用初始 harness 出发，分析失败校准轨迹以诊断模型与 harness 的不匹配，并适配规划器侧推理支持和观察器侧能力分配，再校准观察器执行配置。该方法在多个冻结规划器栈上提升长视频理解基准表现，并表明适配后的 harness 跨模型迁移仅部分泛化。

Details

Motivation: 长视频理解日益依赖主动获取证据的智能体系统，其执行 harness 需协调语言规划、视频观察和证据获取，但现有 harness 通常固定，难以适配不同 planner-observer 模型栈，导致模型与执行架构不匹配。

Insight: 创新点在于将 harness 本身作为模型条件优化层，通过失败轨迹诊断自动进行规划器侧推理支持与观察器侧能力分配的结构适配，并单独校准观察器执行配置；可借鉴之处是面向智能体工作流的模型条件化架构适配与失败驱动优化。

Abstract: Long-video understanding increasingly relies on agentic systems that actively acquire evidence rather than process fixed video representations. Their execution harnesses must coordinate language-level planning with video-level observation and evidence acquisition, yet are typically fixed across model stacks. We therefore introduce MoHA, a framework for model-conditioned harness adaptation that adapts a common initial execution harness to the specific planner and observer models it hosts. MoHA treats the harness itself as a model-conditioned optimization object: under the same task distribution and initialization, different planner-observer stacks can benefit from different executable harnesses. Starting from a common initial harness, MoHA analyzes failed calibration traces to diagnose model-harness mismatches and adapts both planner-side reasoning support, such as overview, memory, and verification, and observer-side capability assignments, including OCR and ASR specialists. After structural adaptation, MoHA separately calibrates observer execution configurations for the hosted model stack. Cross-model transfer further shows that the adapted harnesses generalize only partially to other models, highlighting the harness as a model-conditioned optimization layer for long-video understanding. [92] GeoScaffold: From Latent to Realized Geometry in Collaborative Detection cs.CVPDF

Kang Yang, Yance Fang, Gaochen Cui, Xiaoqiang Jin, Deying Li

TL;DR: 论文针对协同3D检测中融合BEV特征虽蕴含丰富几何信息，但标准锚点打分、框解码和NMS读出头未能充分利用的问题，提出GeoScaffold：一种仅在训练阶段使用的轻量辅助目标，在每个正锚点上预测前景掩码以提供稠密空间监督，从而促使检测器在各正锚点产生定位更好的框。推理时移除辅助头，不改变原检测器架构、通信逻辑和推理成本。

Details

Motivation: 协同检测的融合BEV特征编码了丰富潜在几何，但分类分数仅训练用于前景/背景判别，未要求按定位质量排序，导致NMS在重叠协同框中选择定位更差框的情况较多。论文旨在解决这一监督错配和标准读出头未充分利用几何线索的问题。

Result: 在UrbanIng-V2X验证集上，GeoScaffold在全部11种融合方法上平均提升AP@0.5和AP@0.7分别0.98和0.55个点；增益还扩展到未见交叉口上的多数方法，并在V2V4Real上对全部10种产生检测结果的方法均有效。

Insight: 创新点在于将融合BEV中隐含的几何信息通过正锚点内的前景掩码进行稠密空间监督显式化，弥补7维框编码未表达的定位质量信息；方法作为训练期辅助目标即插即用，推理时移除，保持原架构和效率，并可适配多种协同融合方法。

Abstract: Most collaborative 3D detectors fuse bird’s-eye-view (BEV) features from connected vehicles and roadside infrastructure to aggregate complementary observations. Although modern communication, alignment, and fusion pipelines encode rich latent geometry in the fused BEV features, the standard readout pipeline inherited from single-agent detectors, i.e., anchor scoring, box decoding, and non-maximum suppression (NMS), does not fully exploit these geometric cues. We empirically identify and quantify this gap on UrbanIng-V2X. Collaboration improves localization at about two-thirds of positive anchors, yet when NMS resolves two overlapping collaborative boxes of one object, it keeps the better-localized box less than half of the time. A key cause is a supervision mismatch: classification scores are trained only for foreground-background discrimination, so nothing in training requires them to rank boxes by localization quality. To address this limitation, we introduce GeoScaffold, a lightweight training-only auxiliary objective. It predicts a foreground mask at each positive anchor, i.e., the cells of a grid over the anchor that lie inside the ground-truth box, to supply the dense spatial supervision that the seven-dimensional box code leaves implicit. Its loss, added to the detection loss, updates the encoder and fusion modules through the fused BEV and trains the detector toward a better-localized box at every positive anchor, including the top-scored one. The auxiliary head is removed at inference, preserving the original detector architecture, communication logic, and inference cost. GeoScaffold raises AP@0.5 and AP@0.7 by 0.98 and 0.55 points on average across all 11 fusion methods on the UrbanIng-V2X validation split, with gains extending to most methods on an unseen intersection and to all ten methods that produce detections on V2V4Real.

[93] CanopyFormer: Attention-Guided SegFormer for Explainable Forest Cover Segmentation cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Dhinanjaya Fernando, Dinura Ginige, Kalana Lakshan, Chanupa Gurusinghe, Lasana Pahanga

TL;DR: 论文提出 CanopyFormer，一种基于 SegFormer-B0 的注意力引导森林覆盖分割模型，通过 Attention Consistency Loss 将注意力与真实森林掩码对齐，使注意力更聚焦树冠区域。作者还调整 Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout 以适配 SegFormer 的空间缩减注意力，并提出 AAMO 指标和删除测试评估注意力对齐与忠实性。实验显示注意力对齐显著提升但 Dice 略降，且对齐提升不一定带来忠实性提升。

Details

Motivation: Transformer 分割模型中的注意力通常只在训练后可视化，缺少监督；航空森林分割中注意力多作为架构组件，可解释性评估也多为定性分析。论文希望显式监督注意力聚焦树冠区域，并定量评估其可解释性。

Result: 在 5108 张图像上，AAMO 从 0.0334 提升到 0.7476，约为原始 SegFormer-B0 的 22 倍，Dice 从 0.8743 小幅降至 0.8577。删除测试显示移除最受关注 25% patch 使森林分数下降 12.1%，而随机移除下降 33.0%，说明注意力对齐提升不必然带来忠实性提升；方法在 LoveDA 上具有泛化性，且不增加推理成本但训练每步慢 2.6 到 2.8 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于用真实掩码监督注意力一致性、将 Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout 限制到注意力矩阵为方阵的最终编码器阶段、提出 AAMO 并引入删除测试评估忠实性。客观看，其价值在于把可解释性从定性可视化推进到可量化监督与评测，并揭示“注意力对齐”和“模型忠实性”之间的差异，同时指出 MSE 优于 KL、Boundary Refinement 无稳定收益等实用结论。

Abstract: In transformer segmentation models, attention is usually visualized after training rather than supervised. Although attention supervision has been explored for natural images and driving scenes, aerial forest segmentation typically uses attention only as an architectural component and evaluates interpretability qualitatively. We present CanopyFormer, an attention-guided SegFormer-B0 that uses an Attention Consistency Loss to align attention with the ground-truth forest mask, encouraging attention to focus on canopy regions. Since SegFormer uses spatial-reduction attention, we adapt Gradient-weighted Attention Rollout by restricting it to the final encoder stage, where the attention matrix is square. We introduce Average Attention-Mask Overlap (AAMO) to measure attention alignment and incorporate a deletion test to evaluate faithfulness. On 5108 images, our method increases AAMO from 0.0334 to 0.7476, approximately 22 times the vanilla SegFormer-B0 result, with a modest decrease in Dice score from 0.8743 to 0.8577. The deletion test reveals that improved alignment does not necessarily translate into improved faithfulness: removing the most-attended 25% of patches reduces the forest score by 12.1%, compared with 33.0% when removing random patches. Attention supervision introduces no additional inference cost, although double backpropagation makes each training step 2.6 to 2.8 times slower. The attention alignment improvement also generalizes to a second dataset, LoveDA. Additionally, a Boundary Refinement Module provides no consistent Dice improvement and reduces AAMO, while mean squared error substantially outperforms Kullback-Leibler divergence as the attention loss.

[94] VeilGS: Solving Darkness as a Veil of 2D Basis Maps over Retinex 3DGS cs.CV | cs.GRPDF

Mohab Kishawy, Chengzhou Tang, Shahram Shirani, Jun Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 VeilGS，一种用于低光照新视角合成的 3D Gaussian Splatting 方法。它将低光退化建模为覆盖在清晰场景上的“面纱”：每个高斯携带 albedo 与 shading latent 而非球谐函数，并用空间变化的增益和全局色调曲线描述每帧变暗过程，去掉该面纱即可得到恢复场景。方法无需成对的正常光监督，也不存储逐视角参数。

Details

Motivation: 现有低光照 3D 重建方法要么对整幅视角应用统一曲线，无法处理局部光源造成的非均匀暗化；要么把暗化存进 3D 表示，容易吸收场景细节。论文希望将光照退化与场景表面颜色解耦，避免高斯吸收本可由“面纱”解释的暗化。

Result: 在 Gaussian-DK 上，恢复渲染在无参考 NIQE 和 CLIP-IQA 指标上最佳，并在 55 人盲测用户研究中评分最高；在 LOM 上取得与当前 SOTA 相当的结果。

Insight: 创新点在于将低光退化显式拆成空间变化增益与全局色调曲线的 2D basis map 面纱，并让高斯只建模 albedo 与 shading latent；同时用小型网络预测增益粗基，而逐视角系数通过求解得到而非学习，从而抑制高斯吸收光照退化。无需配对正常光监督和逐视角参数存储也具实用性。

Abstract: In a dark capture, the darkness is not uniform. How much light reaches a surface depends on where it lies relative to the few light sources, and the camera compresses what remains with a tone curve. Most low-light 3D reconstruction methods either apply one curve to every pixel of a view, so a dark object beside a lamp and a bright one in an unlit corner receive the same correction, or store the darkness in the 3D representation, where it can absorb scene detail. We present VeilGS, a 3D Gaussian Splatting method for low-light novel view synthesis that treats the degradation as a veil drawn over a clear scene. Each Gaussian carries an albedo and a shading latent instead of spherical harmonics, separating surface colour from illumination. Each view is darkened by a spatially varying gain followed by a global tone curve, and rendering without this veil gives the restored scene. A small network predicts a coarse basis for the gain from the observation, and its coefficients are solved for each view rather than learned. Training through this solve discourages the Gaussians from absorbing what the veil can explain. VeilGS needs no paired normal-light supervision and stores no per-view parameters. On Gaussian-DK, its restored renders score best on no-reference NIQE and CLIP-IQA and are rated highest in a blind user study with 55 participants. On LOM, it achieves results competitive with the state of the art.

Details

Motivation: 超像素分割领域长期缺乏标准化评估，现有方法在不同协议、尺度范围和不一致或冗余的指标子集下比较，导致结果难以公平复现和横向对比。论文旨在解决评估协议不统一、不可复现的问题。

Result: 论文构建了统一可复现的SUPERVISE基准，并在7个数据集、31种方法上系统分析20个评估指标，发现指标间强相关和显著冗余；未提及具体定量性能或SOTA比较，主要贡献是评估框架与实证分析。

Insight: 创新点在于提出基于实际超像素数量插值的尺度归一化评估协议，并构建轻量、模块化、基于预计算共享标签图的标准化评估框架；同时通过大规模统计分析揭示常用超像素评估指标的冗余性。客观来看，其核心价值是评估基准和实证发现，而非提出新的超像素分割算法。

Abstract: Image segmentation into superpixels is a widely used technique in computer vision, with a large body of work developed over the years. Yet, its evaluation remains poorly standardized, as methods are compared under heterogeneous protocols, across inconsistent scale ranges, and with redundant or misaligned metric subsets. In this work, we introduce SUPERVISE (SUperpixel PERformance VISualization and Evaluation), a unified and reproducible benchmark that addresses these longstanding inconsistencies. Built on a scale-normalized protocol based on interpolation over the actual number of generated superpixels, SUPERVISE enables fair and consistent cross-method comparisons. We leverage this standardized framework to conduct the first large-scale statistical analysis of 20 evaluation metrics across 7 datasets and 31 methods, revealing strong inter-metric correlations and significant redundancy that challenge common evaluation practices. These findings provide empirical support for reporting a compact set of representative metrics. SUPERVISE is released as a lightweight, modular framework built on precomputed shared label maps, making large-scale reproducible evaluation accessible to the community. All code, label maps, and evaluation results are publicly available at: https://github.com/waldo-j/supervise

Details

Motivation: 现有高精度 REC 方法产生依赖图像的多模态表示，视频逐帧重复计算文本侧信息，即使指称表达式不变也造成浪费；YOLO-World 虽可预编码文本，但单一表达式嵌入缺乏显式词元级对应关系。

Result: 在 RefCOCOg val 上达到 81.29% Acc@0.5，为具有解耦文本分支的 REC 模型中最优，并稳定优于微调 YOLO-World；预计算使单图推理时间降低 18.5%，达到 47.0 FPS，Core ML 版在 iPhone 15 Pro 上达 15.73 FPS。
