Abstract: Many high-accuracy referring expression comprehension (REC) methods produce image-dependent multimodal representations. These representations must be recomputed for every video frame, even when the referring expression remains unchanged. Fast open-vocabulary detectors such as YOLO-World instead pre-encode text once and reuse it throughout the stream. However, encoding each referring expression as a single embedding prevents explicit token-level correspondence. We propose Ref-YOLO-World, which separates text-side computation that depends only on the referring expression from image processing and reuses its outputs across frames. Two lightweight modules inject cached per-token text features into YOLO-World’s detection path. Ref-YOLO-World achieves 81.29% Acc@0.5 on RefCOCOg val, the highest among REC models with a decoupled text branch, and consistently outperforms a fine-tuned YOLO-World. Precomputation reduces per-image inference time by 18.5%, yielding 47.0 FPS. A Core ML version runs at 15.73 FPS on an iPhone 15 Pro, demonstrating efficient on-device REC. Code is available at https://github.com/sakusaku3939/Ref-YOLO-World.

[97] Search-RSI: Expanding Search Worlds Along Multimodal Agents’ Capability Frontier cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Chengchao Lv, Yin Wang, Chunzheng Zhu, Wanli Chang

TL;DR: 论文提出Search-RSI，一个面向多模态搜索智能体的递归自我改进框架。它在每一轮中让当前策略诊断自身在跳数深度、关系稀有度和干扰密度所构成的任务空间中尚未掌握的区域，并在生成新任务的同时同步扩充可检索证据库；同一策略再判定新任务的用途：探针结果不一致的样本用于强化学习，超出能力范围的样本转为提示引导的示范。该方法在九个多模态搜索基准上持续提升，克服了静态任务增长的饱和问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有多跳多模态搜索智能体的训练数据虽然在线增长，但可检索世界被视作固定，导致问题本身可验证、证据却不在检索库中的“不可达负样本”不断累积，智能体正确查询却检索不到内容并被错误惩罚，从而干扰策略学习。

Result: 在九个多模态搜索基准上，Search-RSI 在静态任务增长已饱和处仍持续提升，将所基于方法的平均准确率在9B和27B模型上分别提高9.8和9.5个点，其中7.0个点仅靠保持检索世界一致无法获得；计算量约为静态增长的1.27倍。另外，改进器若在训练开始时冻结会损失3.2个点。

Insight: 创新在于把检索世界与任务空间沿能力前沿共同扩展，避免不可达负样本；并由同一在线策略同时决定任务生成与使用方式，将探针不一致样本用于强化学习、超能力样本转为自示范，无需外部模型且训练时移除特权任务结构。

Abstract: Multimodal search agents answer knowledge intensive visual questions by grounding them in retrieved evidence, and recent recipes train them on multi hop tasks that are synthesized from knowledge graphs and grown online to keep pace with the agent. These recipes, however, treat the searchable world as fixed: a new question can be well formed and verifiable while its evidence is absent from the retrieval backend, so the agent issues the right query, retrieves nothing, and is penalized as if its policy were at fault. Such unreachable negatives accumulate with every round of growth. We propose Search-RSI, a recursive self improvement framework that expands the search world along the agent’s capability frontier. In each round, the current policy diagnoses its partially mastered regions of a task space spanned by hop depth, relation rarity, and distractor density; new tasks there are grown together with their evidence; and the same policy decides how they are used: candidates on which its probes disagree feed reinforcement learning, while those beyond its reach become hint guided demonstrations of its own. Keeping the policy inside this loop matters: an improver frozen at the start of training loses 3.2 points. On nine multimodal search benchmarks, Search-RSI keeps improving where static task growth saturates, raising the average accuracy of the recipe it builds on by 9.8 and 9.5 points at 9B and 27B, of which 7.0 points are out of reach for a world that is merely made consistent. The loop uses no external model and only privileged task structure that is removed before training, at 1.27x the compute of static growth.

[98] When Does Looking Closer Pay Off? Simple Foveated ViTs for Fine-Grained Recognition cs.CVPDF

Boris Kriuk, Mohamed Sobhy

TL;DR: 论文研究一种简单的注视式Vision Transformer（FoVT）用于细粒度识别：先用224像素低分辨率图像，通过最后一层类注意力选择需细化区域，再用448像素细粒度图像块替换选中的粗网格，并由同一骨干网络处理混合分辨率输入。作者在五个基准、两个随机种子和每数据集26种配置上系统评估，分析注意力选择、特征复用、跳过细化及门控策略的收益。结果表明，注视式机制的主要价值在于节省计算，而非稳定提升精度。

Details

Motivation: 细粒度识别通常受益于高分辨率，但并非所有图像区域都需要高分辨率；论文希望以输入自适应方式只细化关键区域，在尽量不损失精度的情况下降低Vision Transformer的计算量。

Result: 类注意力选择在所有五个基准上优于随机选择，总体最多提升2.45个百分点，最小目标最多提升4.18个百分点；与CF-ViT移植版在等Transformer成本下无显著劣势，但也无跨两个种子的一致优势。相对统一448像素模型，未门控FoVT减少44%的Transformer运算量，四个基准无显著精度差异，FGVC-Aircraft下降0.53个百分点；在统一336像素成本下，门控FoVT在四个基准提升精度，NABirds和FGVC-Aircraft收益最大，Stanford Dogs无显著提升。

Insight: 创新点在于用最后一层类注意力直接选择需细化区域，并以同一骨干分类混合分辨率输入，免除特征复用、蒸馏损失和选择调度；同时可跳过最自信的19%–24%图像而不改变精度。客观看，论文的重要贡献是系统刻画注视式ViT何时有效：选择机制有效且节省Transformer运算量，但实际耗时收益小于运算量收益，且精度优势并不稳定。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition benefits from high resolution, but not every image region needs it. We study a foveated Vision Transformer (FoVT) that uses last-block class attention from a 224-pixel glance to select regions for refinement. Fine patches from the 448-pixel image replace the selected coarse cells, and the same backbone classifies the mixed-resolution input. We evaluate FoVT on five benchmarks with two seeds and 26 configurations per dataset. Attention beats random selection on all five benchmarks, by up to 2.45 percentage points overall and 4.18 points on the smallest objects. At equal transformer cost, Holm-corrected tests detect no FoVT deficit relative to a port of the official CF-ViT, but no dataset shows a corrected FoVT advantage in both seeds. FoVT requires no feature reuse, distillation loss, or selection schedule. Reusing coarse features shows no significant benefit and costs 1 to 3 points when the reuse module is trained at the head learning rate. Skipping refinement for the most confident 19 to 24 percent of images leaves accuracy unchanged to two decimal places. At the transformer cost of uniform 336-pixel inference, gated FoVT improves accuracy on four benchmarks, with the largest gains on NABirds and FGVC-Aircraft and no significant gain on Stanford Dogs. Foveation primarily saves computation: relative to a uniform 448-pixel model trained with the same auxiliary glance loss, ungated FoVT uses 44 percent fewer transformer operations, with no significant accuracy difference on four benchmarks and a 0.53-point deficit on FGVC-Aircraft. Wall-clock savings are smaller than the reduction in transformer operations.

[99] $\varepsilon$-SegFormer: Multi-Resolution Vision Transformers for Sparsely Supervised Segmentation in Electron Microscopy cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Erfan Amidi, Sheida RahnamaiKordasiabi, Edoardo Giacomello, Florian Jug

TL;DR: 论文提出 ε-SegFormer，一种面向电子显微镜稀疏监督语义分割的多分辨率 Vision Transformer。它为每个有标注像素采样三种物理分辨率下的局部视图，将各视图 patch 嵌入同一 token 序列并编码图像坐标位置，使注意力在统一坐标系中关联多尺度上下文；训练时隐藏该像素自身 token，迫使模型依据上下文预测标签，并以单阶段训练和辅助重建损失完成训练。

Details

Motivation: 电子显微镜体数据中的细胞器分割需要专家耗费数月制作密集标注，且成像分辨率提升会进一步增加标注成本；现有稀疏监督方法多基于卷积网络，感受野有限，而细胞器身份往往依赖更远距离的上下文。

Result: 在 BetaSeg FIB-SEM 数据集上，模型仅从三个细胞约 6.7 亿体素中的约 1 万个标注像素学习，在第四个未见细胞切片上对细胞核、线粒体和胰岛素颗粒取得平均 Dice 0.89，后处理后为 0.91；消融显示更粗粒度视图可提升精度，局部 token 上的紧凑分割头配合编码器注意力引入广域上下文效果最佳。

Insight: 创新点在于将多物理分辨率视图围绕每个标注像素组织为统一 token 序列，并用图像坐标位置编码让注意力跨视图共享坐标系；训练时遮蔽目标像素自身 token，使标签必须由上下文而非局部外观预测。客观看，其可借鉴之处是稀疏监督下用多尺度 ViT 和上下文预测机制替代密集标注，并以单阶段、无预训练方式结合辅助重建损失降低训练流程复杂度。

Abstract: Segmenting organelles in electron microscopy (EM) volumes requires dense annotations that take experts months to produce, and every gain in imaging resolution adds to this cost. Sparsely supervised methods reduce this cost to a small fraction of labeled pixels, but the convolutional networks they build on see a limited neighborhood around each pixel, while the identity of an organelle often depends on context far beyond it. We introduce $\varepsilon$-SegFormer, a multi-resolution Vision Transformer for sparsely supervised semantic segmentation, which builds on the architecture of MuViT and extends the training method of $\varepsilon$-Seg. For each labeled pixel, $\varepsilon$-SegFormer samples views at three physical resolutions centered on it, embeds their patches into a single token sequence, and encodes every token’s position in image coordinates, so that attention relates all views in one shared frame. A segmentation head classifies the pixel from the tokens around it; during training, the pixel’s own token is hidden, so the label must be predicted from context rather than local appearance. The model is trained in a single stage, without pretraining, with an auxiliary reconstruction loss. On the BetaSeg FIB-SEM dataset, $\varepsilon$-SegFormer learns from about 10,000 labeled pixels among the 670 million voxels inside three cells, and, given the cell mask, segments nuclei, mitochondria and insulin granules in a slice of a fourth, unseen cell with an average Dice of 0.89 (0.91 after post-processing). Ablations show that coarser views improve accuracy and that a compact head on local tokens works best, with the wider context entering through attention in the encoder.

Hongru Yan, Yue Wu, Jian-Wei Zhang, Liangbin Xie, Mingzhe Zheng

TL;DR: 论文提出 Refrain，面向多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头一致性：通过对同一原视频的不同参考片段施加不同风格编辑，让模型在共享风格提示下自监督恢复未编辑原片，从而学习统一视觉风格并保持主体与场景内容。方法遵循 A²O（任意参考、任意时长、单时间线）原则，构建 DurMix-300K 数据集，并提出 SDC 评估指标。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法多依赖额外注意力控制或记忆机制维持跨镜头连续性，但难以在同一框架中同时处理各镜头风格差异和参考-目标时长不匹配；同时 CLIP/DINO 等指标难以准确评价风格冲突与叙事切镜。

Result: 实验表明 Refrain 在跨镜头一致性上达到 SOTA，并在镜头时序、参考遵循和文本对齐方面表现突出；SDC 利用真实视频库去偏，更合理地评估跨镜头风格连续性。

Insight: 主要创新是将“不一致参考”作为自监督扰动信号，通过恢复未编辑原片学习跨镜头一致性；结合 A²O 共享时间画布实现多参考、多时长统一建模而无需辅助条件模块。客观来看，核心贡献还包括 DurMix-300K 数据构造和去偏评估指标 SDC。

Abstract: A comic-book character, a photorealistic cityscape, an oil-painted coffee cup – such stylistically diverse references provide visual guidance across shots and frames, yet generating a coherent multi-shot video that unifies their visual styles while preserving subject identities and scene content remains challenging. Existing methods maintain cross-shot continuity through additional attention control or memory processing. However, they struggle to resolve disparate per-shot styles or handle reference-target duration mismatches within a single framework. We introduce Refrain, a unified model that learns cross-shot consistency through a self-supervised reconstruction task. Reference clips from the same original video are deliberately perturbed with distinct visual edits, and the model recovers the unedited source footage under a shared style prompt. The resulting pipeline yields DurMix-300K, which pairs independently edited references of varying durations with their original videos. Operating on the A$^2$O principle (Any Reference, Any Duration, One Timeline), Refrain maps image and video references onto a shared temporal canvas without auxiliary conditioning modules. For evaluation, experiments show that standard CLIP or DINO metrics frequently overlook stylistic clashes or misinterpret intended narrative cuts as inconsistency. We propose Shot-Debiased Consistency (SDC), which uses an extensive real-video bank to establish a baseline for style variation associated with content changes within shots. By discounting this baseline divergence, SDC evaluates cross-shot style shifts against real-world distributions, where smaller residual differences indicate higher stylistic continuity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Refrain achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-shot consistency, while excelling at shot timing, reference adherence, and text alignment.

[101] Anchors That Move Without Collapsing: Exclusive-Region Anchor Routing for Vision Transformers cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Aadi Gupta

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉Transformer中锚点（anchor token）路由机制，发现在共享骨干上Perceiver式路径和AnchorFormer路径会塌缩为单一摘要（池化图相似度0.99），而软注意力路径仍部分冗余（0.59-0.79）。作者提出基于补丁网格独占划分的锚点路由，并进一步提出focus anchors——将多数锚点放在学习到的重要性图峰值处，同时保持所有锚点的独占Voronoi划分。

Details

Motivation: 锚点token本应让ViT通过少量摘要交换全局信息，但没有机制保证不同锚点总结不同内容，导致锚点塌缩、表征冗余，浪费了锚点的表达能力。

Result: 独占划分路由在CIFAR-100上比Representative Attention高2.4-3.6个点；focus anchors在杂乱画布上比固定划分高1.5个点（95% CI [0.5, 2.6]），但在全高清交通标志识别上无优势。两种变体在约1/5 FLOPs下匹配带局部窗口的稠密注意力，固定划分在FlashAttention下落训练快1.5倍但内存更高；ResNet-18在所有任务上更准确，锚点仅在约1536x1536像素以上才更快且从不省内存。

Insight: 创新点在于指出并量化锚点塌缩问题，提出独占区域划分作为构造上不可塌缩的稳定路由，并设计focus anchors在独占Voronoi划分下自适应分配锚点位置。客观来看，硬独占分配是该文测试中唯一既稳定又最准确的路由方式（softmax/Sinkhorn散度大、Gumbel-softmax精度低），但方法在大分辨率下优势有限，且未超越CNN基线。

Abstract: Anchor tokens let a vision transformer exchange global information through a few summaries, but nothing forces different anchors to summarise different things. On one shared backbone we show that a Perceiver-style pathway and an AnchorFormer pathway collapse to a single summary, the latter despite its mass normalisation (pooling-map similarity 0.99), while other soft pathways, including Representative Attention, stay partly redundant (0.59-0.79). These results concern the routing modules inside our block, not the full published models. An exclusive partition of the patch grid cannot collapse by construction, and matched component by component it is 2.4-3.6 points more accurate than Representative Attention on CIFAR-100. We then propose focus anchors, which place most anchors at the peaks of a learned importance map while all anchors keep one exclusive Voronoi partition. Focus anchors beat a fixed partition on a cluttered canvas (+1.5 points, 95% CI [0.5, 2.6]) but not on full-HD traffic-sign recognition. There, both variants match dense attention with local windows at about a fifth of its FLOPs, and the fixed partition also trains 1.5x faster per step than dense attention with FlashAttention, though both use more memory; a ViT on a downsampled input loses half its mean average precision. Hard exclusive assignment is the only region assignment we tested that is both stable and most accurate: softmax and Sinkhorn relaxations diverge, and Gumbel-softmax trains but is less accurate. We also report the limits: a ResNet-18 is more accurate on every task we trained it on in this from-scratch regime, and anchors train faster than FlashAttention only from about 1536x1536 pixels and never use less memory.

Abstract: Nautical charts encode safety-critical maritime information through standardized symbols, attributes, text, and heterogeneous geometries. Interpreting these charts and assessing the navigational significance of successive chart updates requires recognizing nautical features, determining their spatial relationships, and applying navigational knowledge. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising approach to nautical-chart interpretation, yet systematic evaluation of their fine-grained nautical feature recognition, chart-based spatial reasoning, and application of navigational knowledge to assessing chart changes remains limited. We introduce ENC-QA, a diagnostic benchmark comprising 77,312 questions derived from 6,612 National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) Electronic Navigational Charts (ENCs). The benchmark evaluates four complementary capabilities: ENC Perception, Spatial Reasoning, Joint Perception and Spatial Reasoning, and Application. Of these, 8,227 questions form a progressive subset that decomposes selected chart-change cases into linked questions progressing from change detection to criticality assessment, enabling case-level analysis of how models use perceptual and spatial evidence to determine the navigational significance of chart changes. Evaluations of 10 open-weight VLMs reveal substantial limitations in fine-grained nautical feature recognition and feature-grounded spatial reasoning. Controlled evidence interventions show that relevant perceptual and spatial information improves change-criticality accuracy. TL;DR: 论文提出 StereoVLM，首个面向度量 3D 空间推理的几何感知双目视觉语言模型。该方法采用分层学习范式，从跨视角对应与视差估计，逐步到标定条件下的度量深度感知和语义空间推理，并构建 Stereo3D-Bench 基准进行评估。实验表明，视差监督比直接双目深度监督具有更好的跨环境泛化性，且模型性能确实受益于有效的跨视角几何信息。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding but remain limited in their ability to reason about metric 3D space in the physical world. Existing VLMs typically infer 3D scene structure from one or more images without known camera calibration or cross-view geometric constraints. This makes metric 3D inference inherently ambiguous and leaves it unclear whether their spatial predictions are grounded in visual evidence or driven primarily by semantic priors. In contrast, calibrated stereo provides explicit cross-view geometry that resolves this ambiguity and offers a more reliable foundation for metric 3D perception and geometric generalization. We investigate how VLMs can learn transferable 3D representations directly from calibrated binocular observations. To this end, we introduce StereoVLM, the first geometry-aware binocular VLM for metric 3D spatial reasoning. StereoVLM follows a hierarchical learning paradigm spanning from cross-view correspondence and disparity estimation to calibration-conditioned metric depth perception and semantic spatial reasoning. We further find that disparity supervision generalizes substantially better than direct binocular depth supervision on unseen environments. To evaluate these capabilities, we introduce Stereo3D-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark across three levels, from point-level geometry to object-level spatial reasoning. StereoVLM-8B achieves 95.17% depth accuracy ($δ_1$) and a 66.98 average object-level score on Stereo3D-Bench. Intervention results further indicate that the model’s performance on Stereo3D-Bench benefits from valid cross-view geometry and cannot be explained solely by additional visual tokens or semantic priors. Details

Abstract: Video corpus moment retrieval asks a system to find, in a large collection of untrimmed videos with subtitles, the video and the time interval that a natural-language query describes. We find that most top-ranked errors of a strong corpus-level scorer come from choosing the wrong video, not from imprecise boundaries, and that re-weighting the evidence the scorer already uses does not fix them. We present DiTraR, which addresses video selection from two complementary directions. A witness-interval scorer decomposes the query into probes and scores every candidate interval of every corpus video exactly; a multi-scorer energy ensemble averages the energies of independently trained scorers before one exhaustive search; and a transcript verifier, a frozen language model that reads the time-stamped dialogue of the highest-ranked videos, re-orders the moments without changing their boundaries. Abstract: We study a new problem of preventing object penetration in interactive video world models. Our evaluation reveals that none of the open-source models can reliably prevent such failures. Surprisingly, however, these models already encode the information needed to predict such violations. TL;DR: 论文针对低比特量化感知训练中损失曲面平坦、易陷鞍点的问题，提出RelaxQAT，将量化位宽在训练过程中连续松弛并逐步调整，而非固定或突变位精度。方法在视觉和自然语言任务上相比领先QAT方法取得更高平均准确率。

Motivation: 低比特量化（尤其2-bit及以下）在channelwise方案下难以训练，受限于量化级别少以及损失曲面存在鞍点或平坦区域。论文希望从动态改变位宽的角度缓解平坦量化损失曲面问题。

Abstract: Quantization is a critical piece of the deployment pipeline in the era of large generative models. Low-bit quantization, particularly 2-bits or sub-2-bits, is a notoriously difficult problem, particularly when using a channelwise quantization scheme which has been shown as a leading candidate for hardware-efficiency. The key challenges with low-bit quantization range from limited quantization levels to saddle points or flat regions in the loss surface. In this paper, we look at the problem of flat quantization loss surfaces through a lens of dynamically changing bitwidth. Unlike existing techniques which either change the bit-precision abruptly or keep it fixed, we propose to gradually alter the bitwidth as the model trains. Details

Motivation: 仅凭最终答案正确，无法判断LLM是否真的依赖CoT中表达的推理步骤，也无法判断推理是否高效；需要更可靠的CoT评估方法。

Result: 在数学和多跳推理任务上，答案级不忠实性与更高预测不确定性相关，而不忠实性和低效性与答案正确性仅呈弱或不一致关联；图感知微调可改善目标推理属性，且不直接优化昂贵指标、不损害答案准确率。

Insight: 创新点在于用语义推理图统一刻画CoT忠实性与效率，并通过二维依赖分解得到全局和步骤级度量；客观来看，可借鉴之处是将CoT结构图化、区分语义支持与历史依赖及答案路径与离路径推理，并将评估指标转化为训练目标。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning exposes intermediate steps, but a correct answer does not establish that an LLM relies on the dependence expressed by those steps or reasons efficiently. We operationalize CoT faithfulness and efficiency within a common framework. Specifically, we represent a CoT as a semantic reasoning graph and decompose the model’s predictive dependence along two complementary axes: dependence explained by semantic support versus additional reasoning history, and dependence allocated to answer-reaching versus off-path reasoning. This decomposition yields global and step-level measures of unfaithfulness and inefficiency. Controlled interventions show that the proposed metrics respond to hidden dependence and induced off-path reasoning as intended. Across mathematical and multi-hop reasoning tasks, answer-level unfaithfulness is consistently associated with greater predictive uncertainty, whereas both unfaithfulness and inefficiency exhibit weak or inconsistent associations with answer correctness. We further derive theoretically grounded, graph-aware fine-tuning objectives that improve the targeted reasoning properties without directly optimizing the computationally expensive metrics or compromising answer accuracy. Results indicate that accuracy and brevity provide incomplete assessments of reasoning quality: reliable CoT evaluation should also consider whether the LLM’s predictive dependence is faithfully represented and efficiently allocated.

[108] Belief-State Management Meets Rubric Rewards for Agentic Forecasting cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Jingyi Chai, Yun Liao, Hao Lu, Kemeng Zhang, Bingyang Zheng

TL;DR: 论文针对LLM智能体在真实世界事件预测任务上的表现不足，提出一套包含智能体框架设计与后训练的预测训练框架：框架侧设计主动信念状态管理（Proactive Belief-State Management, PBM），为智能体配备fold工具，将冗长的推理与证据上下文压缩为紧凑的信念状态并重置上下文以继续探索；训练侧提出SEIPH——一个五维、与结果无关的评分量表（rubric），提供过程质量奖励，并用PPO与GRPO对35B模型进行后训练，得到DeepForecaster-PPO与DeepForecaster-GRPO。

Details

Motivation: 现有LLM智能体难以预测真实事件，原因有二：一是标准ReAct框架把推理与证据不断堆积在单一持续增长的上下文中，导致上下文膨胀；二是预测任务的结果型奖励信号稀疏且噪声大，无法区分合理推理与侥幸猜中。论文旨在同时从框架与训练信号两方面解决这两个问题。

Result: DeepForecaster-PPO（35B）在ForecastBench与Polymarket-Sim上取得最佳Brier分数，优于基于DeepSeek-V4-Pro（1.6T）、Kimi-K2.6（1T）和GPT-5.5的前沿智能体；DeepForecaster-GRPO在FutureX两项指标上排名第二。消融实验显示PBM无需训练即可提升基座模型；校准分析表明DeepForecaster-PPO的高置信预测比Qwen3.6与GPT-5.5更准确；轨迹分析显示预测随证据积累与信念修正逐步改善。

Insight: 创新点在于将“信念状态”作为可压缩、可重置的显式中间表示引入智能体框架，并用与结果无关的多维过程评分量表替代结果奖励作为强化学习信号；理论上还证明了理想化的基于fold的停止策略收敛、以及校准后的局部奖励可降低分段级梯度方差，为过程奖励在长程智能体任务中的可行性提供了依据。

Abstract: Current large language model (LLM) agents still struggle to forecast real-world events. This stems from two primary limitations: i) standard ReAct harness accumulates reasoning and evidence within one growing context, and ii) outcome-based rewards in forecasting lead to a slow and noisy signal that cannot distinguish sound reasoning from lucky guesses. To address these, we introduce a forecasting training framework including harness design and post-training. For the harness side, we propose Proactive Belief-State Management (PBM), equipping agents with a fold tool to compress lengthy context into compact belief states and reset the context for subsequent exploration. For the training side, we develop SEIPH, a five-dimensional, outcome-blind rubric, providing process-quality rewards. Using the rubric-based reward, we post-train a 35B model with PPO and GRPO, yielding DeepForecaster-PPO and DeepForecaster-GRPO. Theoretically, we prove that idealized fold-based stopping converges and calibrated local rewards reduce segment-level gradient variance. Empirically, DeepForecaster-PPO (35B) achieves the best Brier score on both ForecastBench and Polymarket-Sim, outperforming frontier agents based on DeepSeek-V4-Pro (1.6T), Kimi-K2.6 (1T), and GPT-5.5. DeepForecaster-GRPO ranks second on both FutureX metrics. Ablations reveal that PBM also improves the untrained base model without any training. Calibration analysis shows that DeepForecaster-PPO makes more accurate high-confidence predictions than Qwen3.6 and GPT-5.5. Trajectory analysis further shows that forecasts generally improve from the first to the final belief state as agents gather evidence and revise their judgments.

[109] When Are Images Needed in Multimodal Machine Translation? Text-Only Routing for On-Demand Visual Context cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态机器翻译系统通常无条件处理图像，或仅在视觉编码后做门控，难以避免冗余视觉计算；而表面歧义并不等于必须依赖视觉，句内文本上下文往往已足够消歧，因此需要一种预视觉、仅依赖源文本的路由决策。

Insight: 创新点在于将视觉调用形式化为源文本充分性预测，并构建三细粒度标签数据集与预视觉轻量路由器 PreVIR，使路由在后端和视觉编码之前完成。客观来看，其价值在于区分“真正视觉依赖”与“文本可解决的歧义”，从系统层面降低多模态计算开销。

Abstract: Image-guided multimodal machine translation (MMT) leverages visual context to resolve translation-relevant uncertainty. However, prevailing systems either process images unconditionally or apply gating only after visual encoding, leaving redundant visual computation unavoidable. To mitigate this computational overhead, routing decisions must rely on a pre-visual source signal. Crucially, surface-level ambiguity does not equate to visual dependence, as intra-sentence textual context often suffices for disambiguation. We therefore formulate visual invocation as source-text sufficiency prediction: determining exclusively from the source text whether translation-relevant uncertainty remains unresolved. To support this formulation, we construct a visual-dependency dataset with three fine-grained labels (no_ambiguity, text_resolved, and vision_needed) and verify discriminative visual evidence for vision_needed instances. We further propose PreVIR, a lightweight, backend-agnostic text-only router that selects between text-only and multimodal branches before computing visual features. Experiments on English-to-Chinese MMT demonstrate that PreVIR achieves a favorable COMET-budget trade-off relative to strong routing baselines. At its default operating point, it substantially reduces visual calls while maintaining translation quality comparable to always-on multimodal inference. Label-wise and case analyses show that this trade-off stems from distinguishing genuine visual dependency from contextually resolved ambiguity.

Abstract: Audio language models (ALMs) can answer clinical questions from speech, outperforming baselines built on handcrafted acoustic features. Each statement the model makes about the voice needs to be checked against clinicians’ perceptual ratings of the same recording. On clinical speech, existing evaluations score only the final answer. We introduce Speech Language Pathologist Bench (SLP-Bench), a benchmark grading each acoustic claim in the chain of thought against clinicians’ perceptual ratings of the same recording. It covers five clinician-rated datasets and 25 perceptual dimensions, and assesses claims for factuality, stability under resampling, and consistency across reworded and different questions. Across ten open-weight reasoning ALMs evaluated zero-shot, claim balanced accuracy ranges from 0.52 to 0.58, claims change under resampling and rewording, and the most consistent models are not the most factual. SLP-Bench is available upon request, subject to approval by the providers of each dataset.

Abstract: Accurate differential diagnosis of neurodegenerative disorders remains challenging due to overlapping clinical manifestations and heterogeneous, often incomplete multimodal observations. We therefore propose Knowledge-enhanced Structural Reasoning (KnowSR), which aligns multimodal patient representations with a literature-grounded knowledge bank of disease prototypes, signed evidence directions, and pairwise diagnostic relations. Details

Abstract: Multimodal large language models can infer sensitive geographic information from ordinary images by combining visual, textual, architectural, and environmental cues. This capability creates a privacy risk: users may reveal fine-grained location information even when explicit metadata such as GPS tags has been removed. We study this problem as geolocation inference in multimodal language models and present GeoCloak, a cue-aware perceptual editing framework for reducing location leakage from shared images. GeoCloak uses an accessible multimodal model as a proxy to identify geolocation-relevant cues, localizes high-impact regions through object grounding, and applies localized semantic edits designed to disrupt downstream geolocation predictions while preserving the overall scene content. Experiments on street-level image datasets show that GeoCloak substantially reduces administrative-level geolocation accuracy across multiple multimodal models, while maintaining automatic perceptual-quality scores closer to those of the original images than pixel-level perturbation baselines. These results suggest that cue-level semantic editing is a promising direction for reasoning-based visual privacy, while also highlighting open challenges in deployment cost, semantic plausibility, and adaptive evaluation.

Ahaan Dabholkar, Harshvardhan Patel, Michalis Polychronakis, Saurabh Bagchi

TL;DR: 论文提出 TrojanMaxxing，将视觉语言模型（VLM）的后门注入重新表述为权重空间编程（WSP）任务。利用 Claude Opus 5.5 和 Mythos 5.2 智能体直接检查并编辑模型权重，同时协同设计不可感知的视觉触发器，在无需梯度优化或训练的情况下实现后门注入。

Details

Motivation: 现有 WSP 表明 LLM 可直接理解和编辑其他网络权重以提升性能，论文关注其安全风险，探索是否能让智能体以训练无关方式对 VLM 植入后门。

Result: TrojanMaxxing 在内容审核任务上达到 93%-100% 攻击成功率，在 OCR 任务上达到 78%-100%，同时保持干净准确率；说明仅“让智能体去做”即可实现免训练后门注入。

Insight: 创新点在于将 VLM 后门攻击重构为权重空间编程任务，由智能体直接检查权重、施加定向编辑并联合设计隐蔽视觉触发器，展示了无需优化或训练的“one-shot”后门注入范式和智能体安全风险。

Abstract: Recent work shows that large language models (LLMs) can reason directly about the weights of other neural networks. Given only a natural-language objective, an LLM can inspect a model’s weights, understand what they compute, and edit them directly without gradient-based optimization. This capability, termed weight-space programming (WSP), has been used to improve model performance on math reasoning, medical QA and other benchmarks. We propose TrojanMaxxing, a novel strategy to backdoor vision-language models (VLMs), by reframing it as a WSP task. We use Claude Opus 5.5 and Mythos 5.2 agents to backdoor VLMs without any optimization or training methods. The agents inspect the model weights, apply targeted edits, and co-design an imperceptible visual trigger that activates the backdoor. We then study the agents’ strategies and present a thorough evaluation of the trojaned models on various benchmark datasets. TrojanMaxxing achieves attack success rates of 93%-100% on content moderation and 78%-100% on OCR while maintaining clean accuracy. Perhaps most consequentially, we show that injecting backdoors in a training-free manner is possible by simply, “asking an agent to do it”.

[114] Don’t Discard Your Rollouts: Reusing Teacher RL Traces for Student Distillation cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Vashisth Tiwari, Emma Strubell, J. Zico Kolter

TL;DR: 论文提出复用教师模型在PPO/GRPO强化学习后训练中保存的rollout轨迹来蒸馏学生模型，而不是丢弃后重新生成数据。方法将复用视为数据选择问题：按prompt通过率排序，先用最简单prompt做SFT热启动，再用混合成功/失败prompt做DPO，并利用教师保存的log-likelihood选择学生兼容的completion。实验表明该两阶段方法在数学推理和Python编程任务上达到与标准蒸馏相当的平均性能，且构建数据集无额外推理成本。

Details

Motivation: PPO/GRPO等RL后训练计算昂贵，采样rollout消耗大量资源；标准蒸馏却丢弃这些包含多completion及奖励的轨迹，并额外计算从最终教师重新生成数据。论文希望验证并实现如何复用已保存的教师RL rollout来蒸馏学生，以降低数据构造成本。

Result: 在数学推理和Python编码任务、多个模型家族的学生上，所提两阶段数据选择方法平均性能与canonical distillation相当；同时数据集构建不产生额外推理成本。摘要未给出具体benchmark数值或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新在于把教师RL rollout复用转化为数据选择问题，利用GRPO训练中已记录的prompt通过率和teacher log-likelihood等元数据安排SFT warm start与DPO，并筛选学生兼容completion，避免naive SFT性能不足和naive DPO在弱学生上不稳定崩溃。

Abstract: RL post-training methods such as PPO and GRPO are computationally expensive, with substantial resources devoted to sampling rollouts, which contain multiple completions per prompt along with their respective rewards. Canonical distillation, however, discards these traces and uses more compute to regenerate data from the final teacher. We ask if and how we can reuse the saved teacher RL rollouts to distil student models. Reusing rollouts is non-trivial: naive SFT on the rollouts underperforms canonical distillation, and naive DPO, while performant, is unstable, collapsing on weaker students. We show that treating rollout reuse as a data selection problem using metadata already recorded during GRPO substantially reduces this gap. We use prompt pass rates to order the data, using the easiest prompts for an SFT warm start prior to using mixed-success prompts for DPO. For each prompt, we use saved teacher log-likelihoods as a proxy for selecting student-compatible completions. Across mathematical reasoning and Python coding tasks, with students from multiple model families, our two-stage recipe achieves comparable average performance to canonical distillation, while incurring no additional inference cost for dataset construction.

[115] ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Sibi Gokul, Jyoti Prakash Meher

TL;DR: 论文提出 ORION 框架，通过辅助头对精确规范执行状态进行监督，将 Transformer 的隐状态轨迹锚定到有效计算流形上，以提升系统泛化能力。作者在 Folding、GridShift 和 CLRS-30 上开展预注册 2×2 析因实验，发现 RoPE 主要改善长度外推，而 ORION 主要改善深度外推，二者在联合长度-深度外推中呈现互补的严格加性增益。

Details

Motivation: 自回归 Transformer 在分布外推理上表现不佳，主要受长序列中的位置损坏和深层执行步骤中的结构状态漂移影响。论文希望分别缓解长度外推和深度外推问题，并验证二者是否对应正交的能力维度。

Result: 在 Folding、GridShift 和 CLRS-30 上，RoPE 将长度外推恢复到 59.8%，ORION 将深度外推恢复到 66.7%；二者组合在联合长度-深度外推上取得严格加性增益。负对照表明，收益依赖精确的规范状态对齐，而哈希化状态统计无法约束深度外推。

Insight: 创新点在于用辅助头监督精确规范执行状态，将潜在轨迹锚定到有效计算流形，从而提升系统泛化；其与 RoPE 作用于正交维度，并通过负对照强调“精确规范对齐”而非一般状态统计信息才是关键。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformers generally fail on out-of-distribution reasoning due to two primary reasons: positional corruption over long sequences and structural state drift across deep execution steps. We introduce ORION, a framework that supervises exact canonical execution states via auxiliary heads to anchor latent trajectories to valid computational manifolds. Across Folding, GridShift, and CLRS-30, a pre-registered 2 x 2 factorial shows that RoPE and ORION address orthogonal axes. RoPE recovers length extrapolation (59.8%), while ORION recovers depth extrapolation (66.7%). Moreover, their combination complementarily achieves strict additive gain in joint length-depth extrapolation, while either method alone offers near satisfactory results. Negative controls confirm that the gain depends strictly on exact canonical alignment; hashed state statistics fail to constrain depth extrapolation.

[116] Preserving Pretrained CFG Structure in RL for Diffusion Models with Conditional Contrast Regularization cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Yunfei Zheng, Xiaolong Wang, Dandan Zheng, Xianjun Deng, Shenghao Liu

TL;DR: 论文研究扩散模型RL后训练中奖励提升但生成质量不稳定的问题，提出将预训练CFG结构作为RL先验。作者从几何角度统一解释CFG和RL为预测空间中的修正向量，并提出条件对比正则化CCR，以冻结参考模型的条件对比作为局部结构先验，从而在保持奖励的同时更稳定地保留生成质量。

Details

Motivation: 标准RL往往提升目标奖励却不能可靠保持整体生成质量，导致不稳定的奖励-质量权衡，甚至被视为奖励黑客。由于CFG有助于视觉清晰度、提示对齐和感知质量，论文希望利用其预训练结构作为RL过程中的质量先验。

Result: 在多种奖励设置和骨干模型上，CCR在保持有竞争力奖励的同时更一致地保留生成质量，取得优于标准RL和通用正则化方法的奖励-质量权衡；摘要未给出具体数值或benchmark名称。

Insight: 创新点在于用统一几何视角解释CFG与RL修正向量，并将RL修正到预训练CFG修正的投影作为质量相关结构保持的诊断指标；进一步提出CCR，用冻结参考模型的条件对比作为局部结构先验约束RL更新以缓解奖励黑客。客观看，将CFG方向作为可诊断、可正则的结构先验是较有借鉴意义的思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important component in the post-training of diffusion models. In practice, however, standard RL often improves the target reward without reliably preserving holistic generation quality, leading to unstable reward-quality trade-offs that we view as a form of reward hacking. Since Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) is known to improve visual clarity, prompt alignment, and perceptual quality in generation, we investigate whether its underlying structure can serve as a useful prior during RL. We present a unified geometric view that interprets both CFG and RL as correction vectors in a common prediction space, and identify the projection of the RL-induced correction onto the pretrained CFG correction as a useful diagnostic of quality-related structural preservation. Geometric analysis and rotation intervention further show that the pretrained CFG direction carries information useful for generation quality that reward-driven RL updates do not necessarily preserve. Based on this insight, we propose Conditional Contrast Regularization (CCR), which uses the frozen reference model’s conditional contrast as a local structural prior during RL. Experiments across multiple reward settings and backbones show that CCR more consistently preserves generation quality while maintaining competitive reward, yielding a better reward-quality trade-off than standard RL and generic regularization methods.

[117] HR-Merge: Model Merging with Headroom-Weighted Residual Refinement cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Jiakang Huang, Xueyan Zhang, Renyi Cai, Jingcheng Niu, Jiaru Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 HR-Merge，一种无需额外训练、可套用于现有模型合并方法的残差精修框架。它利用每个任务相对合并更新的残差，并按任务验证集上的“headroom”（合并造成的专家精度损失比例）加权，再用基础合并算子重新合并这些加权残差，并以验证集选择回填强度。方法在 LLaMA-3 8B 的 NLI LoRA 适配器和 CLIP 视觉多任务设置上均带来提升或保持基础性能。

Details

Motivation: 现有模型合并方法对所有任务采用同一合并规则，但合并后各任务精度损失并不均衡；论文希望定位哪些任务的损失更值得恢复，并利用任务残差补偿这些损失。

Result: 在 LLaMA-3 8B 的六个 NLI 适配器上，HR-Merge 将 TIES 的归一化精度从 93.29 提升到 94.29，超过所有测试的基础合并方法，主要收益来自恢复最弱任务；在视觉侧，覆盖三个 CLIP 骨干、最多 20 个任务及 LoRA 合并，通常提升或保持基础精度，并在每个设置中取得最佳模型。

Insight: 创新点在于用任务验证集 headroom 衡量残差恢复价值，将不同任务残差视为会相互干扰而需重新合并的信号，并通过验证集选择回填强度且可回退到原合并结果；客观看，其价值主要是训练无关、模型无关的即插即用后处理，能针对任务间不均衡损失做细粒度补偿。

Abstract: Model merging combines experts fine-tuned from a shared backbone, such as LoRA adapters of a large language model, into one multi-task model without further training. Existing methods apply one rule to every task, yet the accuracy they lose is uneven across tasks. We show that the lost accuracy remains recoverable from each task’s residual, its task vector minus the merged update, and that a task’s validation headroom, the share of its expert validation accuracy the merge loses, indicates where recovery pays off. Residuals from different tasks interfere, so they must be merged rather than summed. We propose HR-Merge, a training-free wrapper around any merging method. It weights each residual by its task’s headroom, re-merges the weighted residuals with the base operator, and adds the result back at a strength selected on validation, falling back to the base merge when no strength helps. On six NLI adapters of LLaMA-3 8B, HR-Merge lifts TIES from 93.29 to 94.29 normalized accuracy, above all tested bases, mainly by recovering its weakest task. In vision, on three CLIP backbones, up to 20 tasks, and LoRA merging, it generally improves or preserves base accuracy and yields the best model in every setting.

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in understanding the behaviour of LLM agents from the perspective of human preferences or corpus statistics. In this paper we propose agent states as intermediate variables through which post-training can be measured and predicted. From a statistical physics perspective, we study the emergence of a global potential which governs the transition of state in LLM agent reasoning. We introduce a partition function to model the ideal KL-regularised transition distribution and track potential structure, mean reward and state entropy. We demonstrate our approach in two Qwen3.5-9B tasks: safety-test selection and physics-reasoning auditing. We observe that the potential structure strengthens during post-training as predicted from our framework, while joint changes in reward and state entropy provide consistency evidence. Abstract: Data selection has emerged as an effective approach to reducing the model training costs. However, most existing methods often rely heavily on sample hardness, which may overlook differences in sample learning dynamics and lead to suboptimal sample selection. In this paper, we propose LeDAS, an end-to-end Learning Dynamics-Aware Selection framework for dynamic data selection, which jointly characterizes sample importance through hardness, learning progress, and deterioration, enabling adaptive sample prioritization throughout training with marginal additional computational overhead. Specifically, we track per-sample loss dynamics using exponential moving averages (EMA) to obtain three complementary signals that capture current learning difficulty, ongoing learning improvements, and potential degradation. To accommodate evolving training dynamics, we introduce Adaptive Metric Fusion, a contextual bandit-based mechanism that dynamically adjusts the relative importance of these signals based on training feedback. Furthermore, a bounded staleness compensation mechanism accounts for historical selection patterns by increasing the selection priority of samples that have not been selected for extended periods. Extensive experiments across diverse vision and language tasks demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of LeDAS. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, LeDAS consistently achieves superior performance, matching or surpassing full-dataset training at a 50% selection ratio on ImageNet-1K and in LLM supervised fine-tuning. Moreover, its modular and task-agnostic design enables the integration of complementary selection signals, demonstrating strong robustness to noisy and corrupted datasets.

Details

Abstract: Human deepfake detection remains unreliable, and whether reassessment with corrective feedback improves subsequent judgments remains unclear. This study examined differences between initial and repeated deepfake assessments among 110 participants using audiovisual stimuli from the FakeAVCeleb and Deepfake Detection Challenge (DFDC) datasets. Participants evaluated 40 videos from each dataset in phase 1 (PhaseI) and reassessed the same videos in phase 2 (PhaseII) under a procedure combining repeated presentation and corrective feedback. For the FakeAVCeleb dataset, the participants’ mean accuracy increased by 4.68 percentage points from 63.30 in PhaseI to 67.98 in PhaseII (t(109)=5.61, p<.001, d_z=0.54). A judgment-level logistic regression with standard errors clustered by participant and video produced a consistent result: beta=0.208, SE=0.040, z=5.23, p<.001, {OR}=1.23. For the DFDC dataset, the participants’ mean accuracy increased by 8.64 percentage points from 59.52% in PhaseI to 68.16% in PhaseII (t(109)=7.37, p<.001, d_z=0.70); the logistic model yielded: beta=0.375, SE=0.065, z=5.78, p<.001, {OR}=1.46. Improvements primarily came from fewer false negatives, while false-positive rates remained unchanged. However, judgment reversals occurred in 27.55% of FakeAVCeleb and 32.91% of DFDC assessments, with some initially correct judgments becoming incorrect upon reassessment. Despite the accuracy gains, repeated assessment did not eliminate judgment instability or residual error. Participants also exhibited consistent overconfidence, with mean self-rated confidence exceeding actual accuracy by 11.99 percentage points for FakeAVCeleb and 12.82 percentage points for DFDC across both phases. Details

Motivation: 第一视角视频可用于机器人学习，但单目视频存在尺度-深度歧义和手部遮挡，仅依赖图像接触与形状先验无法唯一确定度量尺度，因此需要引入触觉接触来恢复度量物体几何与运动。

Result: 在 100 条 ARCTIC 和 HO3D 序列（含合成触觉观测）上，TacHOI 取得最低的中位 ADD-S*，ARCTIC 上误差从 15.6 cm 降至 3.6 cm，HO3D 上达到 2.8 cm；在 OpenTouch 上验证了对实测触觉观测的适用性。

Abstract: Egocentric videos offer a scalable source of human demonstrations for robot learning. However, recovering metric object geometry and motion from monocular video is hindered by scale-depth ambiguity and hand-induced occlusion, while image-derived contact and shape priors alone cannot uniquely determine metric scale. We introduce TacHOI, a training-free framework that integrates tactile contact with monocular vision for joint hand-object reconstruction and tracking, without requiring object templates or depth sensors. Starting from pretrained visual estimates, TacHOI uses tactile contact to constrain object depth relative to the estimated hand, while spatially separated contacts on a metrically calibrated hand provide a physical ruler for object extent. It incorporates these constraints into a joint optimization of hand poses, object trajectories, and anisotropic object scale alongside visual alignment. Across 100 ARCTIC and HO3D sequences with synthesized tactile observations, TacHOI achieves the lowest median ADD-S* among the evaluated baselines, reducing error from 15.6 to 3.6 cm on ARCTIC and achieving 2.8 cm on HO3D. Insight: 创新点包括采用整流流实现一步推理、引入双向Mamba视觉编码器捕获长时序双向依赖，以及提出特征级时间步条件设计，使视觉嵌入按流时间步自适应调制。客观看，核心贡献在于诊断“整流流+时序编码器”简单组合失效的原因，并通过显式时间步条件恢复梯度通路和模块间 coherent 交互。

Abstract: Vision-model-based robotic navigation has gained increasing popularity, yet existing approaches still struggle to deliver effective and efficient navigation performance, \textit{e.g.}, diffusion-based policies that require costly iterative denoising. In this work, we propose three improvements to bridge these gaps. First, we adopt a rectified flow framework to reduce the required inference steps to one while achieving comparable or even superior performance. Second, we leverage a bidirectional Mamba-based visual encoder that employs selective state-space modeling to more effectively capture bidirectional temporal dependencies across observation frames, even with longer input sequences. Most importantly, we find that naïvely combining these two components to balance accuracy with inference speed yields no further performance gains or even produces inferior results. Upon extensive analysis, we realized that the root cause lies in the fact that the Hydra-derived temporal context is shared unchanged across all flow timesteps, and timestep information is introduced only within the velocity network. This weak coupling limits the model’s ability to adaptively exploit temporal visual features at different flow states. To address this, we propose a feature-wise timestep conditioning design that explicitly conditions the visual embedding on the flow timestep, enabling noise-level-aware trajectory generation, thereby restoring a meaningful gradient pathway and enabling coherent interaction between the rectified flow and the temporal encoder. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms both baseline and competitive methods.

Abstract: As humanoid robots increasingly enter real-world environments, recovering their 3D whole-body pose from monocular imagery is becoming increasingly important for external state perception, motion analysis, and robot learning. However, Humanoid Robot Pose Estimation (HRPE) lies at the intersection of conventional Robot Pose Estimation (RPE) and Human Pose Estimation (HPE), while not being fully addressed by either paradigm: RPE primarily focuses on non-humanoid robots, particularly fixed-base manipulators, whereas HPE is built upon human-specific skeletal topologies, body models, and motion priors. Despite recent progress in humanoid 2D keypoint detection and HPE adaptation, humanoid-native 3D pose recovery remains insufficiently studied, and a dedicated benchmark with suitable training data and evaluation protocols is still lacking. To address this gap, we introduce Humanoids, a benchmark for monocular 3D humanoid robot pose estimation, and propose HumanoidPose, a framework for this task. Humanoids provides synthetic 3D supervision across diverse humanoid embodiments, manually annotated real-world 2D data, and real-world RGB sequences paired with robot states for 3D evaluation on a physical Roban humanoid. HumanoidPose combines human-inspired visual perception with robot articulation modeling and explicit kinematic grounding. Experiments on Humanoids and the DHRP-H1 benchmark demonstrate strong performance in articulation recovery and 3D pose estimation, including zero-shot transfer to real-world 2D and 3D evaluation. [126] Tripwire: Robot Agents that Program Their Own Interrupts under Human Disturbance cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Sunli Chen, Yun Zong, Qinhong Zhou, Xiaowen Qiu, Jiaben Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 TRIP，一种无需训练的事件驱动智能体执行框架，用于解决机器人智能体在调用 VLA/WAM 等预训练策略后无法及时响应人类干扰的问题。智能体每次调用策略时编写轻量、有状态的 Python“绊线”程序，与策略并行运行并可查询小型 VLM 判断是否中断。绊线触发后，框架暂停策略并将控制权及原因返回智能体，智能体重新评估并重写绊线后继续执行。

Details

Motivation: 传统回合制智能体框架在调用连续反应的机器人策略时存在决策盲区，人类闯入路径或移动物体时无法及时中断；而频繁调用推理智能体又会带来秒级延迟。论文旨在以事件驱动方式在控制频率与推理成本之间取得平衡。

Result: 在 RoboHD 基准的 168 个 RoboCasa365 场景中，使用三个 VLA 和一个 WAM 评估。与相同智能体和工具的回合制框架相比，TRIP 在最强策略上将人机接触率减半，安全性从 61.3% 提升到 81.3%，任务成功率也从 31.0% 提升到 38.7%。真实机器人实验表明，TRIP 能在人类干扰下完成两个操作任务，而回合制框架和单独策略基本失败。

Insight: 创新点在于让智能体自行编写可执行的中断监控程序，将固定频率或人工规则的中断机制转化为动态生成、可查询 VLM 的状态化绊线。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是把高延迟的智能体推理与低延迟的并行监控解耦，通过事件触发按需重规划，在无需额外训练的情况下提升人机共存安全性。

Abstract: Agent harnesses are turn-based: the agent acts, waits for the result, then reasons. When the action invokes a pretrained robot policy, such as a vision-language-action (VLA) or world action model (WAM), the policy may react continuously to observations, but the agent cannot revisit its decision until the call returns. Meanwhile, a person may step into the robot’s path or move the object it is about to grasp. Invoking the agent more often narrows this blind interval, but a reasoning model takes seconds per decision and most control steps need no new one. We propose TRIP, a training-free, event-driven harness in which the agent programs its own interrupts. With each call, the agent writes a tripwire: a lightweight, stateful Python program that runs alongside the policy, can query a compact VLM (for example, to locate a person and measure their distance), and decides whether the call continues or control returns to the agent. When it fires, the harness halts the policy and returns control with the reason; the agent then reassesses, adapts, and continues under a rewritten tripwire. We evaluate TRIP with three VLAs and one WAM on RoboHD, a benchmark of 168 RoboCasa365 scenes in which a simulated person disturbs the robot and any human-robot contact counts as failure. Compared with a turn-based harness using the same agent and tools, TRIP halves the rate of human contact with the strongest policy, raising safety from 61.3% to 81.3%, without sacrificing task success (38.7% vs. 31.0%). Real-robot experiments show that TRIP completes two manipulation tasks under human interference where the turn-based harness and the policy alone largely fail.

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models imagine future observations under candidate robot actions, providing a promising visual tool for robot policy evaluation and interaction simulation. However, existing video world models often optimize global reconstruction errors instead of emphasizing interaction-relevant regions. As a result, they may preserve overall scene appearance but overlook local interaction details, leading to physically implausible outcomes such as hallucinated contact effects and inconsistent robot-object interactions. We introduce MoReWorld, a unified motion-region-guided framework that uses a shared motion region, the part of the environment that moves in response to the robot’s actions, to focus dynamics learning and video generation on interaction-relevant regions. At inference time, the motion region is predicted as a motion probability map from visual history and robot actions, without requiring future observations or ground-truth motion labels. Details

Motivation: 驾驶模型可能正确描述风险，却因其他原因选择路径，说明“看见/说出事实”不等于“影响规划”。论文关注场景推理在端到端规划器中的可检查接入位置，以判断其影响是否真实且可验证。

Abstract: Driving models that reason may describe a hazard correctly while choosing their path for other reasons. We study where scene reasoning can enter an end-to-end planner so its influence can be checked. We freeze Drive-JEPA and use a fine-tuned vision-language model to state scene facts. Although fine-tuning improves fact accuracy, the two learned components we test show no detectable benefit from reading them. A rule-based fact check instead rejects or shortens paths that contradict the facts, linking each intervention to a stated fact. On the frozen planner alone, it gains 2.4 EPDMS points on navhard. Its 2.0-point margin over a control that changes the same frames without consulting facts remains statistically uncertain. A diagnostic using exact facts from maps and annotations yields a 3.6-point margin over its corresponding control, with a 95% confidence interval above zero. Separately, a path scorer and plan memory that read no stated facts raise navhard from 41.2 to 51.8. Bounding travel distance using the reasoner’s facts adds 0.3 there and reduces navtest by 2.0. [129] SOAR: Training-Free Rank-Consistent Rollout Compression for Latent World Model Planning cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Anqi Li, Kai Liu, Linghe Kong, Yulun Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 SOAR，一种无需训练的 latent world model 规划加速方法。它利用规划器只需保持候选动作排序一致的特点，对每次决策自适应压缩 rollout 的 token 预算，从而在不重新训练模型的情况下显著减少计算。方法通过少量候选探测选择能保持排序的最小预算，并保持第一步精确、缓存上下文帧。

Details

Motivation: Latent world model 规划需要对大量候选动作序列进行 transformer rollout，导致单次决策耗时很长。现有方法沿用视觉语言模型的空间 token 剪枝，但规划本质上是基于预测代价对候选排序，因此只要候选排序不变，rollout 就可以被大幅压缩。

Result: 在 7 个导航与操作任务、冻结 checkpoint 的设定下，SOAR 在 2.3 倍加速时达到与稠密规划相当的规划成功率，在 2.5 倍加速时仍保留 98% 的成功率。

Insight: 创新点在于将压缩准则从重建/预测精度转为候选排序一致性，并据每次决策自适应选择最小 token 预算，无需任务标签或训练；同时通过少量候选探测确定预算，并保持第一步精确与上下文缓存，兼顾效率与规划可靠性。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by rolling out hundreds of candidate action sequences through a transformer predictor, so a single decision can take tens of seconds. Existing accelerations prune spatial tokens as in vision-language models, yet a sampling-based planner uses predicted costs only to rank candidates, so a rollout can be compressed freely as long as the ranking survives. We propose SOAR, a training-free method that adapts rollout compression to each planning decision. At every decision, SOAR probes a few candidates under several token budgets and keeps the smallest budget that preserves their ranking, so it compresses as far as the current decision allows, without task labels. A closed-loop study of more than 80,000 episodes fixes the rest of the rollout: the first step, which alone reads a real observation, stays exact, and the context frame is cached rather than recomputed. On seven navigation and manipulation tasks with frozen checkpoints, SOAR matches dense-level planning success at 2.3x speedup and retains 98% at 2.5x. Our code is available at https://github.com/starrywiki/SOAR.

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM)-based agents offer a promising approach to robotic manipulation, yet remain limited by inadequate memory organization, uncertain failure attribution, and limited reuse of execution experience. We propose Embodied-Harness, a dual-agent framework that improves manipulation by refining the execution harness without updating model parameters. The Actor interacts with the environment through a robot-control interface, while the Reflector converts execution evidence into reusable operational guidance. Hierarchical Memory Architecture maintains short-term execution context and long-term experiential knowledge to support execution and reflection. Evidence-based Experience Reflection grounds failure diagnosis in observed interactions, while Recursive Harness Self-Improvement refines operational guidance across successive attempts. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RoboSPA, and RMBench demonstrate the effectiveness of Embodied-Harness in generalization under environmental variations, spatial reasoning, long-horizon planning, and memory-dependent manipulation. Our method achieves success rates of 82.4%, 76.7%, and 94.4% on the three benchmarks, respectively. Real-world evaluation further yields an average success rate of 96.7% across three manipulation tasks. These results highlight the potential of Embodied-Harness to improve robotic manipulation through experience-driven harness self-improvement.

Details

Motivation: 现有SAR基础模型在输入、训练数据、目标、架构和发布策略上差异很大，但领域缺乏统一分类和比较框架；任务特定基准难以公平比较不同输入输出格式和任务范式的模型，且常忽视数据透明性、可复现性、可解释性和部署可用性。SAR数据可获取性有限也导致高报告性能不一定意味着可复现、可基准测试和可部署。

Result: 论文对76个VFMs和40个VLMs进行了多维评估；结果显示，在其框架下的SAR基准任务表现中，SAR原生VLMs并未显著优于在包含SAR的多模态混合语料上预训练的遥感VLMs。作者认为这一缺乏明显优势主要源于大规模高质量公开标注SAR数据仍然稀缺，限制了SAR原生模型和数据规模。

Insight: 创新点在于提出面向SAR基础模型的系统分类与分层公平评测框架，包含五级评分、六维准则、VFMs/VLMs分离协议、多维能力画像和用户自定义权重。其可借鉴之处是将模型能力评估与数据透明性、可复现性、可解释性和部署准备度结合，并支持按需求进行模型选择。

Abstract: Foundation models have advanced SAR image interpretation but created a heterogeneous, fragmented model ecosystem. Existing models differ substantially in their input, training data, objectives, architectures, and release policy, yet the field lacks a unified taxonomy and comparative framework. Current evaluations rely primarily on task-specific benchmarks, which are ill-suited to fair comparison among models with fundamentally different input-output formats and task paradigms, and often overlook data transparency, reproducibility, interpretability, and deployment readiness. Limited SAR data accessibility also means high reported performance does not guarantee access to training data, code, or weights, limiting reproduction, benchmarking, and deployment. To address these issues, we propose a systematic taxonomy and hierarchical evaluation framework for SAR-related foundation models: five rating levels, six dimensions, and fine-grained criteria, with separate protocols for VFMs and VLMs. Beyond overall ratings, it yields interpretable multidimensional capability profiles and supports user-defined weighting, so rankings follow user priorities. Utilizing a fixed auxiliary multimodal large language model in combination with human review, we conduct a systematic meta-analysis and multidimensional assessment of 76 VFMs and 40 VLMs. In terms of SAR benchmark task performance under our framework, SAR-native VLMs do not significantly outperform remote sensing VLMs pretrained on mixed multimodal corpora in which SAR is one of several modalities. This lack of clear advantage is largely due to data availability: large-scale, high-quality, public annotated SAR data remain scarce, restricting both model and training-data scale for SAR-native models. The framework provides a structured basis for requirement-aware model selection and remains extensible as new models emerge.

[133] Dem2TA: Beyond Pixels, Towards Perception eess.IV | cs.CVPDF

Ping Chen, Zhongwei Lin, Xiang He, Xiangming Wang, Kai Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出Dem2TA框架，将保真度监督的去马赛克显式纳入面向下游感知的RAW-to-RGB ISP适配流程。方法先联合适配重建前端与下游网络，并引入重建监督、任务监督及语义/任务表示对齐；随后冻结前端，联合优化轻量局部ISP与下游模型进行任务特定渲染。实验在合成RAW检测、框提示分割及真实拍摄数据上验证有效性。

Details

Motivation: 现有任务导向ISP常将去马赛克简化、固定或主要按重建质量优化，导致该阶段未充分融入下游任务适配。论文旨在解决RAW-to-RGB感知流水线中去马赛克与任务感知目标脱节的问题。

Result: 在合成COCO RAW上，相比同一适配基础的TA-ISP，检测AP提升0.46点，分割IoU提升1.06点；在人工验证的真实拍摄数据集上，分割IoU提升0.63点，表明方法可迁移到真实输入。

Insight: 创新点在于将去马赛克作为可适配前端纳入任务感知ISP，并采用表示引导的联合适配与后续局部渲染两阶段优化，在不改变重建线性RGB的前提下细化任务特定渲染。客观看，其价值在于显式打通重建前端与下游感知表示，并以语义/任务对齐和轻量局部ISP兼顾保真与任务性能。

Abstract: Task-oriented image signal processors (ISPs) adapt RAW-to-RGB processing for downstream perception. However, explicit demosaicing is often simplified, kept fixed, or optimized primarily for reconstruction quality, leaving this stage insufficiently integrated into task adaptation. We propose \textbf{Dem2TA}, a framework that incorporates fidelity-supervised demosaicing into the adaptation of the perception pipeline. Starting from a pretrained demosaicing model, we jointly adapt the reconstruction frontend and downstream network using reconstruction and task supervision, together with complementary semantic and task representation alignment. We then freeze the adapted frontend and jointly optimize a lightweight local ISP with the downstream model to refine task-specific rendering without changing the reconstructed linear RGB. Experiments on synthetic RAW detection and box-prompted segmentation demonstrate the effectiveness of representation-guided joint adaptation and subsequent local rendering. Evaluation on a manually verified real-capture dataset further supports transfer beyond synthetic inputs. Compared with TA-ISP on the same adapted foundation, Dem2TA improves detection AP by 0.46 points and segmentation IoU by 1.06 points on synthetic COCO RAW, and segmentation IoU by 0.63 points on real captures.

q-bio.NC [Back]

[134] Serial Dependence Emerges from Optimizing Vision for Noise Robustness q-bio.NC | cs.CVPDF

Hiroshi Higashi, Keigo Sugihara, Yuichi Tanaka

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了一个规范性假设：视觉系统的序列依赖可能是为抵御感觉噪声而优化视觉处理的副产物。作者训练由CNN编码器、ConvGRU记忆模块和CNN解码器组成的循环自编码器，在无任何感知历史目标的情况下重建受高斯噪声污染的自然视频。模型仅通过时间记忆就自发表现出对先前朝向的吸引偏差和空间连续性场，并在控制响应自相关混淆后与人类朝向判断定量匹配。

Details

Motivation: 序列依赖的功能目的仍存争议：已有机制模型能解释残余记忆痕迹如何产生该现象，但未说明视觉系统为何要向前携带信息。论文旨在检验序列依赖是否可作为优化视觉对感觉噪声鲁棒性的规范后果而自然涌现。

Result: 使用时间记忆的模型重建误差低于无记忆模型，且训练噪声越大优势越明显，说明整合过去上下文是自适应去噪策略。Gabor和点序列探测显示模型自发产生序列依赖，其对紧邻前一刺激最强并随延迟单调衰减；在控制响应自相关混淆后，模型与人类朝向判断定量匹配，包括一个独立公开数据集。

Insight: 创新点在于用自然视频去噪训练循环自编码器，在不引入感知历史目标的情况下让序列依赖自发涌现，并将其解释为噪声鲁棒性优化的副产物，提供了补充机制解释的规范性账户。客观上看，论文将行为现象与计算目标直接联系，并通过控制测量伪影增强模型-人类匹配论证，具有较好的可解释性和理论整合价值。

Abstract: Human visual perception is dynamically shaped by the recent past, a phenomenon known as serial dependence. Mechanistic models show that a residual memory trace can generate this dependency, yet its functional purpose (why the visual system should carry information forward in time at all) remains debated. We tested the hypothesis that serial dependence emerges as a normative consequence of optimizing vision for robustness against sensory noise. We trained recurrent autoencoders (a CNN encoder, a ConvGRU memory module, and a CNN decoder) to reconstruct natural videos corrupted by Gaussian noise, with no objective referring to perceptual history. Models that used their temporal memory achieved lower reconstruction error than memory-free counterparts, and this advantage widened as training noise increased, indicating that integrating past context is an adaptive denoising strategy. Probing the trained models with Gabor and dot sequences, we found that memory gave rise to serial dependence spontaneously: an attractive bias toward previous orientations and a spatial “continuity field” toward recently seen locations, both strengthening with training noise. Across temporal delays, the model’s serial dependence was strongest for the immediately preceding stimulus and decayed monotonically. This profile quantitatively matched human orientation judgments, including an independent public dataset, once a known response-autocorrelation confound was controlled: an apparent non-monotonicity in one dataset reflected this measurement artifact rather than a genuine model-human mismatch. These findings suggest that serial dependence need not be an idiosyncratic quirk but can arise as a byproduct of optimizing for noise robustness, offering a normative account of perceptual history-dependence that complements existing mechanistic explanations and links behavior to an underlying computational objective.

stat.ML [Back]

[135] SDG: State-Dependent Gating at Chunk Boundaries for Linear Attention stat.ML | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Renjia Deng, Yuxi Liu, Yutong He, Xue Wang, Kun Yuan

TL;DR: 论文提出状态依赖门控SDG，用于门控线性注意力模型：在每个chunk边界根据记忆状态的变化计算逐元素门控，并施加于chunk边界，从而在保留chunk内并行计算的同时，为遗忘机制引入状态反馈。作者为Gated DeltaNet、Kimi Delta Attention、Gated DeltaNet-2和Mamba-3实现了融合kernel，并从理论和实验上验证了该方法能提升现有线性注意力模型。

Details

Motivation: 现有门控线性注意力通常使用状态无关门控，门控仅由token表示计算而不直接读取循环记忆，导致相同token在不同记忆状态下产生相同门控，无法根据层内存储信息自适应遗忘。论文旨在解决该问题，同时不破坏chunkwise并行计算效率。

Result: 在1.3B参数线性注意力及混合模型、100B token训练设置下，SDG在语言建模、常识推理、合成与真实检索以及长上下文理解等任务上均取得提升；摘要未给出具体数值，但表明其能有效增强现有线性注意力模型。

Insight: 创新点在于将逐元素门控从token表示依赖改为chunk内记忆变化依赖，并在chunk边界施加，从而兼顾状态反馈与并行效率；同时为多种线性注意力/状态空间模型实现融合kernel。理论分析在信号—噪声模型下量化了状态依赖逐元素门控的潜在重构收益，并指出固定门控时边界衰减等价于逐元素ridge近端步。客观来看，其可借鉴之处在于用chunk边界作为低成本状态反馈注入点，以及将门控设计与高效kernel和理论分析结合。

Abstract: Recent gated linear attention models use state-independent gates, computed from token representations without directly reading the recurrent memory. This design supports efficient chunkwise computation: sequences are divided into chunks of consecutive tokens, with parallel computation within each chunk. However, the same token representation produces the same gate, even when the layer stores different information. We introduce State-Dependent Gating (SDG), which computes element-wise gates from the change in memory over a chunk and applies them at the chunk boundary. This provides state feedback for forgetting while preserving parallel computation within each chunk. We develop fused kernels for Gated DeltaNet, Kimi Delta Attention, Gated DeltaNet-2, and Mamba-3. Our theoretical analysis quantifies the potential reconstruction benefits of state-dependent, element-wise gating under a signal–noise model. We also show that boundary decay is an element-wise ridge proximal step when the gate is held fixed. Empirical evaluation of 1.3B linear attention and hybrid models trained on 100B tokens shows improvements across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, synthetic and real-world retrieval, and long-context understanding. These results demonstrate that SDG is an effective approach to enhancing existing linear attention models.
